[1] TETHER: Training-Free, Elastic, ToC-Anchored Hierarchy Extraction and Recovery in Design-Driven Long Documents cs.CL | cs.CVPDF

Yu Zou, Yan Chen, Jialei Zeng, Xiaorui Zhou, Aixi Zhong

TL;DR: 论文提出 TETHER，一个无需训练、以目录（ToC）为锚点的弹性层级抽取与恢复框架，用于将设计驱动的长文档转换为结构化知识。它通过两阶段神经符号机制，先用大型多模态模型在时空语义约束下从原始目录构建文档骨架，再用基于锚点的对齐策略将内容块绑定到骨架，从而解决复杂布局中的边界模糊问题。

Details

Motivation: 现有自底向上方法忽略目录这一宏观结构，依赖局部视觉线索，在高熵布局中视觉显著性与逻辑深度脱节时易失效；直接让 LLM 抽取又缺乏空间 grounding，长上下文下易产生幻觉。论文旨在将目录从被丢弃的噪声提升为关键结构先验，实现跨设计驱动长文档的鲁棒层级恢复。

Result: 在多语言 ESG 报告、产品手册和年报基准上，TETHER 实现了高精度、SOTA 的零样本层级恢复，优于深度层级恢复模型、专用解析器和通用 LMM；压力测试显示其对稀疏遗漏和文本噪声具有韧性，消融验证架构不可约性，并相比端到端基线实现 90% token 减少和大幅加速。

Insight: 创新点在于训练免费、以 ToC 为宏观结构先验，并结合 LMM 构建骨架与锚点对齐消歧的两阶段神经符号机制。客观看，其价值在于把符号化目录结构与语义对齐结合，缓解视觉显著性与逻辑深度解耦及 LLM 空间 grounding 缺失问题，同时兼顾结构精度与部署可扩展性。

Abstract: Converting complex documents into structured knowledge constitutes the critical infrastructure urgently required for deploying Large Language Models (LLMs) in downstream research. However, blind to the Table of Contents (ToC) as a macro-structure, existing bottom-up paradigms rely on localized visual cues, inducing heuristic fragility in high-entropy layouts where visual prominence decouples from logical depth; meanwhile, direct LLM extraction lacks spatial grounding, triggering hallucinations in long contexts. To address this, we introduce TETHER, a training-free framework that elevates the ToC from discarded noise to a vital structural prior, enabling robust adaptation across design-driven long documents. TETHER operates via a two-stage neuro-symbolic mechanism: first, it constructs a document skeleton from the raw ToC using a Large Multimodal Model (LMM) empowered by spatio-semantic constraints; second, it employs an anchor-based alignment strategy to tether content blocks to this skeleton, effectively resolving boundary ambiguities. Evaluated across a multilingual benchmark of ESG reports, product manuals, and annual reports, TETHER delivers highly precise, state-of-the-art (SOTA) zero-shot hierarchy recovery, outperforming deep hierarchical recovery models, dedicated parsers, and general-purpose LMMs. Moreover, stress tests highlight TETHER’s resilience to sparse omissions and textual noise, demonstrating that its precise semantic alignment is fundamentally anchored in topological and depth fidelity. Crucially, ablations confirm the framework’s architectural irreducibility, while achieving 90% token reduction and drastic speedups over end-to-end baselines, establishing a compelling equilibrium between structural precision and deployment scalability.

[2] Physics of Language Models: Part 4.2, Canon Layers at Scale where Synthetic Pretraining Resonates in Reality cs.CL | cs.AIPDF

Zeyuan Allen-Zhu

TL;DR: 论文通过64次从零预训练（1.3–8B参数、1–2T Nemotron-CC token）比较Transformer与三类线性模型，发现小规模下架构差异易被调参掩盖，而在1–2T token规模下Canon层可稳定提升Transformer并节省约30%预训练token；同时提出GLA5和GDN2改进线性注意力，并揭示标准benchmark高分可能掩盖短上下文检索和简单两跳推理的缺陷。

Details

Motivation: 关注标准benchmark能否反映真正重要的架构差异，以及大规模预训练中Transformer与线性模型的能力边界；论文还试图说明小规模合成实验能否预测真实大规模训练中的架构表现。

Result: 在8B/1T设置下，Canon层使Transformer在相同MMLU准确率下节省约30%预训练token；带Canon的GLA在最简单线性模型中可匹配Gated DeltaNet并超过Mamba2。但即使8B/1T，匹配或超过Transformer的线性模型仍不能可靠检索500-token短上下文，且所有模型在简单两跳问题上接近随机猜测。

Insight: 创新点包括大规模从零预训练对照、Canon局部token混合层、GLA5/GDN2低秩输出门改进，以及用<100M合成实验预测大规模架构差异。客观看，论文的核心价值是揭示标准benchmark与真实上下文推理能力之间的错位，并提示应尽早将上下文推理样本纳入预训练数据。

Abstract: Do standard benchmarks see the architectural differences that matter? We study it with 64 from-scratch pretraining runs of Transformers and three linear-model families at 1.3-8B parameters on 1-2T Nemotron-CC tokens, at a cost of roughly 100k USD per 8B run. In academic-scale pretraining (1.3B parameters, 100B tokens), architectural differences can all but disappear when the models are carefully tuned. Instead, at 1-2T tokens they become measurable: Canon layers, which add local token mixing, consistently improve Transformers, saving about 30% of pretraining tokens for the same MMLU accuracy at 8B. We also introduce GLA5 and GDN2, which improve gated linear attention and Gated DeltaNet with low-rank output gates. Surprisingly, with Canon, the simplest linear model, gated linear attention, matches Gated DeltaNet and outperforms Mamba2 even at 8B/1T. Therefore, the extra machinery of Gated DeltaNet and Mamba2 may do little more than mix neighboring tokens. Even at 8B/1T, however, high scores on standard benchmarks can hide weaknesses in basic in-context skills. In particular, linear models that match or beat Transformers on these benchmarks cannot, in fact, reliably retrieve information from a short 500-token context. Moreover, no model, Transformer or linear, reliably beats random guessing on arguably the simplest two-hop question, stated in a few short sentences, even at this scale. These findings $\textbf{challenge traditional architecture research}$ based primarily on standard benchmark scores. In contrast, small synthetic playgrounds, which predicted the architectural differences above at <100M parameters, can reveal what these benchmarks miss. For practitioners, one lesson is to include in-context reasoning examples in pretraining data from an early stage, rather than reserve them for later mid-training, let alone post-training.

[3] Global Importance Estimation for KV Cache Eviction cs.CL | cs.AIPDF

Seojin Kim, Noseong Park

TL;DR: 论文提出免训练的KV缓存驱逐方法PRKV，通过在注意力诱导的转移图上运行个性化PageRank来估计token的多跳全局重要性，从而在长上下文LLM推理中压缩KV缓存。方法无需修改模型，并在长上下文理解、稀疏检索和思维链推理任务上验证了其有效性。

Details

Motivation: 长上下文LLM推理中KV缓存内存占用是核心瓶颈；现有驱逐方法多依赖最近性或瞬时注意力，难以捕捉token的全局影响。论文希望在不训练、不改模型的前提下更准确地评估token重要性。

Result: 在多个LLM系列和长上下文理解、稀疏检索、思维链推理基准上，PRKV在中等至激进压缩率（最高90%）下取得与已有驱逐基线有竞争力的任务表现，同时降低峰值内存且端到端开销较低。

Insight: 创新点在于将KV驱逐中的token重要性建模为注意力图上的多跳全局影响传播，并用个性化PageRank实现免训练估计；客观看，其价值在于把图排序思想引入缓存驱逐，兼顾全局重要性与低开销，提供了精度-内存折中。

Abstract: Memory consumption of the key-value (KV) cache remains a central bottleneck for long-context LLM inference. We introduce PRKV, a training-free KV eviction method that estimates multi-hop global token importance by applying Personalized PageRank over attention-induced transition graphs. This enables importance propagation beyond recency or instantaneous attention, without modifying the model. We benchmark PRKV on long-context understanding, sparse retrieval, and chain-of-thought reasoning tasks using multiple LLM families. Under moderate to aggressive compression ratios, including up to 90%, PRKV achieves competitive task performance against established eviction baselines while reducing peak memory usage with low end-to-end overhead. These results demonstrate that multi-hop global influence estimation yields a favorable accuracy–memory trade-off for scalable long-context inference.

[4] What the model knows but does not say: monitoring medical LLMs through verbalized thoughts cs.CL | cs.AIPDF

Frederik Hauke, Jan Clusmann, Hanna Kreutzer, Christiane Kuhl, Tianyu Han

TL;DR: 论文提出通过自然语言自编码器将多模态医学大模型（Gemma-3-27B）的内部激活转化为可读文本，形成“思维通道”，并用输入盲判官分别审查思维通道和模型输出。实验表明，在多种临床攻击目标、输入模态和投递机制下，思维通道比输出更能发现被操纵模型隐藏的注入行为，且无需重新训练。

Details

Motivation: 现有临床语言模型安全监测主要关注输入和输出，但被操纵的模型可能给出看似正常的回答，从而绕过输出层检查。论文希望利用模型内部状态中“知道但没说”的信息，提升对医学LLM操纵和有害请求的监测能力。

Result: 在遗漏病理和隐蔽推荐药物两类临床攻击、两种输入模态和两种投递机制共八个注入条件下，思维通道发现注入的比率均显著高于答案通道（p<0.05），且两者在干净输入上误报率接近零；遗漏病理结果可迁移到Llama-3.3-70B。思维通道还能在模型阅读不安全临床请求、尚未决定是否拒绝时识别其有害性。

Insight: 创新点在于将模型激活用现成自然语言自编码器转为文本，并用通用输入盲判官独立审查“思维通道”，把内部状态作为输出之外的安全监测信号。该方法无需重训练，具有一定跨模型泛化性，为医学LLM的可解释安全监控提供了可借鉴思路。

Abstract: Efforts to keep clinical language models safe have concentrated on what enters and leaves the model, but a manipulated model can return an answer that looks entirely ordinary. Here we show that the model’s own internal state, rendered in plain language, exposes manipulations the output conceals. Using a natural-language autoencoder, we translate the activations of a multimodal LLM (Gemma-3-27B) into text and let an input-blind judge read this verbalized internal state (the thoughts channel) and the model’s answer separately. Across two clinical attack objectives (omitting a pathology, covertly recommending a drug), two input modalities, and two delivery mechanisms, the thoughts channel caught the injection significantly more often than the answer in all eight injected conditions (p<0.05), while both channels held near-zero false-positive rates on clean inputs; the omission result carried over to Llama-3.3-70B. The thoughts channel also registered unsafe clinical requests as harmful while the model was still reading them, before it decided whether to refuse. None of this requires retraining: an off-the-shelf verbalizer and a general-purpose judge are enough.

[5] Forget Freely, Grep Later: Lossless, Index-Free Memory for Long-Horizon LLM Agents cs.CL | cs.AIPDF

Ziyan Yang, Taiwei Shi, Jieyu Zhao

TL;DR: 论文提出 GrepMem，一种面向长程 LLM Agent 的无损、无索引记忆机制：将 Agent 完整历史追加写入纯文本日志，并通过正则搜索按需召回。它不依赖摘要、掩码、结构化记忆、嵌入、检索索引或额外模型，并用强化学习训练 Qwen3.5-9B 在五种上下文管理策略下使用该机制。实验表明其在多项长上下文记忆与问答任务上显著优于有损基线，并保持较优的准确率- token 成本权衡。

Details

Motivation: 长程 LLM Agent 常超出上下文窗口，现有摘要、掩码或结构化记忆需提前决定保留和检索内容，可能造成有损压缩并丢失未来所需细节。论文希望实现无损、无需复杂索引基础设施的长期记忆。

Result: GrepMem 在 ContextBench 上达到 95-97% 准确率，而有损基线最高仅 21%；在 BCP-Multi 上比 SUPO 的摘要方法高 8.8 个百分点。它可扩展到更长输入流，并在五个评估任务上处于准确率- token 成本帕累托前沿。

Insight: 创新点在于用“追加式纯文本日志 + 正则搜索”替代复杂结构化记忆，实现无损、无索引、按需召回，且无需嵌入、记忆抽取或额外模型。客观看，其价值在于以极简系统设计重新审视长程 Agent 记忆，并证明简单 grep 式检索在准确率与成本权衡上可具竞争力。

Abstract: Long-horizon LLM agents often exceed their context windows, forcing them to discard information that may be needed later. Existing context management strategies rely on summarization, masking, or structured memory, requiring decisions about what to preserve or how to retrieve it before future needs are known. However, these methods can introduce lossy compression, potentially discarding details needed for future tasks. To address this limitation, we introduce GrepMem, a simple, lossless, and index-free memory mechanism built on two components: an append-only plain-text log of the agent’s complete history and a regular-expression search tool for on-demand recall. Unlike structured memory systems, GrepMem requires no embeddings, retrieval index, memory extraction, or additional models. We train Qwen3.5-9B with reinforcement learning under five context management strategies and evaluate on multi-question research sessions, long-stream recall, and agent-trajectory question answering. GrepMem achieves 95-97% accuracy on ContextBench, versus at most 21% for lossy baselines, and outperforms SUPO’s summarization by 8.8 percentage points on BCP-Multi. GrepMem scales effectively to increasingly long input streams while remaining on the accuracy-token-cost Pareto frontier across all five evaluation tasks. Our results show that a plain-text log and grep provide a simple but competitive approach to long-horizon agent memory, without requiring complex structured memory infrastructure.

[6] Enhancing Understanding of Language Models with Varied Explanations cs.CLPDF

Jingyi Sun

TL;DR: 该论文围绕语言模型在复杂推理任务中的可解释性展开，系统研究高亮解释、自然语言解释和思维链推理三类解释形式。论文提出多属性/多类型高亮解释评估框架及思维链忠实性指标元评估框架，提出两种生成忠实、简洁且连贯的自然语言解释的技术，并通过人机交互研究探讨证据与解释在事实核查中帮助用户验证模型裁决的作用。

Details

Motivation: 语言模型预测难以解释和验证，用户需要可靠解释来判断是否信任模型输出；现有解释需兼顾忠实性、可理解性和实用性，但生成满足多质量维度的解释及跨形式可靠评估仍具挑战。

Result: 摘要未给出具体定量结果或benchmark/SOTA对比，主要贡献为评估框架、解释生成技术和人机交互研究，属于方法与评估体系层面的工作。

Insight: 创新点在于从评估到生成再到人机验证的系统性研究：构建高亮解释的多属性评估与思维链忠实性指标元评估，生成兼顾忠实性与简洁连贯的自然语言解释，并研究事实核查场景中证据和解释对用户验证模型裁决的作用。

Abstract: Language models are increasingly used to support human judgement in complex reasoning tasks, yet their predictions often remain difficult to interpret and validate. Explainability techniques provide insight into model decision-making, helping expose errors, failure modes, and potential bias so users can decide whether to rely on model outputs. Explanations are simplified approximations of a language model’s internal workings. The goal is to obtain explanations that reflect the underlying computation as closely as possible (faithfulness), are understandable to users (plausibility), and help them achieve their intended goals (utility). Evaluation protocols are essential for assessing these dimensions. This thesis studies highlight explanations, natural language explanations, and chain-of-thought reasoning. These forms offer complementary perspectives on model behaviour and exhibit distinct strengths and limitations across tasks. Producing explanations that satisfy multiple quality dimensions remains challenging, as does evaluating them reliably across forms and settings. This thesis aims to improve people’s understanding of models’ decision-making processes in complex reasoning tasks. First, it proposes two evaluation frameworks for assessing highlight explanations across multiple properties and explanation types, together with a meta-evaluation framework for examining the reliability of existing faithfulness metrics for chain-of-thought explanations. Second, it proposes two techniques that generate natural language explanations designed to be faithful to the model’s prediction process while remaining concise, coherent, and helpful for understanding model decisions. Finally, a study of human-AI interaction explores the roles of evidence and explanations in helping users validate model verdicts in a fact-checking setting.

[7] Incorporating argumentation frameworks for LLM decision-making: Task-specific design and evaluation cs.CL | cs.AIPDF

Bochen Han, Anshu Xiong, Mingyang Ma

TL;DR: 论文研究将论辩框架（AFs）引入LLM决策流程，先抽象出四阶段工作流并在辩论式决策与检索式事实核查任务上评估现有论辩增强方法，发现其性能提升并不稳定。为此提出两个任务特定方法：Qnt-Arg围绕辩论材料中的原始发言者组织论点，并在渐进语义下聚合模型估计的论点强度与候选答案先验；Qlt-Arg利用可信度估计建立冲突证据间的偏好，并通过grounded语义选择可接受证据供后续LLM推理。实验分析了生成模型与决策模型的作用，并验证了两种方法在对应任务上的有效性。

Details

Motivation: 论辩框架能显式表示和消解冲突，但其融入LLM决策的收益取决于论点及关系如何构建、评估和接入决策过程。现有论辩增强方法在不同任务上表现不一致，因此需要针对任务特点设计论辩构建与语义推理方式。

Result: 在辩论式决策设置中，Qnt-Arg在匹配配置下持续优于其他论辩增强方法；在检索式事实核查任务中，Qlt-Arg相比基线提升了性能。结果同时表明生成模型与决策模型的选择会影响方法效果。

Insight: 创新点在于提出面向具体任务的论辩增强LLM决策方法，并强调论辩构建、模型选择与形式语义推理角色需与任务对齐。客观来看，其价值在于将形式论辩的冲突消解机制与LLM推理结合，并通过任务特定设计提升论辩增强方法的稳定性和可解释性。

Abstract: Argumentation frameworks (AFs) provide explicit, formal mechanisms for representing and resolving conflicts and thus have been incorporated into large language model (LLM) decision-making. The benefits vary and depend on how arguments and their relations are constructed, evaluated, and incorporated into the decision process. {We abstract a workflow consisting of four stages based on existing argumentation-augmented methods} and investigate their design choices over tasks of debate-based decision-making and retrieval-based fact verification. Our evaluation with recent LLMs shows that existing methods do not consistently improve performance across the considered tasks. Motivated by this observation, we develop two task-specific methods. The first is called Qnt-Arg, which organizes arguments around the original speakers in debate materials and aggregates model-estimated argument strengths and candidate-answer priors under gradual semantics. The second is Qlt-Arg that uses credibility estimates to establish preferences between conflicting evidence items and applies grounded semantics to select acceptable evidence for subsequent LLM inference. We examine the roles of generative and decision models within the two methods. In the debate setting, Qnt-Arg consistently outperforms other argumentation-augmented methods across matched configurations; Qlt-Arg improves retrieval-based fact-verification performance compared with the baselines. These findings demonstrate the value of combining formal argumentation and LLM while highlighting the importance of aligning with tasks in argumentation construction, model selection, and the role of formal semantic reasoning.

[8] Evolve Where It Matters: Coverage-Guided Self-Evolution of Agent Skills cs.CL | cs.AIPDF

Boyin Tan, Jiadong Zhang, Xiaosong Ma, Chun Jason Xue, Xiaowei Huang

TL;DR: 论文提出 SkillPlumber，一个基于指令级覆盖率引导的智能体技能自进化框架。它将技能执行中每条指令标记为已遵循、未遵循或未覆盖，从而区分失败是来自错误指令还是智能体未遵循指令，并据此定向修订技能。系统还通过根因分析智能体将标签转化为修订问题并在后续运行中验证，同时用维护器将跨任务调查蒸馏为共享策略库。

Details

Motivation: 现有智能体自我进化方法通常在整次运行层面根据结果或错误位置判断失败，无法区分失败源于技能指令本身错误，还是智能体没有正确遵循指令，导致技能修订缺乏针对性。论文希望利用指令级覆盖率定位技能真正不足的地方，实现更精准的技能自进化。

Result: 在三个基准和三个执行器上，SkillPlumber 解决 64.7% 到 70.5% 的任务，比初始技能高 25.6 到 30.4 个百分点，比最强基线高 8.7 到 14.0 个百分点。消融显示，在 SkillsBench 上使用 GPT-5.5 时，移除覆盖率机制会使恢复的失败减少 20.8%。

Insight: 创新点在于引入指令级覆盖率标签来分离“指令错误”和“未遵循指令”两类失败原因，并将标签转化为可验证的技能修订问题。进一步地，系统通过持续根因分析和跨任务策略库蒸馏，把局部修订经验沉淀为共享策略，提升技能自进化的针对性和复用性。

Abstract: Agent skills package procedural knowledge as natural-language instructions, and a skill helps only when its instructions are correct and properly followed by the agent. Self-evolving agents revise their own skills from the runs they make, but existing methods judge each run as a whole, by its outcome or by where it went wrong, and cannot tell whether a failure comes from a wrong instruction or from one the agent did not follow. Instruction-level coverage was introduced to test skills and labels each instruction of a run as follow, not-follow, or not covered, which separates these causes. We leverage it for skill self-evolution with SkillPlumber, a framework in which an agent evolves its own skills where coverage shows they fall short. A persistent root cause analysis agent turns each label into a revision question and checks each revision against the next run, while a maintainer distills investigations across tasks into a shared strategy library. Across three benchmarks and three executors, SkillPlumber solves 64.7% to 70.5% of the tasks, 25.6 to 30.4 percentage points higher than the initial skill and 8.7 to 14.0 percentage points higher than the strongest baseline, and removing coverage reduces recovered failures by 20.8% on SkillsBench with GPT-5.5.

Details

Motivation: 大推理模型生成长推理轨迹时存在大量冗余、低效用的token（如循环、自验证、纠正），但这些token可能获得高注意力分数，难以直接移除而不损害推理过程，因此需要针对性的KV缓存压缩。

Abstract: KV cache compression is critical for large reasoning models, where most of the cost comes from long generated reasoning traces rather than the input prompt. These traces often contain redundant and low-utility tokens due to looping, self-verification, and corrections. However, such tokens may still receive high attention scores, making it difficult to remove them without harming the reasoning process. We propose RoCoKV, a KV cache compression method designed for large reasoning models. RoCoKV introduces two key components. First, uncertainty-guided adaptive pruning adjusts the compression strength based on the model’s confidence, allocating more memory during uncertain phases and applying stronger pruning when the model is confident. Second, redundancy-aware token selection reduces the impact of tokens that are highly similar to recent reasoning content, encouraging the model to retain more informative and diverse tokens. Beyond the algorithm, we co-design RoCoKV with the serving stack: we develop fused three-phase CUDA decode kernels and integrate them into vLLM as a drop-in plugin that preserves its paged KV layout and CUDA graph capture, turning algorithmic savings into real end-to-end speedups. Extensive experiments across three reasoning benchmarks and two reasoning models demonstrate that RoCoKV consistently improves reasoning accuracy under constrained KV cache budgets, achieving higher accuracy compared to state-of-the-art KV cache compression methods. For DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Llama-8B in vLLM, RoCoKV achieves a 1.27X speedup and 38% higher throughput on AIME25, the most challenging evaluated reasoning benchmark, and a 1.43X speedup and 45% higher throughput on the long synthetic benchmark, compared with Full-KV.

Abstract: Large language models demonstrate strong medical question-answering capabilities, yet reasoning performance remains uneven across languages. Process reward models (PRMs) provide step-level feedback for improving reasoning, but a fixed PRM may become increasingly misaligned with the trajectory distribution of an evolving policy during reinforcement learning, thereby reducing the reliability of its supervision. We propose a policy–reward alternating training framework that jointly adapts a medical PRM and optimizes a multilingual reasoning policy. Abstract: We present DRVEXO-4B-Unified, a modular multimodal assistant built around a 4B language backbone. [12] ColorRef: Calibrating Verbal Feedback for Color Revision cs.CLPDF

Details

Motivation: 人类在要求修改设计时很少指定精确颜色，只说“稍暗一点”或“更蓝一些”。语言模型必须同时推断修改方向与修改幅度。现有评估只看整体改进，无法判断模型是否真正正确理解了反馈强度，因此论文要研究“按方向修改”与“按合适幅度修改”之间的区别。

Result: 在 1,000 组描述-目标对上，三轮确定性 oracle 反馈将平均目标距离从 42.33 降至 22.15 ΔE76；在 128 个留出起始颜色上，校准将单步平均误差从 12.36 降至 6.86（Qwen3-14B）、从 11.06 降至 5.89（Gemma 12B），优于固定分级措辞规则；收益主要集中在大幅修改上，许多样本未发生变化，阈值成功率没有一致提升。

Insight: 创新点在于将“反馈遵循”拆解为方向理解与幅度校准两个维度，并指出现有模型在幅度上的系统性失败；方法上通过测量每个接收模型对分级副词的实际响应步长来选择措辞，实现按模型校准的语言反馈。客观来看，这一“先测量后匹配”的思路可迁移到其他连续属性（如亮度、大小、风格强度）的语言控制任务中。

Abstract: People rarely specify an exact color when asking someone to revise a design: they might say \textit{a little darker} or \textit{more blue} after seeing a proposal. A language-model assistant must then infer not only which way to change the color, but how far. We study this distinction with \textsc{ColorRef}, a color reference game in which a model guesses a color from a description and a teacher, knowing a recorded target hidden from the guesser, supplies verbal corrections. On 1{,}000 crowd-sourced description–target pairs, three rounds of deterministic oracle feedback reduce mean target distance from 42.33 to 22.15 $ΔE_{76}$. However, those corrections provide additional information, and improvement alone does not establish well-calibrated feedback following. A controlled Qwen3-14B diagnostic reveals a more specific failure: following a requested direction and ordering graded phrases do not guarantee a suitable step size; strong modifiers can induce excessive changes. We therefore measure each receiver’s typical response to \textit{a little}, \textit{somewhat}, and \textit{much} on separate colors and select wording matched to its observed steps. On 128 held-out starting colors, calibration reduces one-step mean error from 12.36 to 6.86 for Qwen3-14B and from 11.06 to 5.89 for Gemma 4 12B against a fixed graded-wording rule. The benefit is concentrated on large corrections, with many unchanged cases and no consistent increase in threshold success. Our findings show that following the direction of feedback is not the same as making an appropriately sized revision.

We investigate whether continuous location representations improve this generalization and encourage geographic knowledge to become spatially organized within an LLM. We frame this organization as a latent spatial field: a learned mapping from locations to descriptions connected through spatial dependencies. Building on a continuous spatial encoder, we project location representations into the model’s embedding space and supply them as spatial tokens. We evaluate the resulting models on two newly curated geo-text benchmarks for Los Angeles and London using two complementary assessments. Geospatial captioning tests both memory and spatial generalization by generating descriptions at observed locations and at locations beyond the spatial coverage of the training observations. Across multiple LLM families, continuous location representations improve captioning at unobserved locations relative to textual coordinates, while their generated descriptions yield modest but consistent improvements in downstream grounding. STARK maintains an Episodic Graph which records interactions as temporally, causally, and semantically linked episodes; and an Insight Graph which represents LLM-extracted behavioral signals scored by how many events support them, connected by the very events that led to them. Abstract: Diffusion language models offer an architectural route to low-budget generation by updating multiple tokens in parallel, which can reduce the number of sequential decoding steps relative to token-by-token autoregressive decoding. However, realizing this advantage at low budgets is challenging, as generation quality often declines when the number of denoising and refinement steps is reduced. Editable diffusion models address this quality loss through post-draft refinement, but useful intermediate results may still be lost before final submission. We introduce CaRR (Commitment-aware Refinement and Recovery), a training-free framework which improves submission quality from a fixed editable checkpoint through updated-context refill and trajectory-based recovery within its existing forward schedule. Token Commitment Remasking (TCR) remasks selected native pure replacements and refills them on the next scheduled edit forward using the updated context. Submission Commitment Recovery (SCR) uses the resulting refinement trajectory to recover supported candidates when the final response does not satisfy task-specific output requirements. Experiments on code generation and mathematical reasoning show consistent gains in average accuracy over native ME-DLM across four inference budgets, with the largest gains under tight budgets. Abstract: Automated hate speech detection degrades over time as everyday vocabulary is weaponized into covert slurs while retaining original benign meanings (polysemy retention). Filtering > 20,000 candidates, our pipeline combines historical (1770-1964) and modern (2001-2024) corpus frequency grounding, masked definition-level scoring to mitigate LLM temporal bias, and a dual-persona reasoning prompt paradigm. [17] Verifiable Logic in Medical LLMs: A Critical Review of Agentic Clinical Reasoning Optimization and Neuro-Symbolic Grounding cs.CL | cs.AI | cs.CY | cs.LGPDF

Youssef ElNahas, Adel Ammar, Soumaya Chaffar, Wadii Boulila

TL;DR: 本文是一篇批判性综述，系统梳理医疗大语言模型中的临床幻觉、智能体幻觉级联、过程奖励建模和神经符号接地四类范式，指出仅凭最终答案评估会漏检无效推理，并提出将智能体失败模式映射到符号干预点的概念框架，主张临床AI认证应同时审计推理轨迹和静态基准准确率。

Details

Motivation: 临床LLM通常只按最终答案是否正确来评判，但模型可能通过无效推理得到流畅且看似合理的输出，自信幻觉、因果无效推断和虚假人口学捷径可绕过传统静态评估，因此需要关注推理过程的可验证性与系统架构安全。

Result: 综述指出，带符号否决层的系统在其编码知识域内可提供相对保证，并在其采用的安全指标上报告较大提升；但这些结果多来自单项研究、模拟或小样本设置，不能直接与神经基线比较，过程奖励模型仍受训练数据统计特性限制。

Abstract: Clinical large language models are usually judged by whether the final answer is right. Yet a model can reach a fluent, plausible output through invalid reasoning that static benchmarks do not detect, and confident hallucination, causally invalid inference, and spurious demographic shortcuts can pass conventional evaluation. This critical review covers four paradigms in turn: how clinical hallucination arises and why static metrics miss it; the agentic hallucination cascade, in which multi-agent and tool-augmented systems propagate and amplify early reasoning errors; process reward modeling as step-level supervision; and neuro-symbolic grounding, in which formal logic engines, ontology reasoners, and knowledge graphs place verifiable constraints on neural output. Within their encoded domains, systems with symbolic veto layers offer guarantees that hold relative to their encoded knowledge, and they report large gains on the safety measures they use. These results come from single studies, mostly in simulated or small-sample settings, and are not directly comparable with neural baselines. Process reward models remain bounded by the statistics of their training data. We propose a conceptual framework that maps each agentic failure mode to a symbolic intervention point, and we argue that clinical AI safety depends substantially on system architecture. Because current regulation emphasizes performance and does not require verification of reasoning steps, we argue that responsible certification should also audit reasoning trajectories alongside accuracy on static benchmarks.

Details

Motivation: 联合识别语音和非语言发声面临训练数据稀缺、事件标签不准确或缺失的挑战，论文旨在通过数据挖掘和标注细化来扩展和精炼监督信号。

Abstract: Joint recognition of speech and non-verbal vocalizations (NVVs) is hindered by scarce event-rich training data and inaccurate or missing event labels. We present Xing-Audio-NVVASR, an end-to-end recognizer trained through progressive data mining and iterative supervision refinement. Guided by unified acoustic criteria, two heterogeneous models cross-audit annotations, flagging disagreements for teacher reannotation. To uncover shared omissions that model agreement may conceal, complementary breath detectors screen the entire corpus for human review. Human-confirmed labels accumulate across rounds and are preserved during subsequent refinement. Over five rounds, we refine annotations for 170,000 utterances from public corpora and incorporate 630,000 mined utterances. The resulting corpus of approximately 800,000 utterances is used to train a single 1.7B-parameter model that transcribes speech with inline tags for six NVV classes. Across four human-annotated benchmarks, Xing-Audio-NVVASR ranks first among nine models in both composite score and six-class Macro-F1. It achieves a mean composite score of 64.46, surpassing the strongest comparison model, StepAudio 3 Chat Preview, by 6.03 points, and exceeds the annotation teacher’s breathing F1 by 14.40 to 31.57 percentage points. We plan to release the model weights, inference code, four evaluation benchmarks, annotation guidelines, and evaluation code.

Details

Motivation: 在保护敏感数据和控制部署成本的需求下，希望用本地部署的开源权重 LLM 自动完成电子表格分析；但复杂表格任务同时需要任务推理和工作簿理解，预定义操作接口还带来工具选择与调用负担。

Abstract: Spreadsheets are widely used for data analysis and supporting decision-making. Automating spreadsheet analysis while protecting sensitive data and controlling deployment costs motivates locally deployed open-weight large language models (LLMs). Meanwhile, complex spreadsheet tasks require both task reasoning and workbook understanding, while predefined operation interfaces impose additional tool-selection and invocation demands. We introduce SheetAlign, a spreadsheet manipulation framework with stage-isolated policy learning, organized around where to operate, what to change, and how to implement transformations. Task-guided inspection and API grounding anchor code generation in workbook observations and available operations, while stage-isolated learning optimizes program generation and review using edit-progress rewards. At inference, execution consistency selects programs through agreement among workbook states. On SpreadsheetBench, our grounded task interface improves Qwen3-4B’s accuracy from 13.5% to 29.2% on average without training. [20] AltRA-Test: An Alternative Annotator Test for LLM Judges Without Shared Annotators cs.CL | cs.AIPDF

Nagham Omar, Rotem Dror, Almog Gueta, Avigdor Gal, Roi Reichart

TL;DR: 论文提出 AltRA-Test，一种无需所有条目由同一批标注者标注的统计检验，用于判断 LLM 能否在标注/评审任务中替代人类标注者。方法通过 bootstrap 重采样条目并构造配对的“全人类面板”和“人类+LLM混合面板”，以混合面板的标注者间一致性（IAA）是否统计非劣于全人类面板来支持替代决策。作者证明了检验的一致性，并在文本、图像、音频、视频的20个数据集上测试18个LLM和5种提示技术。

Details

Motivation: LLM 越来越多被用作评审者和评分者来替代人类标注，但这种替代是否在统计上合理尚缺可靠检验；已有 Alt-Test 要求所有条目由相同标注者标注，难以适用于大规模标注中不同条目由不同标注者子集标注的场景。

Result: 模拟实验中，在已知 LLM 标注优于或劣于人类时，AltRA-Test 比现有基线做出更多正确的替代决策；真实数据实验中约100个条目通常足够，LLM 在约一半设置中通过检验，且主要取决于任务。

Insight: 创新点在于将 LLM 替代人类标注问题转化为混合人-LLM面板相对全人类面板的 IAA 非劣性统计检验，并用 bootstrap 重采样适配无共享标注者的稀疏标注设置，同时给出渐近水平 alpha 的一致性理论保证。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used as judges and graders to replace human annotators, raising the question of when such replacement is statistically justified. To support such decisions, the Alternative Annotator Test (Alt-Test) was introduced for dense settings where all items are annotated by the same annotators. However, in many large-scale annotation settings, items are annotated by different subsets of annotators. We therefore propose the Alternative Annotator Test with Resampled Annotations (AltRA-Test), a statistical test that does not require that all items are annotated by the same annotators. AltRA-Test uses a bootstrap that resamples items, draws k human annotations for each item, and substitutes one of them with an LLM annotation. This produces paired all-human and mixed human-LLM panels. A replacement is supported when the inter-annotator agreement (IAA) of the mixed panel is statistically non-inferior to the IAA of the all-human panel, i.e., when the LLM preserves the signal that the human annotators share. We establish that AltRA-Test is a consistent test of asymptotic level alpha. We then evaluate it through simulations and real-world experiments in which we test 18 LLMs and 5 prompting techniques on 20 datasets across text, image, audio, and video. Across these evaluations, about 100 items often suffice for the test. In simulations, where we know whether the LLM is a better or a worse annotator than the humans, AltRA-Test makes more correct replacement decisions than existing baselines. In real-world data experiments, LLMs pass the test in about half of the setups, depending mainly on the task. Since replacement is not always justified, we encourage researchers to test it for their task.

Atrey Desai, Deniz Böloni-Turgut, Ty Brassington, Minjoon Choi, Jenna Russell

Details

Motivation: 论文关注在线文本作者匿名性面临的现实威胁：现有AI文本检测与作者归属通常依赖训练检测器或额外信息，而前沿模型可能仅凭预训练中见过的大量写作就能完成归属，削弱匿名表达的保护。

Result: 前沿模型在AI/人类判断上可匹配Pangram等训练检测器；Opus 5.5对Anthropic、OpenAI、Google生成文本的实验室识别准确率达98%；Fable 5.2对2026年10-11月公开同行评审作者识别率为39%，Nature子集达78%，对活跃Reddit用户评论用户名识别率为69%。准确率受预训练曝光和推理努力影响。

Insight: 创新点在于系统评估前沿模型的零样本三层作者归属能力，并展示其可解释性和对真实匿名文本（同行评审、Reddit）的识别效果；客观看，重要启示是模型对作者写作风格的记忆与熟悉度可预测可识别性，在线匿名防御面临更大挑战。

Abstract: Given a single text and a simple zero-shot prompt (no web access, tools, candidate lists, or writing samples), frontier models can answer “who wrote this?” at three increasingly specific levels: (1) whether an AI or human wrote it, (2) if an AI, which lab, and (3) if a human, often exactly who. The latest models (Claude Fable 5.2, GPT-6.1 Sol, Gemini 4 Carbon) match trained detectors like Pangram while also explaining their predictions in natural language, and Opus 5.5 names the correct lab for 98% of texts generated by models from Anthropic, OpenAI, and Google. Most strikingly, Fable 5.2 correctly names the author of 39% of public peer reviews published in Oct-Nov 2026 (78% on a subset of reviews from Nature), and identifies the username behind 69% of comments from active Redditors (with >500 posts over the last year). Accuracy depends on exposure to an author’s writing in pretraining data and on reasoning effort, and simply asking a model how familiar it is with someone predicts whether it can identify their writing. For anyone who has published enough writing online, frontier models present a greater threat to anonymity than commonly assumed, and defenses are limited.

Hieu Minh Duong, Todd Gary, Eugene Levin, Long Nguyen

TL;DR: 论文提出STAGE框架，用于弱监督跨推文事件因果发现。它先从局部因果种子检索跨推文语义相关事件，并用因果评分器构建弱标注跨推文因果对，再以这些监督信号训练基于语义增强事件表示和时空上下文图的多头图注意力网络。方法在加州山火和哈维飓风数据上验证，表明可扩展跨推文弱监督能显著提升因果发现性能。

Details

Motivation: 灾害相关社交媒体中的因果事件常分散在独立帖子中，跨推文因果监督稀缺且难以大规模建模；论文旨在解决跨推文事件因果发现中的弱监督构建与关系建模难题。

Result: 在California Wildfire和Hurricane Harvey的人工金标准跨推文测试集上，相比仅局部监督，跨推文监督使AUPRC分别提升0.340和0.107；域内AUROC达到0.885和0.710，并与15个基线比较。跨灾害实验显示存在不对称迁移，表明方法对数据集特定的时空偏移较敏感。

Abstract: Causal relations in disaster-related social media of- ten span independent posts, making cross-tweet causal discovery difficult to supervise and model at scale. We propose STAGE, a framework that separates scalable cross-tweet weak-supervision construction from spatio-temporal relational learning over event occurrences. Starting from local causal seeds, STAGE retrieves semantically related event occurrences across tweets and uses a causal scorer to construct weakly labeled cross-tweet pairs. These pairs supervise a multi-head Graph Attention Network over semantically enriched event representations and a spatio- temporal context graph. We evaluate STAGE on California Wildfire and Hurricane Harvey using human-gold cross-tweet test sets and 15 baselines. Compared with local-only supervision, the constructed cross-tweet supervision improves STAGE AUPRC by 0.340 on Wildfire and 0.107 on Hurricane. STAGE achieves in-domain AUROC of 0.885 on Wildfire and 0.710 on Hurricane. Cross-disaster experiments reveal asymmetric transfer, highlighting sensitivity to dataset-specific spatio-temporal shifts. These results demonstrate the value of scalable cross-tweet supervision combined with relational event modeling for causal learning across fragmented disaster reports.

[23] What If Chain-of-Thought Is Not Enough? cs.CL | cs.AIPDF

Marc Jansen

TL;DR: 论文研究大语言模型的内部思维模式是否可以被有意改变并带来收益，提出用Question–Option–Criteria（QOC）结构化决策过程替代Chain-of-Thought，将任务分解为显式问题、候选选项和评估标准，并设计三代训练流程进行实验。结果表明，基于QOC的微调能提升答案质量，且模型规模足够大时收益更明显。

Details

Motivation: 论文关注Chain-of-Thought可能不足的问题，探索LLM的内部思考模型能否被有意训练改变，以及这种改变是否能提升推理和决策的可靠性、可解释性与任务适应性。

Result: 实验表明，QOC微调相对未微调基座模型能提升答案质量；当模型足够大、能够可靠执行QOC计算时，性能提升尤其显著。摘要未给出具体benchmark和数值结果。

Abstract: Reasoning capabilities in large language models have evolved from prompting-based methods to internalized model behaviors. In this paper, we investigate whether the internal thinking model of an LLM can be deliberately changed and whether such a change is beneficial. Instead of relying on Chain-of-Thought, we train models to follow Question–Option–Criteria (QOC), a structured decision process that decomposes a task into explicit questions, competing options, and evaluation criteria. We present a three-generation development pipeline that ranges from a basic QOC decision core to richer answer synthesis and statistically enriched QOC reasoning. We then evaluate the resulting models with respect to both internal consistency and output quality. Across the reported experiments, QOC-based fine-tuning improves answer quality relative to non-fine-tuned base models and yields particularly strong gains once models are large enough to execute the underlying QOC computations reliably. The results suggest that the internal thinking model of an LLM is trainable and that structured alternatives to Chain-of-Thought can provide a promising path toward more reliable, interpretable, and task-adapted decision support.

[24] Beam Model Technical Report cs.CL | cs.AIPDF

Reflection Team, Adam Abdulhamid, Osama Abuelsorour, Yasmin Alameddine, Carlo Alfano

TL;DR: Beam 是一个面向代码、推理和智能体工作负载优化的稀疏 MoE 模型，总参数 501.3B、激活参数 23.4B。它通过 23.8T token 预训练、两阶段 midtraining 获得 1M token 上下文，并经过大规模异步强化学习后训练，在保持前沿推理能力的同时显著降低推理算力需求，还结合量化与投机解码提升服务吞吐。

Details

Motivation: 论文旨在构建面向编码、推理和 agentic 任务的 frontier 级模型，同时解决这类模型推理成本高的问题，目标是以更少推理算力实现强推理能力，并支持超长上下文和高效部署。

Result: Beam 总参数 501.3B、激活参数 23.4B，推理算力需求比同类模型少 3-4 倍；支持 1M token 上下文，RL 训练使用 10.5K GB300 GPU、4 周生成超 1 亿 rollouts，评估中随 RL 算力增加能力持续提升且未见平台期；安全评估中对有害请求拒绝/转移率为 88.9%，对无害请求合规率为 81.5%，在开源权重 Pareto 前沿模型中取得最高有害请求拒绝/转移率。

Insight: 创新点包括稀疏 MoE 架构与硬件-模型协同设计、基于可验证正确性锚定数据的两阶段 midtraining 实现 1M 上下文、超大规模全异步 RL 后训练，以及 FP8/NVFP4 量化和 1.93B 参数投机解码器 DFlash 2 带来的高效部署；同时将安全拒绝/合规表现纳入 Pareto 前沿分析。

Abstract: Beam is a sparse Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) model with 501.3B total parameters and 23.4B active parameters, specifically built and optimized for coding, reasoning, and agentic workloads. Beam delivers frontier level reasoning, while requiring 3-4x less inference compute than many models of its class. Pretrained on 23.8T diverse, curated, high-quality tokens, Beam features a 1M-token context window achieved through a novel two-stage midtraining phase, built on verifiable, correctness-anchored data. Post-training leveraged a large-scale, fully asynchronous reinforcement learning (RL) campaign across 10.5K GB300 GPUs, generating over 100M rollouts over 4 weeks of training. We believe this is one of the largest scale RL runs conducted by any open lab to date. Across our evaluation suite, capabilities continued to improve as we increased RL compute, with no sign of a plateau. Supported by deep hardware-model co-design, FP8/NVFP4 quantization, and a 1.93B-parameter speculative decoding drafter (DFlash 2), Beam maximizes serving throughput with efficient, flexible deployment. Averaged across conversation turns and harm categories, Beam refuses or pivots on 88.9% of harmful requests while complying with 81.5% of harmless requests, achieving the highest refusal/pivot rate on harmful requests among open-weight models on the Pareto frontier.

[25] MemFolder: Structure-Guided Memory Folding for Efficient Agent Memory Management cs.CL | cs.CVPDF

Xuyang Liu, Chenwen Lu, Junxi Wang, Siyu Ren, Xinying Lin

TL;DR: 论文提出 MemFolder，一个结构引导的记忆折叠框架，用于高效管理长期智能体的外部记忆。它按顺序先减少记忆记录数量，再缩短检索上下文长度，通过共享记忆组和跨记录结构指导证据合并与紧凑表示。实验覆盖三个文本记忆基准和两个多模态基准，验证了方法在保持性能的同时显著降低记录数和上下文长度。

Details

Motivation: 长期智能体在交互中会积累大规模外部记忆，导致存储、检索和响应生成成本上升；记录数增长增加检索开销，检索上下文变长则增加 prefill 计算和解码时注意力成本。现有剪枝可能丢弃有用证据，而合并可能产生更少但更长的检索单元，因此需要同时压缩记录数和上下文长度。

Result: 论文在 LoCoMo、PersonaMem、LongMemEval 三个文本基准以及 M3-Bench-web、M3-Bench-robot 两个多模态基准上验证了方法。在 Mem0 上，RecordFolder 在记录数减少 50% 的情况下保留三个文本基准 99.0% 的原始平均性能，性能保留率超过最强基线 1.9 个百分点；结合 ContextFolder 后，现有记录压缩方法可进一步缩短输出记录并保留大部分性能。

Insight: 创新点在于结构引导的两阶段记忆折叠：共享记忆组，用跨记录结构同时指导证据合并和紧凑表示；RecordFolder 通过实体提及 grounding、代表性且互补的锚点选择、语义与实体亲和性及时间链合并兼容记录；ContextFolder 将组内原始记录编码为紧凑文本，并利用验证反馈修正无法恢复关键事实的编码。客观上看，将记录数压缩与上下文长度压缩解耦并串联，结合结构信息与验证反馈，是较有借鉴价值的设计。

Abstract: Long-term agents accumulate large external memory stores as interactions unfold, increasing the costs of storage, retrieval, and response generation. Efficiency is constrained by two coupled factors: a growing record count increases retrieval overhead, while longer retrieved contexts increase prefill computation and decoding-time attention costs. Existing methods prune or consolidate records, but pruning can discard useful evidence, whereas consolidation may produce fewer yet longer retrieval units. We introduce MemFolder, a structure-guided memory folding framework that sequentially reduces record count and context length. The framework comprises two core components that share memory groups, using cross-record structure to guide both evidence consolidation and compact representation. RecordFolder grounds entity mentions, progressively selects representative yet complementary anchors, and combines semantic and entity affinity with temporal chaining to consolidate compatible records into fewer retrieval units. ContextFolder encodes the original records within these groups into compact text and uses verification feedback to revise encodings when key facts cannot be recovered. Experiments on the three text-based memory benchmarks LoCoMo, PersonaMem, and LongMemEval and the two multimodal benchmarks M3-Bench-web and M3-Bench-robot demonstrate the strong performance of our methods. Notably, on Mem0, RecordFolder retains 99.0% of the original average performance across the three text-based benchmarks with 50% fewer records, exceeding the strongest baseline by 1.9 percentage points in performance retention. Combined with ContextFolder, existing record compression methods further shorten their output records while retaining most of their performance.

[26] Multimodal Error Detection in Chest X-ray Reports: A Large Language Model Reading Image Evidence cs.CLPDF

Yanda Cheng, Bo Wen, Yuhao Lu

TL;DR: 论文提出 Rad-Linter，用预训练胸片图像分类器给出九类病变分数，并用 LoRA 微调的 1.5B 参数语言模型判断这些图像证据与胸片报告是否一致，从而标记潜在报告错误。作者用 OpenI 构建受控的反事实四元组评测，与关键词和否定规则基线比较。

Details

Motivation: 胸片报告可能存在与图像证据不一致的错误，需要自动检测以供复核；论文关注在同一图像衍生分数条件下，语言模型能否比规则方法更准确地识别报告-分数不一致。

Result: 在 131 个四元组上，Rad-Linter 的 pair accuracy 为 98.5%，规则基线为 80.5%；四对全分类正确率为 94.4% 对 56.5%。在包含自动标注分歧的 181 个四元组上，pair accuracy 为 93.4% 对 75.3%；未微调的同一语言模型接近 50%。

Insight: 创新点在于将图像分类分数作为证据，用微调 LLM 检查报告一致性，并设计反事实四元组控制报告和分数单独出现时仅能到 50% 的评测设置；优势主要集中在需要理解发现如何被表述的条目类型。

Abstract: Rad-Linter checks chest X-ray reports against image-derived evidence to flag potential errors for review. A pretrained image classifier supplies scores for nine pathologies, and a 1.5B-parameter language model, fine-tuned with LoRA on 1,816 examples, checks their consistency with the report. We compare it with a keyword-and-negation rule baseline given the same reports and scores. Evaluation uses controlled report-score pairings from OpenI, with consistency labels derived from automatic report labels and score thresholds. Each counterfactual quad crosses two real reports with two real score vectors from other patients, creating two consistent and two inconsistent pairs. Every report and score vector occurs in both conditions, so either input alone permits only 50% accuracy. On 131 quads, Rad-Linter achieves 98.5% pair accuracy versus 80.5% for the rule baseline, and classifies all four pairs correctly in 94.4% of quads versus 56.5%. The checkers perform similarly on omission items, including unmentioned and negated findings; larger differences occur on the remaining item types, which require interpreting how findings are described. The same language model without fine-tuning remains near 50% accuracy. On a second set of 181 quads that also includes reports on which the automatic labelers disagree, pair accuracy is 93.4% versus 75.3%. These controlled results show that, given the same image-derived scores, a trained language model detects report-score inconsistencies more accurately than the tested rule baseline, with the advantage concentrated in interpreting how findings are worded.

[27] AutoMaster: Autonomous Post-Training by Making the Same Model a Better Teacher cs.CLPDF

Guoxin Chen, Shuang Sun, Fanzhe Meng, Xinyu Tang, Ruihua Song

TL;DR: 论文提出 AutoMaster 自主后训练系统，核心方法是 Skill-FT：利用自然语言技能在教师模型 rollout 中抑制不良行为、激发有效行为，再用得到的轨迹对目标模型进行监督微调。AutoMaster 让 LLM agent 自动完成轨迹分析、技能设计、数据选择和基于反馈的迭代优化。实验表明该方法能让同一固定模型成为更有效的教师，并提升目标模型在多种任务上的平均性能。

Details

Motivation: 仅以任务成功作为后训练信号并不充分，成功的教师轨迹也可能包含无依据假设、低效重复或浅层验证等不良行为。论文希望自动塑造教师行为，使固定模型在生成训练数据时表现更好。

Result: 在 Terminal、Search 和软件工程任务上，针对 4B 与 9B 目标模型，AutoMaster 相比基于成功无引导教师轨迹的 Vanilla SFT 平均提升 1.42–8.99 个百分点，且教师、任务池、训练集规模和 SFT 配方固定。消融还显示，SFT 时保留技能指令在全部六个设置中平均性能更高。

Insight: 创新点在于用自然语言技能引导教师 rollout 进行行为塑造，并通过 LLM agent 形成轨迹分析、技能设计、数据选择与评估反馈的自动闭环。客观来看，论文把后训练数据构建从“只筛成功轨迹”推进到“控制和优化教师行为”，并发现训练时保留技能上下文有助于目标模型内化有效行为。

Abstract: Task success alone does not specify which behaviors post-training should reinforce. Even successful teacher trajectories can contain unsupported assumptions, unproductive repetition, or superficial verification. We propose Skill-FT (Skill-Guided Fine-Tuning), which uses natural-language skills to suppress undesirable habits and elicit underrepresented effective behaviors during teacher rollouts, then fine-tunes a target model on the resulting trajectories. We introduce AutoMaster, an autonomous post-training system in which an LLM agent develops these interventions through trajectory analysis, skill design, data selection, and feedback from rollouts and target-model evaluation. Across Terminal, Search, and software engineering tasks with 4B and 9B target models, AutoMaster improves mean performance over Vanilla SFT on successful unguided teacher trajectories by 1.42-8.99 percentage points, with the teacher, task pool, training set size, and SFT recipe held fixed. Together with the ablations and trajectory analyses, these results suggest that behavioral shaping can make a fixed model a more effective teacher. We further find that retaining skill instructions during SFT yields higher mean performance than removing them across all six settings, despite their absence at evaluation and identical supervised outputs. These findings suggest that preserving skill context as part of a training demonstration can support better behavioral internalization.

[28] EgoPass-Bench: Identifying Objects That May Affect the Wearer’s Passage in Egocentric Video cs.CVPDF

Hongyu Wang, Chuhan Li, Congjin Xu, Xuandong Zhao, Hua Shen

TL;DR: 论文提出 EgoPass-Bench，一个面向真实世界第一视角视频的基准，用于评估多模态基础模型是否理解周围实体如何影响佩戴者的通行。该基准包含 284 段人工审核片段（Ray-Ban Meta Gen 2 智能眼镜录制）和 2,201 个问题，覆盖运动感知、指定实体的交互判断、以及无目标提示下的相关实体发现五类任务。评估 8 种基于帧和 6 种原生视频配置后发现，模型能较好识别物体运动，但在通行约束理解和相关实体发现上明显不足。

Details

Motivation: 现有第一视角环境评估多关注观察者定位、运动轨迹和动作可行性，却缺乏对动态观察者-实体交互、尤其是哪些物体会影响佩戴者通行的发现与解释能力的评估。论文旨在填补这一空白。

Result: 最强评估模型 GPT-6 Astra 在 394 个同目标比较中物体运动识别正确率达 91.37%，但通行约束变化识别仅 66.50%；在 574 个实体发现问题中，它比两人人类基线低 23.65±7.45 个百分点，同时比 always-empty 基线（36.76%）至少高 18.9 个百分点。

Insight: 创新点在于构建第一视角、观察者相对、面向通行约束的交互理解基准，并设置无目标线索的相关实体发现任务。客观来看，其价值在于揭示“准确运动识别不等于可靠交互理解”，强调模型需进一步推理实体与佩戴者物理通行之间的约束关系。

Abstract: Understanding an egocentric environment requires reasoning about how surrounding entities affect the observer’s actions. Existing evaluations of multimodal foundation models (MFMs) examine observer localization, motion trajectories, and action feasibility, but leave the discovery and interpretation of evolving observer-entity interactions insufficiently assessed. We introduce EgoPass-Bench, a benchmark for observer-relative interaction understanding in real-world egocentric video. EgoPass-Bench comprises 284 manually reviewed evaluation clips recorded using Ray-Ban Meta (Gen 2) smart glasses and 2,201 distinct questions across five tasks assessing motion perception, interaction judgments about specified entities, and relevant-entity discovery without target cues. Our evaluation of eight frame-based and six native-video configurations reveals a pronounced discrepancy between motion perception and interaction understanding. The strongest evaluated model, GPT-6 Astra, correctly recognizes object motion in 91.37% of 394 comparisons involving the same targets, but correctly identifies changes in passage constraints in only 66.50%. Across all 574 entity-discovery questions, it trails the two-person human baseline by $23.65 \pm 7.45$ percentage points (paired 95% CI), while exceeding the always-empty baseline (36.76%) by at least 18.9 percentage points. Further analysis reveals frequent inclusion of entities that neither pose a plausible physical conflict nor constrain passage, showing that accurate motion recognition does not ensure reliable relevant-entity discovery.

[29] Infer3D: Inverse Generative Modeling Enables Robust 3D Reconstruction cs.CV | cs.LG | cs.ROPDF

Amani Kiruga, Mark Hamilton, Joshua B. Tenenbaum, William T. Freeman, Vincent Sitzmann

TL;DR: 论文提出 Infer3D，一种逆生成建模框架，用于从单目图像鲁棒重建3D结构。该方法不采用传统前馈式模式匹配，而是优化生成模型的潜变量以匹配输入，并借助冻结的3D lifting网络约束优化空间，提升分布外场景下的重建精度。论文还引入自适应推理策略，对模糊OOD样本分配更多测试时计算。

Details

Motivation: 现有3D重建网络多采用摊销式前馈推理，速度快但在训练中未见的OOD场景下性能显著下降；受人类自上而下生成式视觉推断启发，论文希望提升极端或陌生视角下的泛化重建能力。

Result: 在多种可控合成和真实世界任务中，Infer3D的OOD重建精度优于在同等或更大数据集上训练的前馈网络；摘要未给出具体数值或明确SOTA声明，但强调其OOD鲁棒性优势。

Insight: 创新点在于将逆生成建模用于3D重建，通过恢复生成模型潜变量匹配单目输入，并用冻结3D lifting网络约束到多视图一致的3D流形以避免退化平坦输出；同时提出按样本难度自适应分配测试时计算，兼顾OOD鲁棒性与ID效率。

Abstract: Humans can understand the 3D structure of scenes even from highly unfamiliar or extreme viewpoints. Cognitive science suggests this generalization stems from a top-down generative inference strategy. In contrast, state-of-the-art 3D reconstruction neural networks typically rely on amortized, feedforward inference. While fast, this pattern-matching approach degrades when faced with out-of-distribution (OOD) scene conditions unseen during training. Inspired by the generative nature of human vision, we propose Infer3D, an inverse generative modeling framework that recovers the latent variables of a generative model to best match the monocular input. To prevent degenerate flat outputs, we couple the generative image prior with a frozen 3D lifting network that constrains the optimization space to a valid, multi-view-consistent 3D manifold. Across a variety of controlled synthetic and real-world tasks, Infer3D demonstrates superior OOD reconstruction accuracy compared to feedforward networks trained on equal or larger datasets. Furthermore, mirroring how human perception requires more time to process confusing stimuli, we introduce an adaptive inference strategy that expends more test-time compute on ambiguous OOD examples while efficiently routing in-distribution inputs. Project website: https://amanikiruga.github.io/infer3d-site/

[30] VisAlloc: Allocating Visual Supervision for On-Policy Self-Distillation cs.CV | cs.AIPDF

Haoran Xu, Tian Chen, Yuhe Wu, Yanbo Zhao, Junchi Liao

TL;DR: 论文提出 VisAlloc，用于多模态大模型在线自蒸馏中的视觉监督分配。该方法在样本级用正负裁剪配对教师筛选真正依赖视觉证据的样本，在 token 级用双 EMA 教师评分差异识别对视角敏感的 token，并进行反事实重加权。推理时仅需全图学生模型，无需额外教师或裁剪输入。

Details

Motivation: 在线自蒸馏中并非所有样本都同等受益于特权视觉指导，且对所有 token 均匀监督会稀释真正基于视觉证据的信号，因此需要筛选有效样本并细粒度分配 token 级视觉监督。

Result: 在八个 benchmark 上，4B 和 9B 模型分别取得 81.44 和 84.92 的宏平均，较 Vision-OPD 复现结果分别提升 0.97 和 0.52 个点。

Insight: 创新点在于双层视觉监督分配：样本级用正负空间裁剪配对教师过滤视觉依赖样本，token 级用视图敏感性和教师分布分歧选择需额外监督的 token，并通过多重门控与逆频率重分配控制蒸馏权重，同时保持推理零额外开销。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) offers a promising approach to improving visual reasoning in multimodal large language models (MLLMs). However, not all samples benefit equally from privileged visual guidance, and uniform token supervision can dilute visually grounded signals. We propose VisAlloc, a dual-level framework combining sample-level visual filtering with token-level counterfactual reweighting. A fixed paired-teacher filter retains samples answered correctly from an evidence-centered positive crop but incorrectly from a spatially displaced negative crop. At the token level, both EMA teachers score the same student-generated response, and their absolute log-probability differences measure view sensitivity. Additional supervision targets tokens admitted by sensitivity or teacher-distribution disagreement, with shared frozen-prior, template-bigram, and student–teacher divergence gates. Inverse-frequency redistribution preserves the total additional weight within each micro-batch, while the positive teacher remains the sole distillation target. Inference requires only the full-image student. Across eight benchmarks, our 4B and 9B models achieve macro-averages of 81.44 and 84.92, outperforming Vision-OPD reproductions by 0.97 and 0.52 points, respectively. Code on GitHub: https://github.com/Kaelyn01/VisAlloc

Hui Li, Pengfei Yang, Xinyi Feng, Junhong Liu, Tianyang Zheng

TL;DR: 论文提出 TALD，一个两阶段跨模态知识蒸馏框架，在类别对齐的 logit 空间中分解教师模型与目标模态参考学生模型的 logit，分离出模态通用和模态特有成分，并通过样本自适应调制器控制分解约束与融合权重，最终用融合后的模态通用成分监督学生模型，以缓解跨模态蒸馏中的负迁移。

Details

Motivation: 跨模态知识蒸馏中，源模态监督可能与目标模态的判别特性冲突，导致负迁移，因此需要识别并只迁移对目标模态有益的知识。

Result: 在视觉、音频、文本五个 benchmark 的十个迁移方向上，TALD 取得最高平均准确率增益和最高正迁移频率；相对无蒸馏训练在 84% 评估中提升，基线最高为 70%，并具有较好的准确率-效率权衡。

Insight: 创新点在于从 logit 层面进行可迁移性感知的模态通用/特有成分解耦，并结合样本自适应融合权重来抑制有害跨模态监督；客观看，其价值在于将负迁移缓解落到 logit 分解与自适应融合机制上。

Abstract: Cross-modal knowledge distillation can incur negative transfer when source-modality supervision conflicts with the target modality’s discriminative characteristics. We propose TALD, a two-stage framework for Transferability-Aware Logit Disentanglement in class-aligned logit space. Guided by task-dependent self-benefiting and cross-modal exclusivity criteria, a Residual Disentangler decomposes logits from a teacher and an independently trained target-modality reference student into modality-general and modality-specific components. A Sample-Adaptive Modulator regulates decomposition constraints and sample-specific fusion weights. The learned modality-general components are then adaptively fused to supervise the final student. Across five benchmarks spanning vision, audio, and text, TALD achieves the largest mean accuracy gain and highest positive-transfer frequency among the compared methods. Across ten transfer directions with five runs each, TALD improves over training without distillation in 84% of evaluations, compared with at most 70% for the baselines, while offering a favorable accuracy-efficiency trade-off. We plan to release the code.

[32] MV-JEPA: Post-Training Video Encoders for Cross-View Understanding cs.CVPDF

Yangchen Zhao, Ali Payani, Caiwen Ding

TL;DR: 论文提出MV-JEPA，一种针对JEPA视频编码器的多视角预测后训练方法。通过让共享编码器独立处理掩码上下文视图，并用训练时预测器联合预测掩码区域和完全留出视图的潜在token，交替进行跨视角预测与单视图掩码预测，从而在保留单视图推理能力的同时增强跨视角实例与时间对应关系。

Details

Motivation: 现有JEPA视频编码器的标准预训练仅在单图像或视频片段内预测特征，未显式利用同一物体或时刻的配对相机视图，导致学习到的表示难以可靠支持跨相机视角的匹配。

Result: 在单GPU上仅9000次后训练更新后，MV-JEPA在多个数据集上提升了跨视角实例检索和粗粒度跨相机时刻检索，在AE2上零样本动作阶段检索和冻结特征分类也有增益；冻结探针评估表明后训练基本保留了运动识别、图像识别和密集语义分割性能。

Abstract: JEPA-based video encoders learn strong representations for video and image understanding through prediction in latent space. However, their standard pretraining predicts features within individual images or video clips, without explicitly exploiting paired camera views of the same object or moment. Consequently, the learned representations may not reliably support matching across different camera viewpoints. To solve this problem, we introduce multi-view predictive post-training to strengthen cross-view instance and temporal correspondence while retaining independent single-view inference. A shared encoder processes masked context views independently, and a training-time predictor jointly predicts latent tokens for masked context regions and a fully held-out view. We alternate cross-view prediction with single-view masked prediction on general image and video data to help retain the encoder’s pretrained capabilities. After only 9,000 post-training updates on a single GPU, MV-JEPA improves cross-view instance retrieval and coarse cross-camera moment retrieval on various datasets, with further gains in zero-shot action-phase retrieval and frozen-feature classification on AE2. Frozen-probe evaluations further indicate that post-training largely preserves motion recognition, image recognition, and dense semantic segmentation performance on the evaluated benchmarks. Together, these results demonstrate that multi-view predictive post-training can strengthen instance and coarse temporal correspondence in JEPA video encoders without modifying the encoder architecture or single-view inference interface.

[33] Identity World Model: Recognizing People in an Imagined Latent World cs.CVPDF

Dingqiang Ye, Kartik Narayan, Vishal M. Patel

TL;DR: 论文提出 IDWorld，一种身份世界模型，用于全身行人识别。它不在像素空间生成反事实图像，而是在潜空间中单次前向预测同一人在服装、视角、轮廓等条件变化下的表征，并自适应融合多种想象条件。方法在多个全身行人识别基准上取得领先结果，且潜空间想象相比像素级想象速度提升超过 11 倍。

Details

Motivation: 现有全身行人识别方法容易依赖服装、纹理、姿态、视角等瞬时线索，在真实世界变化下不够鲁棒。论文认为识别器不应只学习忽略这些线索，而应想象人物在条件变化后的身份表征。

Result: IDWorld 在 CCPG 和 CCGR-MINI 上达到 SOTA，在 MEVID 上优于对比方法，在 CASIA-B* 和 SUSTech1K 上泛化最好，并在 CCVID 上取得最佳 mAP；潜空间想象保持接近像素级想象的精度，速度超过 11 倍，达 5.9 ms/帧。

Insight: 创新点在于将世界模型思想引入身份识别，通过潜空间反事实想象生成共享规范条件下的身份特征，避免像素渲染开销，并按 probe-gallery 对自适应融合不同想象条件。客观来看，其可借鉴之处是用潜空间条件预测增强身份表征的鲁棒性，而非仅依赖数据增强或显式忽略干扰因素。

Abstract: Whole-body human recognition has advanced rapidly with large vision models. However, existing methods remain fragile under real-world variations, as they tie identity to transient cues such as clothing, texture, pose, or viewpoint. We argue that a robust recognizer should not merely learn to ignore these cues, but imagine how a person would be represented if they changed. To this end, we propose IDWorld, an identity world model: just as a latent world model predicts the features of a scene after an action, IDWorld predicts how a person would be represented under a changed condition. In a single forward pass, IDWorld imagines these counterfactual features under shared canonical conditions, such as the same outfit, a fixed view, or a bare silhouette, without rendering any pixels. Compared with explicit imagination in pixels, this latent imagination keeps nearly the same accuracy while being over 11x faster, at 5.9 ms per frame. Since each imagined condition has its own strengths, IDWorld then fuses them adaptively, weighting each condition for every probe-gallery pair. Extensive experiments show that IDWorld achieves state-of-the-art results on CCPG and CCGR-MINI, outperforms the compared methods on MEVID, and generalizes best to CASIA-B* and SUSTech1K, with the best mAP on CCVID. These results suggest identity world models offer a simple yet effective path to robust recognition. Code will be released.

[34] Recurrent Vision Language Models Solve the Shell Game cs.CVPDF

Dingqiang Ye, Jiacong Xu, Wen Zhang, Yiqun Mei, Vishal M. Patel

TL;DR: 论文研究视觉语言模型（VLM）在“猜球/贝壳游戏”中追踪被隐藏物体的能力瓶颈，提出 RecurrentVLM：让预训练 VLM 的每个注意力层在时间上递归，用本层输出计算已完成帧组的 key/value。模型仅用单个答案 token 就在 VET-Bench 达到 100%、MET-Bench 达到 99.6%，并在最多训练 30 次交换后于 50 次交换游戏上达到 99%。

Details

Motivation: VLM 直接回答隐藏物体追踪任务时接近随机，思考模型在长序列上失败，微调只能泛化到见过的长度。作者认为限制在于串行计算：每次杯子交换都需要与上一状态组合，而固定深度 VLM 的状态转移次数有限。

Result: RecurrentVLM 在 VET-Bench 上达到 100%，在 MET-Bench 上达到 99.6%；仅训练最多 30 次交换，就在 50 次交换游戏中达到 99%，表现出很强的长度泛化能力。

Insight: 核心创新是让预训练 VLM 的注意力层沿时间递归，用层自身输出为已完成帧组生成 key/value，从而以有限深度完成多次状态组合。摘要还揭示了长度泛化突然出现、无递归时每次交换约消耗一个注意力层、球状态在前四层形成、递归保留最新状态但牺牲早期细节等现象。

Abstract: Tracking a hidden object through a video is effortless for humans, yet it remains a bottleneck for vision language models (VLMs). In the shell game, where a ball is hidden under one of several identical cups that are then swapped, VLMs that answer directly stay near chance, thinking models fail on long games, and fine-tuning learns only the lengths it has seen. We argue that the limit is serial computation: each swap must be composed with the last result, and a VLM of fixed depth has only a fixed number of state transitions for it. To address this, we propose RecurrentVLM, which makes every attention layer of a pretrained VLM recur over time by computing the keys and values of finished frame groups from the layer’s own output. With a single answer token, RecurrentVLM reaches 100% on VET-Bench and 99.6% on MET-Bench, and 99% on 50-swap games after training on at most 30 swaps. We also observe several interesting phenomena: length generalization emerges suddenly at an aha moment; without recurrence, each swap costs an attention layer; the ball’s state forms in the first four attention layers; and recurrence keeps the latest state at the cost of details of earlier moments. These results suggest that recurrent VLMs are a potential solution for tracking the states of unobserved objects in video. Code will be released.

[35] Beyond Restoration: Content-Degradation Factor Unfolding for Real-World Concealed Object Segmentation cs.CV | cs.AIPDF

Rihan Zhang, Fengyang Xiao, Yuhan Chen, Sina Farsiu

TL;DR: 论文面向真实退化场景下的隐蔽目标分割（COS），指出关键不是恢复图像，而是从退化变化中区分可靠的内容证据。作者将Retinex扩展到内容-退化分解，提出因子展开网络FUN：用内容因子R作为分割的唯一直接语义证据，用退化因子L生成空间可靠性图进行校准，并通过统一能量模型交替优化形成由粗到细的网络，无需干净图像监督。方法将退化用于感知而非去除，在干净/退化条件和多种分割任务上验证了鲁棒性与泛化性。

Details

Motivation: 现有RGB空间分割容易纠缠内容与退化线索，基于复原的方法偏重图像恢复和任务交互，难以在弱前景的COS中识别可靠内容证据；因此需要显式分解内容证据与退化影响，并评估内容证据的可靠性。

Result: 摘要未给出具体数值或benchmark名称；在干净与退化条件及多种分割任务上的广泛实验显示方法具有鲁棒性能和泛化能力，代码将开源。

Insight: 创新在于将Retinex思想扩展为任务非对称的内容-退化因子分解：内容因子独占语义证据，退化因子转为空间可靠性校准；统一能量优化在无干净图像监督下实现由粗到细分割，强调“为感知分解退化”而非复原。

Abstract: Reliable visual perception under degradation requires distinguishing task-relevant content from degradation-induced variations, especially in concealed object segmentation (COS), where foreground cues are inherently weak. The central challenge is not merely improving visibility, but identifying reliable content evidence amid degradation-induced variations. RGB-space segmentation often entangles content and degradation cues, while restoration-based methods primarily focus on image recovery and task interaction. This motivates explicit factorization to distinguish content evidence from degradation effects and assess its reliability. Retinex provides a spatially aligned basis for this separation but remains illumination-centric. We therefore extend it to content-degradation formation with task-asymmetric factor roles. The Factor Unfolding Network (FUN) uses the content factor R as the sole source of direct semantic evidence for segmentation, while the degradation factor L captures degradation-related variations and generates a spatial reliability map to calibrate this evidence. A unified energy couples observation formation with reliability-weighted foreground-background separation, and its alternating updates yield a coarse-to-fine network without clean-image supervision. In this way, FUN factorizes degradation for perception rather than removing it. Extensive experiments across clean and degraded conditions and diverse segmentation tasks demonstrate robust performance and generalization. Code will be released.

[36] Keel: A Frontier Model Teaches a Vision-Language Model by Rewriting Its Weights cs.CV | cs.AIPDF

Jiajun Fan, Neil He, Bangji Yang, Qinglong Zheng, Ziwen Wang

TL;DR: 论文提出Keel，这是首个面向视觉语言模型（VLM）图像任务的智能体式权重编辑方法：让前沿多模态LLM充当编辑智能体agent，它只被告知“思考模式下反复循环”这一症状，并借助失败样本普查以及注意力等探针工具，在权重中定位病因，直接修改权重并测试；再由验证框架只保留“明显减少失败、且不降低未见过问题准确率”的编辑。修复无需训练，不改变模型规模与每token成本，也可作用于已被编辑过的权重。

Details

Motivation: 小型推理VLM（如Qwen3.5）在thinking模式下回答图像问题容易陷入自我重复的循环，直到耗尽token上限而丢失答案；现有补救要么只在解码端干预、模型本身未变，要么必须事先告知该改什么，而真正的病因其实写在权重里。

Result: 在Qwen3.5-0.8B和2B上，覆盖从视觉数学到GUI grounding的六个图像基准，该编辑在全部基准上消除了大部分循环并缩短回答，平均准确率显著高于thinking模式原模型；较大模型在练习题未涉及的GUI grounding上提升最大；与budget forcing结合后，在两个模型上均取得最佳平均准确率，较大模型上显著优于所有对比的免训练基线。

Insight: 创新点在于首个针对VLM的智能体式权重编辑：由前沿模型作为agent，仅凭症状自主完成“定位—编辑—验证”闭环，并用多编辑器与验证harness剔除损害held-out准确率的编辑，且不增参数与推理成本；同时揭示循环源自中间层注意力输出、模型在循环中不再参考图像。客观看，它把“训练修复”转化为“可解释探针＋权重编辑＋验证筛选”的新范式，颇具借鉴价值。

Abstract: Can a frontier model repair a vision-language model (VLM) by rewriting its weights? Small reasoning VLMs such as Qwen3.5 need such a repair: in thinking mode they fall into loops on many image questions, repeating themselves until the token limit, spending the whole budget and losing the answer. The usual remedies act on the decoding, leaving the looping model as it was, or must be told what to change. Yet the cause is written in the weights. Keel, the first agentic weight-editing method for VLMs on image tasks, finds and removes it there. Told only the symptom, an editor, a frontier multimodal LLM acting as an agent, locates the cause, edits the weights and tests them, with a failure census and instruments showing it the failing images and where the VLM attends; among several editors, the harness keeps only edits that clearly reduce the failure without lowering accuracy on questions no editor saw. The repair needs no training, keeps the model’s size and per-token cost, and applies to already-edited weights. The editors expose where the loops live: from the original weights, independent editors on both models trace them to the attention outputs of the middle layers, and the smaller VLM stops consulting the image inside a loop. On Qwen3.5-0.8B and Qwen3.5-2B, across six image benchmarks from visual mathematics to GUI grounding, the edit removes most loops on every benchmark and shortens answers, and the edited models are significantly more accurate than the thinking models on average; the larger one gains most on GUI grounding, a task absent from the practice questions. Because the repair lives in the weights, it combines with budget forcing where held-out questions favor it: on the best available weights, Keel has the best average accuracy on both models, significantly above every training-free baseline we compare on the larger one.

Abstract: We extend PoseTReID with online appearance-bank matching (Gallery) and a residual-conflict guard for registered SVM recognition. All methods use supplied ground-truth (GT) person boxes. We average all-box assessment IDF1/HOTA equally across videos. On six SportsMOT clips, StrongSORT leads standalone trackers (79.44/84.72%) and exceeds both Gallery variants. Over eleven videos, Gallery AIN (83.29/85.44%) and COPE (84.66/86.38%) exceed standalone trackers with complete results. This lead depends on two retrospectively added GTA development compilations and is absent on the original nine videos. Registered SVMs lead both cohorts among complete results, with AIN at 95.72/94.82% over eleven videos. They use every GT crop from approximately 20% of frames, including future appearances. On MOT20-03, registered AIN reached 96.80% IDF1 and assigned the correct enrolled name to 90.72% of assessment GT observations. Gallery and standalone trackers receive no per-video labelled enrollment. Both identity routes restore identities across internal title cards, but Gallery trails SORT on three videos (four in HOTA) and both SVMs trail SORT on MOT16-04/MOT20-03. Under six-clip persistent-only scoring, native Gallery gains 2.39 percentage points in mean IDF1 over a matched control, with lower correct-P coverage and 5.02 times as many encoder attempts. Pose gating is mixed; the guard’s metric effect is not isolated.

[38] ScaleDiag: Diagnosing the Limits of Test-Time Scaling in GUI Grounding cs.CVPDF

Chen Liu, Zhen Fang, Xin Yu, Dadong Wang, Ling Chen

TL;DR: 论文提出 ScaleDiag 诊断框架，系统分析 GUI grounding 中视觉缩放、指令缩放和采样缩放这三类测试时扩展策略的有效边界。作者在 5 个 benchmark 和最多 8 个视觉语言模型上独立控制目标尺度与上下文、目标特定信息与辅助描述、候选覆盖与选择，发现各策略都存在明显饱和区间，收益并非单调增加。

Details

Motivation: 现有 GUI grounding 方法常通过视觉缩放、指令增强或重复采样来提升性能，但尚不清楚这些策略分别解决哪些瓶颈、何时收益饱和甚至有害，因此需要受控诊断来厘清测试时扩展的局限。

Result: 在 5 个 benchmark 和最多 8 个 VLM 上的实验表明：视觉缩放受目标占据比例支配，目标约占裁剪视图 0.32% 时可挽救全屏错误，继续放大会有害；指令缩放主要依赖目标识别信息，外观与空间描述贡献超过 90% 的可达增益，而颜色、大小、形状等属性常中性或有害；重复采样存在 5.1–11.0 分的 oracle 上限空间，但正确坐标难以选择，几何共识几乎无提升。

Insight: 创新点在于构建可控诊断框架，将视觉输入、指令信息和预测采样解耦，揭示不同缩放策略各自狭窄的操作区间与瓶颈。客观来看，其价值不在于提出新的 SOTA 模型，而在于为 GUI grounding 的自适应缩放、信息选择和候选重排提供实证依据。

Abstract: GUI grounding maps a screenshot and a natural language instruction to the coordinates of the intended interface element. Existing approaches improve grounding through visual scaling, instruction scaling, and sampling scaling, yet it remains unclear which grounding bottlenecks these strategies address and where their gains saturate. To address this gap, we introduce ScaleDiag, a controlled diagnostic framework that isolates the effects of visual input, instruction information, and prediction sampling on GUI grounding by independently varying target scale and surrounding context, target-specific information and auxiliary descriptions, and candidate coverage and selection, respectively. We evaluate ScaleDiag across five benchmarks and up to eight vision-language models and find that each scaling strategy operates within a much narrower regime than commonly assumed. (1) Visual scaling is governed by target occupancy rather than a fixed crop ratio, with full-screen errors typically rescued once the target occupies only 0.32% of the cropped view, while further zooming eventually becomes harmful. (2) Instruction scaling is driven by information that identifies the target rather than description richness itself, as even ground-truth-derived attributes such as color, size, and shape are often neutral or harmful, while appearance and spatial descriptions account for over 90% of the achievable gain. (3) Repeated sampling exposes a different limitation, as correct coordinates are frequently generated but remain difficult to select, leaving 5.1–11.0 points of oracle headroom while geometric consensus provides almost no improvement. Together, these results show that test-time scaling is constrained by distinct operating regimes rather than benefiting monotonically from more visual focus, richer instructions, or additional samples. Our code is available at https://github.com/YenanLiu/ScaleDiag.

[39] VideoRNG: Relightable Neural Gaussians from Generative Video Models cs.CV | cs.GRPDF

Bowen Xue, Saeed Hadadan, Fabrice Rousselle, Ravi Ramamoorthi, Zahra Montazeri

TL;DR: VideoRNG提出两阶段方法，从文本或单张图像生成完整、可重光照的3D资产：先用微调的视频扩散模型生成已知姿态、相机和光照下的RGBA转台视频，再用前馈大重建模型将视频VAE隐变量直接映射为数百万带神经外观特征的各向异性高斯。该表示称为rtRNG，通过共享方向解码器和随机高斯光线追踪在新视角与新光照下生成辐射。方法还引入深度锚定外观训练以保留高频细节，并用前景token选择与成本感知分布式采样使百万级高斯重建可行。

Details

Motivation: 传统网格和紧凑PBR材质难以表达由细几何或复杂光传输形成的外观，而现有高斯泼溅生成器常把固定光照烘焙进外观，难以重光照。论文希望无需预设网格或解析材质模型，直接生成可重光照、可交互渲染的完整3D资产。

Result: 摘要未给出定量benchmark或SOTA比较，主要定性展示能生成毛发、羽毛、蜘蛛网等复杂资产，并支持交互渲染、重光照和场景集成。

Insight: 创新点在于利用视频扩散先验生成结构化转台序列，再前馈重建百万级可重光照神经高斯，并用共享方向解码器与随机高斯光线追踪解耦外观和光照。可借鉴的是depth-anchored appearance training在学几何时保留高频外观，以及前景token选择和成本感知分布式采样提升大规模高斯重建的实用性。

Abstract: Generating realistic 3D assets remains constrained by classical representations: surface meshes and compact physically based materials struggle to express objects whose appearance arises from fine geometry or complex light transport, while existing Gaussian-splatting generators typically bake fixed illumination into appearance. We introduce VideoRNG, a two-stage method that generates complete, relightable 3D assets from a text prompt or a single image without prescribing a mesh or analytic material model. A video generator fine-tuned from a pretrained video diffusion backbone first produces a structured RGBA turntable sequence under a known pose, camera, and illumination. A feed-forward large reconstruction model initialized from a pretrained video diffusion backbone then maps the video-VAE latent frames of selected turntable frames directly to millions of anisotropic Gaussians with learned neural appearance features. A shared directional decoder and stochastic Gaussian ray tracer turn this representation, which we call ray-traced Relightable Neural Gaussians (rtRNG), into radiance under novel viewpoints and illumination. To preserve high-frequency appearance while learning geometry, we introduce depth-anchored appearance training, and we make million-Gaussian reconstruction practical through foreground token selection and cost-aware distributed sampling. VideoRNG generates complex assets such as fur, feathers, and spider webs that can be interactively rendered, relit, and integrated into scenes.

Abstract: Reconstructing an editable 3D scene from video requires matching visible structure while plausibly completing unseen surfaces. Agents can write scene-construction programs, but direct reconstruction attempts often produce errors in layout, scale, and lighting. [41] Fold the Loop: Decoding-Time KV Memory for Looped Vision-Language Models cs.CVPDF

Kun Zhou, Wenmian Yang, Zhensheng Wang, Yanzeng Li, Weijia Jia

TL;DR: 论文针对循环视觉语言模型在解码时KV缓存随循环深度增长、导致内存受限的问题，提出FoldCache：在精确预填充后，沿循环轴压缩视觉KV，将同一模块内多个循环步骤分组并共享每组最终的视觉KV，同时保持问题与答案KV逐步独立。该方法可控制分组粒度，并能与令牌驱逐和量化结合。在LoopVL上约将前缀KV内存减半，且精度匹配全缓存。

Details

Motivation: 循环VLM通过重复应用权重绑定模块增强视觉推理，但每个循环步骤都产生独立KV状态，使缓存保存同一图像的多份副本，限制固定内存预算下的图像尺寸和批大小。

Result: 在LoopVL的五个开放式基准上，FoldCache约将前缀KV内存减半并匹配全缓存准确率；相同GPU内存预算下批大小可达全缓存的1.7倍，更激进的FoldCache-Lite可达2.6倍。

Insight: 创新点在于发现预填充后同一模块内较早循环步骤可复用最终视觉KV，并提出按循环步骤分组共享视觉KV、保持问答KV独立且不跨模块共享的训练无关解码期压缩方法；其可调节粒度并与令牌驱逐、量化兼容，具有较强的实用性和可扩展性。

Abstract: Looped vision-language models (VLMs) deepen visual reasoning by repeatedly applying weight-tied modules. However, their decoding memory grows with recurrent depth and limits the image size and batch size that fit in a fixed memory budget. Each recurrent step produces its own key-value (KV) states, so the cache holds several copies of the same image. We find that, after an exact prefill, earlier steps still need access to visual information but can reuse the final visual KV within the same module. We propose FoldCache, which compresses visual KV along the recurrent axis while preserving the full prefill computation. FoldCache partitions recurrent steps within each module into groups and retains the final visual KV of each group for its members to share during decoding. Sharing stays within module boundaries, while question and answer KV remain separate for each step. The grouping granularity controls the degree of compression, and folding can be combined with token eviction and quantization to further reduce memory use. On LoopVL, FoldCache approximately halves prefix KV memory while matching full-cache accuracy on five open-ended benchmarks. These memory savings support batch sizes up to 1.7 times those of a full cache under the same GPU memory budget, and up to 2.6 times with the more aggressive FoldCache-Lite.

[42] A Unified Framework for Image Manipulation Localization Under Multi-Type Weak Supervision cs.CVPDF

Songlin Li, Hui Ma, Zhiqing Guo, Dan Ma, Gaobo Yang
