Details

Motivation: 现有弱监督图像篡改定位方法通常依赖针对特定标注的训练策略，难以在单一模型中联合利用多种弱标注，限制了可用训练数据规模和性能提升；同时 SAM 对篡改痕迹不敏感，仅靠其质量分难以可靠选择最佳候选掩码。

Result: 实验表明，UniIML 相比当前最先进的图像篡改定位方法取得了更优的平均性能；摘要未给出具体 benchmark 和定量指标。

Insight: 创新点在于将多种弱标注统一表示并用于引导 SAM 候选生成，提出 OSC 通过弱标注约束、SAM 质量分和跨视图稳定性进行鲁棒候选选择，并基于选择稳定性、候选一致性和预测确定性进行可靠性加权蒸馏，从而实现多类型弱监督下的统一训练。

Abstract: Existing weakly supervised image manipulation localization (IML) methods typically rely on annotation-specific training strategies, hindering the joint use of diverse annotations within a single model. This limits the scale of usable training data and constrains further progress. We propose UniIML, a teacher-student framework that maps diverse weak annotations into a unified representation to guide the Segment Anything Model (SAM) in generating multiple candidate masks. However, SAM is designed for general-purpose segmentation and has limited sensitivity to manipulation traces; its predicted quality scores alone may therefore fail to reliably identify the candidate that best delineates the manipulated region. We introduce Ordinal Safe Consensus (OSC), which first filters candidates using trusted weak-annotation constraints encoded in the unified representation. It then ranks the remaining candidates separately by consistency with weak annotations, SAM-predicted quality scores, and cross-view stability, aggregating these rankings for an initial selection. To assess how changes in the evaluation criteria affect candidate selection, OSC repeats ranking and selection while omitting one criterion at a time. The most frequently selected candidate is adopted as the final mask, and its relative selection frequency quantifies selection stability. We construct soft pseudo-labels from the selected candidate’s predicted probabilities and estimate pixel-level supervision weights based on selection stability, pixel-level agreement among candidates, and predictive certainty. The student receives direct supervision in regions defined by weak annotations and learns from soft pseudo-labels elsewhere through reliability-weighted distillation, enabling unified training across weak annotation types. Experiments show that UniIML achieves superior average performance compared with state-of-the-art IML methods.

[43] Zero to Four Views: Unified Panorama Generation and Preference RL for Unobserved Regions cs.CVPDF

Rui Hong, Xugang Ye, Jana Košecká, Kai Fan

TL;DR: 论文提出了一个统一的等距柱状全景生成模型，输入为文本描述和 K∈{0,…,4} 张由四相机装置在同一视点拍摄的透视视图，输出完整全景图。方法分两阶段微调预训练的文本到图像 rectified-flow Transformer：第一阶段将每个条件视图与其相机槽位 ID 一起拼接到 token 序列，并在训练中变化视图数量，使一套权重适配所有输入设置；第二阶段提出 UR-GRPO 强化学习，针对未被任何条件视图覆盖的区域，用视觉语言模型（VLM）的成对偏好作为奖励来优化生成质量。此外还提供可选变体，把每个视图的相机方向写入位置编码，让用户指定各视图在输出中的方位。

Details

Motivation: 现有全景生成器大多只接受纯文本或单张输入图像，难以灵活支持零到多张透视视图作为条件；同时，扩散模型的监督训练只在真值的含噪潜变量上计算损失，从未对模型实际生成并解码出的全景进行评估，导致未被条件视图覆盖区域的质量较差。

Result: 在单张条件视图设置下，在 Laval Indoor 上超过了用相同数据训练、相同代码评估的此前最优方法 360Anything；在 SUN360 室内子集上结果互有胜负。第二阶段的 UR-GRPO 对每一种条件视图数量都带来了提升。

Insight: 创新点在于：（1）用相机槽位 ID 加可变视图数的训练方式，将 0～4 视图条件统一到单一模型；（2）UR-GRPO 以 VLM 在未覆盖区域裁剪块上的成对偏好作为奖励，直接优化模型生成并解码出的全景，弥补了监督训练与推理之间的差距；（3）将相机方向编码进位置编码，实现输出布局的可控性。

Abstract: Panoramas can serve as shared scene backgrounds for multi-shot video generation, but most existing panorama generators take text alone or a single input image. We present a single model that generates a full equirectangular panorama from a text caption and K in {0,…,4} perspective conditioning views taken by a four-camera rig at one viewpoint. We fine-tune a pretrained text-to-image rectified-flow transformer in two stages. In the first stage, each conditioning view is appended to the token sequence together with the ID of its camera slot, and the number of views varies during training, so one set of weights serves every input setting. In the second stage, we improve the region not covered by any conditioning view. Supervised training computes its loss on noisy latents of the ground truth and never scores a panorama the model has generated and decoded, so we propose Uncovered-Region GRPO (UR-GRPO), a reinforcement-learning recipe that rewards the candidates a vision-language model prefers in pairwise comparisons on crops around this region. Because the slot ID alone does not tell the model which way the rig points, we also offer an optional variant that writes each view’s camera direction into its position encoding, which lets the user decide where each view appears in the output. With one conditioning view, our model outperforms the previous state of the art, 360Anything, trained on the same data and evaluated with the same code, on Laval Indoor and gives mixed results on an indoor subset of SUN360; the second stage improves the results for every number of conditioning views.

Yuxuan Li, Derrick Goh Xin Deik, Zheng-Peng Duan, Qianru Sun, Weijie Li

Abstract: Continual SAR interpretation requires multimodal large language models to learn incoming sources while retaining earlier task abilities. This is difficult when acquisition and geographic shifts alter visual evidence, regionally concentrated categories bias answers, and historical images are scarce. We address this problem with Historical On-Policy Distillation (HOPD), which reuses source-acquisition LoRA states as persistent teachers for one evolving student. Archiving each state before consolidation preserves a stable source reference. Abstract: When reconstructing high-quality 3D assets from images, existing approaches often excel at one objective while compromising another, and frequently overlook the intrinsic quality of the reconstructed surfaces. We present MODL, a framework that jointly optimizes geometry and view-dependent appearance directly on a watertight triangle mesh. Abstract: Multimodal large language models have greatly improved input understanding in universal multimodal embedding models. However, this understanding does not ensure that their embeddings preserve the evidence needed to distinguish relevant items from confusable alternatives. To preserve essential discriminative evidence in embeddings, this paper proposes DiLE, a discrimination-aware latent-space learning framework that combines task guidance with on-policy retrieval feedback. Specifically, a few learnable queries are used to encode task-relevant information in a compact latent space. TL;DR: 论文提出Raptor，一个面向视觉语言模型的免训练树状推测解码框架。它利用拒绝反馈提取互补信号，联合调节候选覆盖、树深度、分支宽度和起草预算，以降低长视觉上下文下的推测计算冗余，并在两个视觉语言模型和七个多模态基准上实现最高2.39倍的平均加速。

Abstract: Tree-structured speculative decoding accelerates vision-language models (VLMs) by verifying multiple candidate continuations in parallel. However, long visual contexts increase the cost of speculative computation, making candidate coverage and redundant expansion important considerations beyond tree topology adaptation. We propose Raptor, a training-free framework that uses rejection feedback to jointly regulate candidate coverage and drafting effort. Our key insight is that acceptance length alone cannot distinguish insufficient candidate coverage from excessive deep expansion, and these conditions require different allocation decisions. Raptor therefore extracts complementary rejection-aware signals to characterize speculative progress, frontier coverage, and redundant expansion. These signals jointly guide bounded, momentum-smoothed updates of tree depth, branching width, and drafting budget, together with execution control that limits unnecessary drafting and progressively restores expansion when appropriate. Extensive experiments with two VLMs across seven multimodal benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of Raptor, achieving up to 2.39x average speedup over autoregressive decoding.

We propose Logo-guided Semantic Attack (LoSA), a score-based black-box framework that optimizes a logo’s interior appearance and placement while preserving its silhouette. Details

Motivation: 现有视频生成与虚拟人合成系统可用于生成视觉解释，但已有基准很少评估虚拟人是否准确指向当前讲解的视觉内容。论文旨在填补这一空白，系统评测问题驱动虚拟人视频解释中的事实正确性、视听一致性和指向准确性。

Result: 实验覆盖六个智能体，发现事实精度和视觉质量并不能保证准确的虚拟人-内容指向。26名参与者的用户研究比较四个智能体，Avatar-O Agent 在视觉引导、感知指向准确性和解释有效性上获得最高平均评分。

Insight: 论文的创新点在于引入 Avatar-Content Pointing 作为独立评测维度，并构建面向金融与教育场景的虚拟人视频解释基准。客观来看，其价值在于将“指向对齐”从整体视频质量中解耦评估，并通过显式交互规划的模块化智能体提供了可复现的参考实现。

Abstract: Advances in video generation and avatar synthesis enable new ways to answer user questions through visual explanations. This task requires a system to formulate a correct answer, translate relevant information into visual material, and deliver the explanation through an avatar’s speech and actions. Existing benchmarks rarely assess whether avatars point to the visual content being discussed. We introduce VisualTalk-Bench for question-driven avatar-led video explanations in finance and Chinese and English education. It includes 800 curated reference videos and 100 independent evaluation requests, assessing Content Factuality, Speech-Visual Consistency, Visual Quality, Motion Smoothness, and Avatar-Content Pointing. Yet end-to-end video generators often struggle to align gestures with specific visual targets. To explore this challenge, we develop Avatar-O Agent, a modular reference implementation with explicit interaction planning. Experiments across six agents show that factual precision and visual quality do not guarantee accurate pointing. A 26-participant study comparing four agents finds that Avatar-O Agent receives the highest average ratings for visual guidance, perceived pointing accuracy, and explanation effectiveness. We will release the data and code.

[51] Beyond Resemblance: Image Fusion Reimagined cs.CVPDF

Hao Zhang, Yumin Huang, Linfeng Tang, Junjun Jiang, Jiayi Ma

TL;DR: 论文提出 ApexFuse 生成式图像融合范式，不再要求融合图像同时相似多个源图像，而是学习高质量场景表示的目标分布。方法通过人类-LMM 在环流程，利用大型多模态生成模型和迭代人类反馈构建可靠视觉参考，并用目标分布引导将预训练 FLUX 的生成先验迁移到图像融合。实验表明其在视觉质量、语义分割和目标检测上优于源相似方法，并可跨任务泛化。

Details

Motivation: 图像融合通常缺少 ground-truth 融合图像，现有深度方法通过要求融合结果同时相似多个源图像来间接定义融合目标。但不同模态的成像特性会形成竞争约束，可能限制互补信息的有效整合，因此需要突破源相似性约束。

Result: 实验显示 ApexFuse 能协调整合显著热目标、自然颜色和细纹理，在视觉质量以及语义分割、目标检测性能上优于以往源相似方法；摘要未给出具体数值和 benchmark 名称。方法还可从红外-可见融合泛化到多曝光、多聚焦和可见-深度融合，且无需额外微调。

Insight: 论文宣称的创新是提出超越源相似的生成式融合范式，通过 human-LMM-in-the-loop 将抽象融合目标转化为可靠视觉参考以定义目标分布，并利用预训练 FLUX 的生成先验进行目标分布引导适配。客观来看，其创新点在于重新定义融合监督目标、引入多模态生成模型与人类反馈构建目标分布，并展示了跨融合任务的零微调泛化能力。

Abstract: Image fusion often lacks ground-truth fused images, forcing existing deep methods to indirectly define fusion objectives by requiring the fused image to simultaneously resemble multiple source images. However, the distinct imaging characteristics of different modalities can impose competing constraints on the fused result, potentially limiting the effective integration of their complementary information. To address this long-standing challenge facing the image fusion community, we propose ApexFuse, a novel generative image fusion paradigm that moves beyond source-resemblance constraints toward learning a general distribution of high-quality scene representations. Specifically, we develop a human-LMM-in-the-loop pipeline that leverages large multimodal generative models and iterative human feedback to translate abstract fusion objectives into reliable visual references, thereby defining the target distribution of ideal fusion outcomes. Through target-distribution-guided adaptation, we transfer the rich generative priors of pretrained FLUX to image fusion, enabling high-quality scene reconstruction conditioned on multimodal observations. Extensive experiments demonstrate that ApexFuse harmoniously integrates salient thermal targets, natural colors, and fine textures, achieving superior visual quality as well as semantic segmentation and object detection performance over previous source-resemblance methods. Moreover, ApexFuse exhibits remarkable cross-task generalization, extending from infrared-visible fusion to multi-exposure fusion, multi-focus fusion, and visible-depth fusion without additional fine-tuning.

[52] CANDOR: Contrastive Self-Denoising Rewards for Label-Free Text-to-Image Post-Training cs.CVPDF

Junhyeok Lee, Byung-Hoon Kim, Kyu Sung Choi

TL;DR: 论文提出一种无需标注偏好的文生图强化学习后训练奖励 CANDOR，用同一噪声探针下无条件与条件去噪误差的对数比值衡量提示词对生成样本去噪的帮助。该方法避免依赖外部奖励模型，并克服现有自置信度内在奖励对图像复杂度敏感、难以反映提示词保真度的问题。CANDOR 可作为 Flow-GRPO 的唯一奖励，提升文本渲染、美学和人类偏好表现，也可与外部奖励互补。

Details

Motivation: 现有文生图 RL 后训练依赖需要偏好标注或辅助评估器的外部奖励模型，容易 reward hacking；而近期基于生成器自身去噪准确率的内在奖励并不是提示词保真度的良好代理，同提示词组内排序接近随机。

Result: 在组合生成和文本渲染的同提示词样本对排序中，CANDOR 分别有 60% 和 63% 的成对准确率；作为 Flow-matching 模型 Flow-GRPO 的唯一奖励，其在文本渲染、美学和人类偏好分数上优于基座模型及先前绝对自置信度奖励，并在公开 Flow-GRPO 外部奖励适配器上进一步提升 PickScore 和 ImageReward。

Insight: 创新点在于用共享噪声探针下“无条件/条件去噪误差”的对数比值构建对比式内在奖励，抵消与提示词无关的图像难度因素，从而无需外部偏好或评估器即可衡量提示词对去噪的实际贡献；该奖励还可与已有外部奖励互补。

Abstract: Post-training text-to-image generators with reinforcement learning usually relies on external reward models that need annotated preferences or auxiliary evaluators and are prone to reward hacking. We show that the generator’s own denoising accuracy, used by a recent intrinsic reward, is a poor proxy for prompt fidelity. Within a same-prompt group, this absolute self-confidence is dominated by image complexity and ranks correct samples above incorrect ones near chance. We introduce CANDOR (Contrastive Alignment from Null-referenced Denoising lOg-Ratio), the log-ratio of unconditional to conditional denoising errors under shared noise probes, which measures how much the prompt helps the model denoise its own sample. It cancels prompt-independent difficulty that scales both errors and ranks the better of two same-prompt samples higher in 60% and 63% of pairs on compositional generation and text rendering. As the only Flow-GRPO reward for a flow-matching model, CANDOR improves text rendering, aesthetic and human-preference scores over the base model and a prior absolute self-confidence reward, with text-rendering and preference gains that stronger classifier-free guidance does not reproduce. On public Flow-GRPO adapters trained on external rewards, CANDOR further raises PickScore and ImageReward, so it can complement those rewards. Code is available at https://github.com/jhlee0619/candor.

Abstract: We study how to obtain a task-specific Vision-Language Model (VLM) for language-guided path generation between start and end points on remote sensing images. We explore this question from three aspects. First, we implement three widely used architectural approaches and compare their performance. Second, we design a scalable method for constructing pre-training data and build an effective pre-training dataset with a degree of diversity. Methods trained on this dataset achieve promising zero-shot performance. Third, we construct three benchmarks that support stratified evaluation and design three metrics for path generation. We investigate this problem through extensive experiments. The results show that the zero-shot performance of methods trained on the constructed pre-training dataset can exceed that of some advanced commercial VLMs. Code and Dataset can be available at https://github.com/jzdang/RemoteVLPG

[54] ActMem: From Latent Memory Reasoning to Grounded Robotic Action cs.CV | cs.LGPDF

Linfeng Dong, Jianwei Niu, Jiuyi Zhao, Ruibing Feng, Xinghao Wu

TL;DR: 论文提出 ActMem，一个面向长时程机器人操作的上下文记忆框架。它在单一视觉-语言主干中解耦记忆推理与感知 grounding：先基于带时间戳的交互历史推断当前子任务，并将推理形成的隐状态作为 Latent Memory Chain-of-Thought 来条件化流匹配动作专家，同时通过 Perception Attention 让动作专家直接关注当前视觉输入。论文还提出高效 Memory CoT 推理以在长历史下加速决策。

Details

Motivation: 现有记忆增强 VLA 要么只检索过去观测而不推理其含义，要么在共享上下文中推理完整历史，导致当前感知被挤占。论文受认知科学启发，希望区分“记忆重建过去经验以决定做什么”和“感知引导如何行动”，从而解耦记忆推理与感知 grounding。

Result: 在 RoboMemArena 上达到 71.1% 任务成功率和 80.8% 完成成功率，分别超过最强基线 7.5 和 8.7 个百分点；在 MIKASA-Robo 上平均成功率 73.8%，为对比方法中最佳；真实世界外生、内生和无痕记忆任务平均成功率 68.8%，超过最强记忆增强 VLA 10.0 个百分点。

Insight: 创新点在于用单一视觉-语言主干解耦记忆推理与感知 grounding，并将记忆推理产生的隐状态组织为 Latent Memory CoT 来条件化流匹配动作专家；Perception Attention 使动作专家直接关注当前视图，Efficient Memory CoT Inference 在不改变输出的前提下复用连续决策计算以加速长历史推理。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have shown promising results in real-world long-horizon manipulation, where memory plays a crucial role. However, existing memory-augmented VLAs either retrieve past observations without reasoning about what they imply, or reason over the full history in a shared context where it crowds out current perception. Cognitive science treats them as distinct systems: memory reconstructs past experience to decide what to do, while perception guides how to act. We therefore propose ActMem, an in-context memory framework that decouples memory reasoning from perceptual grounding within a single vision-language backbone. Built on a vision-language model with native video understanding, ActMem infers the current subtask from a timestamped interaction history and keeps the hidden states formed during this inference as a Latent Memory Chain-of-Thought (Memory CoT), which conditions a flow-matching action expert. Perception Attention further lets the expert attend directly to the current view. To keep inference fast under long histories, Efficient Memory CoT Inference reuses computation across consecutive decisions without changing the output. ActMem achieves 71.1% task success and 80.8% completion success on RoboMemArena, surpassing the strongest baseline by 7.5 and 8.7 percentage points, and 73.8% average success on MIKASA-Robo, the best among all compared methods. In the real world, it reaches 68.8% average success on exogenous, endogenous, and traceless memory tasks, 10.0 percentage points above the strongest memory-augmented VLA. Code and checkpoints will be released at https://github.com/LigphiDonk/ActMem and https://huggingface.co/donk111/ActMem.

[55] A Wolf in Sheep’s Pixels: Visual Camouflage Attacks via HDR Gain Maps cs.CV | cs.AIPDF

Qi Shen, Yujia Gong, Yue Zhao, Tong Liu

TL;DR: 论文提出 DRJanus，一种利用 HDR 增益图与 SDR 基图处理差异的视觉伪装攻击：在单个 JPEG 中同时嵌入机器读取的 SDR 图像和人类 HDR 查看器看到的另一幅图像，从而绕过自动审核并影响下游智能体。该方法无需查询或优化目标模型，能精确保留 SDR 基图，并在 HDR 端重建目标图像。

Details

Motivation: 现有视觉安全审核通常只检查图像的标准动态范围基图，而 HDR 显示管线会借助增益图重建最终外观，二者存在视觉信任缺口。论文旨在解决“机器看到的内容”和“用户实际看到的内容”不一致所导致的安全风险。

Result: 在 400 张图像上，DRJanus 重建 HDR 目标的平均 PSNR 达 43.03 dB，且精确保留 SDR 基图。在 100 张色情内容图像基准上，它绕过全部 6 个商业审核服务和 9 个视觉语言模型；在 5 个智能体、每个威胁设置 120 个任务案例中，审核规避成功率为 31.67% 至 100%，任务操纵成功率为 95% 至 100%。

Insight: 创新点在于利用 HDR gain map 的重建机制实现单文件双视图伪装，且无需查询或优化目标模型；同时揭示了显示管线差异带来的安全盲区，并探索跨视图一致性检查作为缓解手段。客观来看，该工作将攻击面从传统像素扰动扩展到图像显示与重建流程的不一致性，具有较强的通用性和安全警示价值。

Abstract: An image can pass automated safety checks while showing harmful content to its human viewer. We introduce DRJanus, a visual camouflage attack that exploits the gap between standard dynamic range (SDR) processing and high dynamic range (HDR) display. Automated systems often read only an image’s SDR base, whereas HDR viewers reconstruct its appearance using an auxiliary gain map. DRJanus inverts this reconstruction to embed two independently chosen color images in a single JPEG, without querying or optimizing against the target model. It preserves the chosen SDR base exactly and reconstructs HDR targets at an average PSNR of 43.03 dB across 400 images. On a benchmark of 100 sexual-content images, DRJanus evades detection by all six tested commercial moderation services and nine vision-language models. Across five agents, each evaluated on 120 task cases per threat setting, attack success ranges from 31.67% to 100% for moderation evasion and from 95% to 100% for task manipulation. We also explore cross-view consistency checks as a mitigation. These findings expose a gap in visual trust: securing what a machine reads does not secure what a user sees. A demo is available at https://qshen0629.github.io/DRJanus/

TL;DR: 论文提出TuringPAI，一套面向端侧物理AI（Physical AI）的模型家族，包括视觉语言模型TuringPAI-VL、自动驾驶模型TuringPAI-Drive和机器人操作模型TuringPAI-Embodied。核心是以0.65B的TuringPAI-VL为共享视觉语言基座，借助HybridViT-MLQ结构与多尺度Latent Query接口统一建模空间、跨视角与时序信息，并将其初始化下游VLA模型。论文验证了该基座在车载SoC上可实现实时轨迹生成，并在机器人操作任务上取得高成功率。

Insight: 创新在于HybridViT-MLQ将卷积局部聚合与Transformer全局建模结合，并用多尺度Latent Query在同一视觉Transformer内融合空间特征与观测历史，形成面向语言模型的紧凑结构化接口；训练上融合通用视觉语言数据与几何、跨视角、时序监督；同时以单一轻量视觉语言基座共享初始化驾驶与操作两类VLA模型，兼顾端侧效率与多任务泛化。

Abstract: Physical AI requires models that understand spatial relationships and dynamic environments and translate observations into actions under limited onboard compute. We present TuringPAI, a family of models for on-device Physical AI comprising TuringPAI-VL for shared vision-language understanding, TuringPAI-Drive for autonomous driving, and TuringPAI-Embodied for robotic manipulation. TuringPAI-VL is a 0.65B model built on HybridViT-MLQ, which combines convolutional local aggregation with Transformer-based global modeling. Multi-scale Latent Queries integrate spatial features and observation history within the same visual Transformer, providing a compact, structured interface to the language model. Training combines general vision-language data with geometric, cross-view, and temporal supervision. TuringPAI-VL supports general multimodal understanding and improves quantitative geometric perception over Qwen2-VL-2B, while providing a shared initialization for the two downstream VLA models. Using XPeng Native Runtime (XNRT) on a single NVIDIA Orin-X SoC, TuringPAI-Drive generates trajectories at 12,Hz in on-vehicle operation. For robotic manipulation, TuringPAI-Embodied achieves a 96.8% average success rate across the four LIBERO suites. These results demonstrate a shared vision-language foundation for driving and robotic manipulation under practical on-device constraints.

[57] Persona Lenses: What Makes a Vision-Language Model See Through Its Persona’s Eyes? cs.CV | cs.AI | cs.HCPDF

Yoonho Shin

TL;DR: 论文研究视觉语言模型在网页注视点模拟中能否体现提示中的人格属性，提出文本、像素、权重三种“人格透镜”将人格信息注入模型与页面之间，并在Qwen3-VL 4B/32B和Qwen3.5-9B、生成与真实页面上比较不同透镜与模型规模对人格区分度和鲁棒性的影响。结论是不同透镜在人格区分、无关属性鲁棒性和成本上各有取舍，选择取决于成本与模型规模。

Details

Motivation: 现有VLM可模拟用户网页注视，但仅靠prompt中的人格描述只能部分影响模拟注视；论文希望理解并增强人格对注视路径的作用，同时区分真正相关属性与不应改变注视的无关profile变化。

Result: 在Qwen3-VL 4B/32B和Qwen3.5-9B及生成/真实页面上比较：文本透镜人格区分强但对不应改变注视的profile变化不鲁棒；像素透镜区分较弱但不受无关属性影响；4B/9B权重透镜能对训练外兴趣区别人格且新页面无需额外步骤；32B仅用基础prompt即可区别人格。

Insight: 创新在于提出“人格透镜”框架，将人格作为模型与页面之间的可插拔中介，并系统比较文本/像素/权重三种注入方式及模型规模影响；客观看，贡献更多是分析性框架与实证发现，而非新模型结构。

Abstract: Vision-language models have been used to simulate where a user looks on a web page. However, a persona in the prompt reaches the simulated gaze only partly. We measure this reach as the difference in one model’s gaze between two MatrAIx personas that differ in a single attribute. We introduce persona lenses, which place the persona between the model and the page: a text lens (a two-step prompt in which the model first writes what the persona would look at), a pixel lens (marks on the region matching the persona’s interests) and a weight lens (a dwell reward gated to keep the scanpath natural). Using Qwen3-VL (4B and 32B) and Qwen3.5-9B on generated and live pages, we compare the lenses with one another and with scale, the 32B model with no lens. It has been confirmed that the text lens separates the personas strongly but is not robust to profile changes that should not move gaze. The pixel lens separates the personas less but is unaffected by attributes unrelated to gaze. At 4B and 9B, the weight lens separates the personas on interests held out from training, with no extra step on a new page. The 32B model separates the personas with the base prompt alone. Therefore, the choice of a lens depends on cost and model size.

[58] Mesh-Free Keypoint Learning for Category-Level 6D Object Pose Estimation cs.CV | cs.ROPDF

Junghoon Seo, Hakjin Lee, Jaehoon Sim

TL;DR: 论文提出MORSEPose，用于类别级6D物体姿态估计中的无网格关键点学习。方法利用标准姿态和度量尺寸标注，将观测3D点映射到归一化物体坐标，并通过容差膨胀的姿态-尺寸包络筛选关键点支撑集，再与多级RGB-D特征、自适应关键点和可学习类别上下文结合。实验表明该监督在REAL275和HouseCat6D上接近基于表面的监督，并保持稳定性和有竞争力的姿态精度。

Details

Motivation: 现有类别级6D姿态估计常借助物体感知重建和自适应关键点保留可见表面几何，但重建目标依赖配准实例表面或表面距离选择，较为繁琐。论文希望直接从标准姿态与尺寸标注构造一致的监督，避免显式网格或表面选择。

Result: 在REAL275和HouseCat6D上，所构造包络在点级和任务级均接近表面监督效果，对边界扰动和视角变化较稳定，并取得有竞争力的类别级姿态估计精度。

Insight: 创新点在于用容差膨胀的姿态-尺寸包络替代传统表面距离选择器来生成关键点支撑集，该构造跨相机帧和度量尺度一致，并可保守包含常规表面选择器的支撑。客观上看，将免网格的标注驱动监督与多级RGB-D特征、自适应关键点和类别上下文结合，是其主要可借鉴之处。

Abstract: Category-level 6D pose estimation relates partial RGB-D observations of unseen instances to a canonical frame shared across a category. Object-aware reconstruction helps adaptive keypoints retain the geometry of the visible surface. Common reconstruction targets select observed points by their distance to a registered instance surface. We present MORSEPose, which derives this support directly from standard pose and metric-size annotations. We map observed 3D points to normalized object coordinates and retain those inside a tolerancedilated pose-and-size envelope. This construction is consistent across camera frames and metric scales. When the annotated extent bounds the object surface, the envelope conservatively contains the support of a conventional surface-distance selector. We integrate this target with multi-level RGB-D features, adaptive keypoints, and learnable category context. Experiments on REAL275 and HouseCat6D show that the envelope closely matches surface-based supervision at both point and task levels, remains stable under boundary perturbations and viewpoint changes, and supports competitive category-level pose accuracy across benchmarks.

[59] Contrast Where It Matters: Visual Evidence-Guided Decoding for Mitigating Hallucinations in LVLMs cs.CVPDF

Yiheng Li, Wenhao Wang, Xingyi Li, Mingda Jia, Zichang Tan

TL;DR: 论文提出免训练的视觉证据引导解码框架 VEGD，用于缓解大型视觉语言模型（LVLMs）的幻觉问题。该方法先定位与查询相关的视觉证据，并在对比解码中引导主分支和辅助分支关注同一证据位置，同时通过自适应分布融合按分布差异组合或对比预测。

Details

Motivation: 现有免训练对比解码主要通过改变辅助输入或计算来暴露语言先验，但未确保解码分支使用与查询相关的视觉证据，导致语言先验压过视觉证据或预测依赖无关、误导性图像区域。

Result: 摘要未报告具体数值，但称在多种 LVLMs 和多个 benchmark 上实验表明 VEGD 能一致降低幻觉。

Insight: 创新在于将查询相关视觉证据的定位贯穿对比解码：聚合强视觉响应且空间连贯的定位头来定位证据，在辅助图像中削弱证据，并引导两分支视觉载体头锚定同一位置；再按分布差异自适应融合预测。客观看，可借鉴点是把证据定位作为对比解码的约束，减少无关区域干扰。

Abstract: Despite strong multimodal capabilities, large vision-language models (LVLMs) often hallucinate when linguistic priors outweigh visual evidence or when predictions are grounded in irrelevant or misleading image regions. Training-free contrastive decoding mitigates hallucinations by contrasting the original prediction with an auxiliary one that exposes linguistic priors. Yet existing methods focus on altering the auxiliary input or computation and do not ensure that either branch uses query-relevant visual evidence during decoding. We propose Visual Evidence-Guided Decoding (VEGD), a training-free framework that identifies query-relevant visual evidence and guides its use throughout contrastive decoding. VEGD locates this evidence by aggregating localization heads with strong visual responses and coherent spatial structure, then selectively weakens it in the auxiliary image. During generation, VEGD directs visual-carrier heads in both branches toward the same evidence location, strengthening access to intact evidence in the main branch while anchoring the auxiliary branch to its weakened counterpart. An adaptive distribution fusion strategy then combines or contrasts their predictions according to distribution divergence. Experiments across diverse LVLMs and benchmarks demonstrate that VEGD consistently reduces hallucinations.

[60] Support-Guided Triadic Decoding for Hallucination Mitigation in Multimodal Dialogue with LVLMs cs.CVPDF

Yiheng Li, Wenhao Wang, Xingyi Li, Zhilin Tang, Zichang Tan

TL;DR: 论文提出一种无需训练的支持引导三元解码框架SGTD，用于缓解多模态多轮对话中LVLM因历史幻觉响应传播而导致的幻觉问题。该方法先估计历史回答的视觉支持度以识别低支持内容，再通过注意力干预削弱其影响，并在解码时融合支持精炼预测、全历史预测和无答案视觉证据，最后用支持自适应合理性约束过滤不合理token。

Details

Motivation: 多模态多轮对话中，对话历史既可能提供有用上下文，也可能因幻觉响应将错误传播到后续轮次。现有免训练方法通常全局调整历史影响，难以在抑制误导信息的同时保留有用上下文。

Result: 在MMHalSnowball和VisDiaHalBench上，针对多种代表性LVLM，SGTD在存在幻觉历史时持续取得最高准确率，同时保留事实性上下文带来的收益。

Insight: 创新点在于无需训练地选择性控制不可靠历史影响，并结合历史答案支持估计、支持目标注意力干预、三元解码融合及视觉一致性约束。客观看，其价值在于细粒度区分可用与误导性历史，并通过多路预测融合提升解码鲁棒性。

Abstract: In multimodal multi-turn dialogue, dialogue history plays a dual role: reliable responses provide useful context for subsequent turns, whereas hallucinated responses can propagate errors across turns. Existing training-free methods globally adjust the effect of dialogue history on generation, making it difficult to suppress misleading information without weakening useful context. To address this trade-off, we introduce Support-Guided Triadic Decoding (SGTD), a training-free framework that selectively controls the influence of unreliable history and fuses complementary predictions during decoding. Specifically, Historical Answer Support Estimation measures the visual support of historical responses against type-matched alternatives and identifies low-support content that may mislead later generation. Support-Targeted Attention Intervention then weakens the influence of this content while preserving the surrounding dialogue, producing a support-refined prediction. Triadic Decoding combines this prediction with the full-history prediction and answer-free visual evidence, while the Support-Adaptive Plausibility Constraint filters implausible tokens using visual agreement and historical support. Experiments on MMHalSnowball and VisDiaHalBench across representative LVLMs show that SGTD consistently achieves the highest accuracy under hallucinated histories while preserving the benefits of factual context.

[61] Query-Aware vs. Query-Agnostic Training-Free Video Token Pruning in Multimodal LLMs cs.CVPDF

Divya Kothandaraman, Timo Kumkel, Jaclyn Pytlarz

TL;DR: 论文对多模态大语言模型中的无训练视频token剪枝进行了受控研究，在统一Qwen-3-VL主干、token预算和解码设置下比较21种剪枝原则，涵盖query-agnostic、浅层query条件、结构化query条件和整体式策略。作者提出Structured Query Pruning，将问题分解为语义锚点，并提出融合锚点相关性与无query视觉多样性的Pareto变体。实验表明原始问题embedding相关性不可靠，而结构化锚点更有效，但强query-agnostic覆盖方法仍具竞争力。

Details

Motivation: 视频LLM需要处理大量视觉token，token剪枝对高效推理很重要；但无训练剪枝方法中是否以及如何使用用户query仍缺乏清晰结论，论文希望厘清query条件化的实际价值。

Result: 在Video-MME、MVBench和LongVideoBench上，基于原始问题embedding的相关性比最佳方法低8到16分，而query分解得到的语义锚点可避免该失败；强query-agnostic覆盖方法在全场比较中仍具竞争力或更优。结构化选择在紧预算下的证据特定和长上下文任务中收益最大，整体式选择器在广泛任务上优于单独相关性或冗余性方法，同时剪枝降低成本和内存。

Insight: 创新点在于系统受控比较多种无训练视频token剪枝原则，并提出基于问题分解的语义锚点剪枝及结合视觉多样性的Pareto变体。客观来看，论文的重要贡献是揭示query访问并非天然占优，并给出“默认保留视觉覆盖，在需要选择性证据检索时加入结构化相关性”的实用设计规则。

Abstract: Video Large Language Models process large numbers of visual tokens, making token pruning important for efficient inference. Training-free pruning methods differ in whether and how they use the user query, and the value of query conditioning remains unclear. We present a controlled study of training-free, pre-LLM video token selection. Using a shared Qwen-3-VL backbone, token budgets, and decoding settings, we compare 21 pruning principles spanning query-agnostic, shallow query-conditioned, structured query-conditioned, and holistic strategies. To instantiate the last two categories, we introduce Structured Query Pruning, which decomposes the question into semantic anchors, and a Pareto variant, which combines anchor relevance with query-agnostic visual diversity. Across Video-MME, MVBench, and LongVideoBench, relevance computed from the raw question embedding is unreliable (8–16 points below the best methods), whereas anchors derived from query decomposition avoid this failure. However, query access is not inherently advantageous: strong query-agnostic coverage methods remain competitive or superior in the full-field comparison. Relative to a redundancy-only baseline, structured selection helps most on evidence-specific and long-context tasks at tight budgets; on broad tasks, the holistic selector improves over either relevance or redundancy alone. Further, pruning reduces cost and memory. These results support a practical design rule: preserve visual coverage by default, and add structured relevance when the task and token budget demand selective evidence retrieval.

[62] TwoPass: Draft-Guided Few-Step Video Prediction for Robotic Manipulation cs.CVPDF

Yijun Liu, Shijia Ge, Tongtong Feng, Xin Wang, Yuan Meng

TL;DR: 论文提出 TwoPass，一种面向机器人操作的动作条件视频预测框架，通过自生成草稿训练与推理阶段的草稿复用迭代精炼，在保持标准 flow-matching 目标的同时显著减少网络调用次数。方法仅需每段视频 4 次函数评估（NFE），并在 Bridge 和 Language Table 上取得领先结果。

Details

Motivation: 现有动作条件视频预测器通常从噪声独立去噪每个未来帧，未利用自身对未来的估计，导致预测质量受限，尤其是被操作物体，并且需要大量网络调用。论文旨在提升预测质量的同时实现少步推理。

Result: 在 Bridge 和 Language Table 基准上，TwoPass 以仅 4 NFE 取得 state-of-the-art 结果，远少于现有预测器所需的 50–59 次调用。

Insight: 创新点在于自生成草稿作为同一噪声状态下的自条件、仅在存在草稿时应用的残差式晚期适配器，以及推理时从每次速度预测同时获得求解器更新和下一草稿的复用机制。客观看，其价值在于不改变 flow-matching 训练目标而实现低 NFE 高质量视频预测。

Abstract: Action-conditioned video prediction lets robots anticipate the visual consequences of their actions for planning and policy evaluation. Existing predictors denoise each future from noise without using their own estimate of it, which limits the quality of the predicted future, in particular of the manipulated objects, and requires many network calls. We present TwoPass, a self-conditioned training-and-inference framework that keeps the standard flow-matching objective. Self-Generated Draft Training supplies a detached clean-video estimate from the shared network as conditioning at the same noisy state. A Refinement-Only Late Adapter applies a dedicated residual transformation when a draft is present. At inference, Draft-Reuse Iterative Refinement obtains both a solver update and the next draft from each velocity prediction, requiring as few as 4 function evaluations (NFE) per video chunk. Training samples discrete solver-query noise levels and updates the pretrained backbone. Extensive experiments on Bridge and Language Table show that TwoPass achieves state-of-the-art results with 4 NFE, far fewer than the 50-59 calls of existing predictors. Code: https://github.com/PlantPotatoOnMoon/TwoPass.

[63] UniReFP: Robust Unified Fingerprinting for Vision Models against Cross-Task Repurposing Attacks cs.CV | cs.CRPDF

Ziye Geng, Guang Yang, Yihang Chen, Lingfeng Yao, Xing Shi

TL;DR: 论文首次识别出一种新的模型修改攻击——跨任务模型重用途攻击，可使现有模型指纹方法失效。为此提出UniReFP统一模型指纹框架，利用共享类级语义空间中的所有权证据而非原始任务特定输出空间，使不同任务视觉模型在被重用途且输出格式异构时仍可进行所有权验证。方法通过构造代理分类器、优化分组指纹，并在验证时用任务感知语义投影和组内一致性判断所有权。

Details

Motivation: 现有模型指纹方法依赖原始任务特定输出空间，当受保护模型被跨任务重用途后，指纹可能不再适用，难以验证模型所有权。论文旨在解决跨任务重用途攻击下视觉模型统一指纹与所有权验证问题。

Result: 摘要称大量实验验证了UniReFP的有效性，在跨任务重用途攻击、常见模型修改攻击及其组合下持续取得更优表现；但未给出具体定量指标、benchmark名称或明确的SOTA比较。

Insight: 创新点在于将所有权证据放在共享类级语义空间，构造代理分类器使指纹生成独立于原任务输出空间，并优化分组指纹编码所有权证据；验证时用任务感知语义投影处理异构输出，并通过组内一致性判断所有权。客观看，其核心贡献是把指纹验证从任务特定输出空间解耦，以语义存在向量对齐跨任务异构输出。

Abstract: We identify, for the first time, a new model modification attack–the cross-task model repurposing attack–that can render fingerprints generated by existing model fingerprinting approaches inapplicable. To address this challenge, we propose UniReFP–a novel unified model fingerprinting framework applicable to diverse vision models across different tasks–that utilizes ownership evidence in a shared class-level semantic space rather than original task-specific output space, enabling effective ownership verification when a protected model is repurposed across tasks with heterogeneous output formats. Specifically, UniReFP constructs a surrogate classifier by repurposing the protected model, enabling fingerprints to be generated independently of the original task-dependent output space of the protected model. Moreover, it optimizes grouped fingerprints to encode ownership evidence: fingerprints within the same group are encouraged to induce consistent semantic responses and intermediate representations on the surrogate classifier, while producing dispersed responses on independently trained reference classifiers. During verification, UniReFP uses a task-aware semantic projection mechanism to map heterogeneous suspect-model outputs into unified semantic-presence vectors, and determines ownership by measuring inner-group consistency. Extensive experimental results validate the effectiveness of UniReFP, consistently showing superior performance under cross-task repurposing attacks, commonly studied model modification attacks, and even their combinations.

[64] Online Mistake Detection in Instruction-Conditioned Procedural Video cs.CV | cs.AIPDF

Amit Feldman, Ilan Shimshoni, Rotem Dror

TL;DR: 论文研究第一视角程序性视频中的在线、指令条件错误检测：模型仅利用已观测视频、任务指令和历史预测，识别并定位已完成动作尝试，分类为正确、修改、失误、纠正或额外动作，遗漏步骤在视频结束后判断。作者比较了分阶段模块化流水线与单步联合模型，并重新划分EgoPER以在所有数据划分中包含错误视频。

Details

Motivation: 现有在线错误检测器不利用指令文本，而指令条件方法多为离线处理；实际助手需要在仍可补救时发现操作错误，因此需要在线结合任务指令判断动作是否按步骤正确执行。

Result: 模块化与联合模型的步骤分割F1@0.5相近（93.6%与92.1%），联合模型二分类错误检测F1@0.5更高（84.1% vs. 80.7%），在slip类上提升最大（56.4% vs. 37.5%）；在含错误测试视频上，两者均优于已发布的AEM和ESTANet检查点。

Insight: 创新点在于形式化并实现在线指令条件的第一视角程序性错误检测，同时区分多种执行偏差并将遗漏判断延后；客观看，联合建模减少模块间误差传播，是其优于模块化流水线的重要分析结论。

Abstract: An assistant monitoring a person performing a procedure is most useful when it can detect mistakes while there is still time to recover. This requires judging actions against task instructions as the video unfolds, identifying the attempted step, locating it in time, and assessing its execution. Existing online detectors do not use the instruction text, while instruction-conditioned methods operate offline. In this paper, we study online, instruction-conditioned mistake detection in egocentric videos. Using only observed video, task instructions, and prior predictions, our model identifies and localizes completed action attempts and classifies them as correct, modified, slip, corrections, or additions; omitted steps are determined after the video ends. We compare a modular pipeline, with separate step identification, temporal localization, and execution verification, to a joint model that predicts all outputs in a single step. To support supervised learning from mistakes, we repartition EgoPER to include erroneous videos in all splits. The approaches achieve similar step-segmentation F1@0.5 (93.6% and 92.1%), while the joint model achieves higher binary mistake-detection F1@0.5 (84.1% vs. 80.7%), with its largest gain on slips (56.4% vs. 37.5%). Component-level analysis suggests that error propagation between stages contributes to the modular approach’s lower detection score. On test videos containing mistakes, both designs outperform released AEM and ESTANet checkpoints under our evaluation protocol.

Details

Motivation: 将 MLLM 从图像扩展到视频时，大量视觉 token 带来显著内存和延迟开销；现有视频 token 压缩方法因训练设置、任务和模型不一致而难以公平比较，同时混合 Mamba-Transformer 架构的兴起也引出压缩策略能否跨骨干迁移的问题。

Result: 在三个互补视频理解任务上进行了免训练和微调设置的系统评估，结果显示压缩行为具有任务依赖性，并刻画了压缩放置、时间编码和骨干选择的影响；摘要未给出具体定量指标或 SOTA 结论。

Abstract: Extending multimodal large language models (MLLMs) from images to videos faces substantial memory and latency challenges due to the massive number of visual tokens. These challenges have driven the development of numerous visual token compression methods and more efficient LLM architectures. However, existing compression methods are often evaluated with different training setups, tasks, and models, making fair comparison difficult. Meanwhile, the growing use of hybrid Mamba-Transformer architectures raises questions about whether compression strategies transfer across backbones. We present VidToC, a controlled evaluation framework for representative compression algorithms, LLM backbones, and time encoding strategies, covering training-free and fine-tuned settings on three complementary video understanding tasks. Our results reveal task-dependent compression behavior and characterize the effects of compression placement, time encoding, and backbone choice. These findings provide empirical guidance for designing and selecting video token compression strategies across tasks and training settings.

[66] Characterizing Age-Conditioned Affective Reasoning in Vision-Language Models cs.CVPDF

Shahriyar Zaman Ridoy, Azmine Toushik Wasi, Lin Gu, Ruogu Fang

TL;DR: 论文系统评估了17个视觉语言模型在年轻、中年、老年成人提示下对900张OASIS图像的情感效价判断，并与822名参与者的图像级年龄差异评分比较。结果表明，无年龄提示时模型能较好追踪人类平均效价，但年龄提示带来的模型评分变化几乎不能预测真实人群的年龄相关情感差异，甚至在敏感图像上方向相反。作者据此强调，基础模型的人口个性化评估必须在单个刺激层面与人类评分对照。

Details

Motivation: 论文关注视觉语言模型是否能通过年龄提示模拟不同年龄人群对图像情感的主观评分，指出现有评估可能只看聚合相关性，而忽略个体图像层面的年龄差异是否真实。

Result: 无年龄线索时模型与人类平均效价相关性很高（中位Pearson r=0.891）；但模型年龄效应与人类年龄效应的相关性仅-0.030至0.093，远低于估计上限0.595。老年提示平均降低效价0.845分，而老年参与者对54张敏感愉悦图像反而比年轻参与者高0.443分；提供其他图像人类评分后方向一致性改善，但600张留出图像上的图像特定保真度仍较低（r_part=0.105）。

Insight: 论文的创新点在于从个体刺激层面刻画VLM的年龄条件情感推理，而非仅报告整体相关性；其客观启示是模型可能学到年龄提示的刻板方向性偏移，却不能复现真实年龄群体的细粒度情感差异， demographic personalization 评估需要人类图像级对照。

Abstract: An age prompt can change how a vision-language model (VLM) rates the emotion evoked by an image. Does the change predict how people of different ages rate that same image? We evaluate 17 VLMs on 900 OASIS images using young-, middle-, and older-adult prompts and estimate image-specific age differences from ratings by 822 participants. Without an age cue, models closely track average human valence (median Pearson $r=0.891$). Under an older-adult prompt, 16 of 17 models assign lower valence on average than under a young-adult prompt. Yet after adjusting for image pleasantness, correlations between model and human age effects range from $-0.030$ to $0.093$, against an estimated ceiling of $0.595$. The discrepancy is clearest on 54 sensitive images rated as pleasant. Across models, the older prompt reduces valence by $0.845$ points on average, while older participants rate these images $0.443$ points higher than younger participants. Providing human ratings from other images improves agreement on the direction of age differences, although image-specific fidelity remains modest on 600 held-out images ($r_{\mathrm{part}}=0.105$). These results show why evaluations of demographic personalization in foundation models need human comparisons at the level of individual stimuli. Project page available at https://shahriyar-zaman.github.io/age-conditioned-emotion-reasoning/

Anh Dao, Xinxin Liu, Hang Yu, Jeffrey A Chan Santiago, Yuzhang Shang

Abstract: World action models (WAMs) couple generative video modeling with robotic action prediction but typically require large video and action transformers, limiting deployment efficiency. We present LoopedWAM, a compact WAM that recurrently applies a shared six-block core in each expert, executing 30 transformer layers while storing only 12 blocks per expert. To account for their asymmetric inference costs, the video and action experts can operate at different depths: visual features are computed once per query, whereas action computation repeats at every sampling step. We further propose elastic self-distillation, which trains a single elastic checkpoint, LoopedWAM-E, across action-depth and sampling-step budgets. With only 0.58B policy parameters and no robot pretraining, LoopedWAM achieves 96.1% success on LIBERO, 91.9% on RoboTwin 2.0, and 52.2% on RoboCasaGR1, while using $7.4\times$ less peak GPU memory than Fast-WAM under matched inference measurements. On LIBERO, LoopedWAM-E achieves 96.0% success in 27.6 ms or 95.2% in 24.7 ms on an H100, providing multiple accuracy-latency operating points from a single checkpoint; with two sampling steps and four action loops, it stays within 0.5 points of LoopedWAM on all three benchmarks. Ablations demonstrate that weight-shared depth preserves competitive performance, while aggressive action-depth reduction requires explicit budget-aware training. On a real AgileX PiPER arm, LoopedWAM succeeds in 71.3% of 80 trials across four tasks.

Abstract: Current multi-shot video generators render only limited durations per pass, requiring long movies to be stitched from local segments across which narrative progression and visual identity drift. To synthesize long-horizon movies with Coherence across Time, we present CaT-Movie, a multi-shot generation system consisting of a global story planner and a local window renderer. The planner expands user requests via a large language model to preserve, infer, and complete story details, while deterministic code maintains the resulting storyboard across shots to compile self-contained shot prompts and index reference frames from generated history. To execute this guidance, the renderer applies reference-routed sparse self-attention and shot-wise cross-attention to route identities from the indexed frames while isolating per-shot textual conditions. Given the scarcity of high-quality open-source multi-shot data, we construct a dataset of over 350K film shots annotated with semantic state transitions, entity grounding, and multi-granularity requests. [69] Physical Laws without Physics Loss? Probing Learned Dynamics in Video World Models through Forced Response cs.CV | cs.LGPDF

TL;DR: 论文提出用非线性受迫响应实验检验视频世界模型是否在仅使用flow-matching损失、没有物理损失的情况下学到物理规律，而非单纯模仿运动。作者引入视觉-数值双条件并适配六个image-to-video骨干预测悬挂物体受激运动；通过基于Taylor展开的三层动力学与几何测试，分析模型对线性响应、共振、高阶非线性和持续动力学的学习情况，并将发现转化为改进视频训练与生成的策略。

Details

Motivation: 现有整体运动与外观评分无法判断视频世界模型是学到了物理规律还是仅复现训练视频中的运动模式，因此需要一个能区分线性响应、高阶非线性和反事实泛化的系统性探测框架。

Result: 六个骨干均能复现线性响应（包括共振），其中五个能捕捉高阶动力学（包括非线性软化）；Wan预测准确且可泛化到未见激励、物体、姿态和场景。值得注意的是，非线性软化在训练中仅占摆动平方位移的0.74%，但模型仍能体现；双摆和摇块实验显示持续动力学比短暂冲击学得更可靠，但状态估计或传播出错时学习到的动力学可能失效。

Insight: 创新点在于用受迫响应、视觉-数值双条件和基于Taylor展开的分层测试来探测视频世界模型中的隐式动力学，并结合反事实实验区分记忆回放与规律学习；客观上看，其可借鉴之处是提供了无需物理损失即可诊断模型是否学到线性/高阶动力学及失效模式的实验范式，并反向指导视频训练与生成。

Abstract: Do video world models learn physical laws without a physics loss, or merely imitate motion? Overall motion and appearance scores cannot settle it. We investigate this through nonlinear forced response: an excitation shakes the support of a suspended object. We introduce dual visual-numeric conditioning and adapt six image-to-video backbones to predict this motion using only the flow-matching loss. Wan predicts motion accurately and generalizes to unseen excitations, objects, poses, and scenes. Counterfactual tests suggest capabilities beyond replaying training videos. But what laws have they learned? We design three tiers of tests using Taylor expansions of the dynamics and geometry. All six reproduce the linear response, including resonance; five capture higher-order dynamics, including nonlinear softening. Remarkably, softening is barely present in training: only 0.74% of the swing’s squared displacement. Extensions to a double pendulum and a rocking block show sustained dynamics are learned more faithfully than brief impacts. Yet learned dynamics may fail to appear when state estimation or propagation goes wrong. We turn these tests and findings into strategies to improve video training and generation.

[70] What One Sample Cannot See: Why Fewer Denoising Steps Can Score Higher on Physics Benchmarks cs.CV | cs.LGPDF

Yutao Li, Chen Wang

TL;DR: 论文指出，将视频生成器作为世界模型评测时，Physics-IQ 因每个条件只与单个录制视频比较，可能奖励生成未来多样性的降低，导致少去噪步数的模糊或早停视频反而获得更高物理分数。作者提出 Kernel Physics-IQ，通过每个条件两个独立样本在期望上消除该激励，并发现多个图像到视频模型在两步采样下新指标下降，而官方分数可能上升。

Details

Motivation: 现有 Physics-IQ 等物理基准用单一样本匹配评估多未来场景，无法区分预测分布是否真实，甚至会偏好坍缩、低多样性的生成结果，从而误判少步去噪采样和世界模型能力。

Result: 提出 Kernel Physics-IQ 后，五个图像到视频模型的两步采样分数均下降；CogVideoX 上官方分数提高 0.055，而新指标下降 0.131；在已知结果概率的受控物理系统中，新指标更偏好真实分布而非坍缩预测。

Insight: 创新点在于精确分解单样本物理评分的偏差，揭示其奖励生成未来变异减少的问题，并提出只需两个独立样本的核化评估指标。客观来看，论文将世界模型评测从“单视频是否匹配已发生结果”推进到“预测分布是否忠实覆盖可能未来”，对视频生成世界模型的基准设计有借鉴意义。

Abstract: When a storm can head north or south, a forecast down the middle may score better under squared error than either plausible path. The same paradox appears when video generators are tested as world models: fewer denoising steps can raise physics scores even as videos blur or stop moving early. Why? Physics-IQ compares each generated video with one recording, although a scene can have multiple valid futures. An exact decomposition shows its scores can reward reduced variation among generated futures, even when their distribution becomes less accurate. We introduce Kernel Physics-IQ to remove this incentive in expectation, needing only two independent samples per condition. Two-step sampling lowers Kernel Physics-IQ in all five image-to-video models. For CogVideoX, the official score rises by 0.055 while ours falls by 0.131. Changing the sampling schedule reveals further differences official scores miss. In controlled physical systems with known outcome probabilities, our metric favors the true distribution over collapsed predictions. In short, a world model should be judged not only by how closely one video matches what happened, but by how faithfully its predictions represent what could have happened.

Abstract: Image color enhancement aims to improve visual appeal, but previous methods often learn to reproduce ground-truth expert targets. By contrast, large generative models learn rich visual knowledge from large image collections and often produce vivid, appealing image edits. In this paper, we introduce AesDistill, a framework for distilling generative aesthetics into image color enhancement. We propose SEAF, a dual-branch framework for selective semantic enrichment and visual evidence fusion. SEAF assigns generated fields to distinct roles: entity background enriches semantic input, while image descriptions supply alignment statistics. The semantic branch combines the original post text, background from a frozen MLLM, and optical character recognition (OCR) text. The aligned visual branch combines global and local visual features with statistics of cross-modal and inter-image similarity. Abstract: We propose VC-VQA, a large-scale benchmark for vision-centric knowledge reasoning, aiming to systematically evaluate the capabilities of Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) in fine-grained visual knowledge acquisition and complex multi-stage reasoning. Unlike existing benchmarks that primarily treat visual information as entity recognition and rely on text-based external knowledge, VC-VQA explicitly models the role of visual information by considering both its source (internal vs. external images) and stage of involvement (pre-retrieval vs. post-retrieval). Specifically, VC-VQA requires models to retrieve external visual evidence, identify visual clues before retrieval, and localize, verify, or match retrieved knowledge against the internal image. Overall, VC-VQA provides a more challenging benchmark for evaluating vision-centric knowledge reasoning in MLLMs. Our code and dataset are available at https://github.com/CASIA-IVA-Lab/VC-VQA.

[74] D3-Cache: Accelerating Diffusion Models with Dynamic Mode Decomposition cs.CVPDF

Yanbo Li, An Xiang, Xiaoxin Hu, Feng Wang, Cheng-zhong Xu

TL;DR: 论文提出 D3-Cache，一种免训练的扩散模型采样加速方法。它将动态模态分解（DMD）应用于扩散采样过程，对短历史精确速度进行拟合，并仅预测调度器实际消费的速度；同时用时域感知门控根据预测误差动态决定何时跳过网络评估。该方法无需训练或逐模型离线校准，内存开销可忽略，并在多个图像和视频扩散模型上实现显著加速。

Details

Motivation: 扩散Transformer生成质量高但采样每步都需完整网络评估，成本很高；现有免训练缓存方法常依赖逐模型校准的跳过准则或逐层预测历史，通用性和部署便利性不足。论文希望在不训练、不逐模型校准的前提下，高效预测采样所需速度并自适应跳过网络评估。

Result: 在四个最先进的图像和视频模型上，D3-Cache 实现 2.6–3.8 倍加速，同时对基础模型输出保持高保真度；四个模型共享同一历史长度和风险容忍度。

Insight: 创新点在于将 DMD 用于扩散采样速度预测，只预测调度器消费的速度，并用时域感知门控按预测误差动态调度网络评估；相比依赖逐层预测历史或逐模型校准曲线的方法，更轻量、免训练且超参可共享。客观来看，其可借鉴之处是结合短历史动态建模与误差驱动跳过策略，在缓存加速中兼顾通用性和保真度。

Abstract: Diffusion Transformers generate high-quality images and videos, but every sampling step requires a full network evaluation. Training-free caching reduces this cost by reusing or forecasting network outputs across steps, but common approaches use per-model calibrated skip criteria or maintain per-layer prediction histories. We present D3-Cache, which applies Dynamic Mode Decomposition (DMD) to Diffusion sampling: it fits DMD to a short history of exact velocities and forecasts only the velocity that the scheduler consumes. A horizon-aware gate decides when to skip: whenever the network is evaluated, its output is compared with the forecast for that step, and the larger the error, the sooner the network is evaluated again, up to a cap on consecutive skipped steps. D3-Cache requires no training or per-model offline calibration curve and adds negligible memory. It shares one history length and risk tolerance across the four evaluated models. On four state-of-the-art image and video models, D3-Cache achieves speedups of 2.6-3.8x with high fidelity to the base model output.

Abstract: Multimodal image fusion traditionally generates a single fused image from paired observations at a specific viewpoint, limiting comprehensive scene perception and adaptability to diverse perceptual objectives. To overcome these limitations, we present LIFT, a novel framework for our proposed language-controlled 3D image fusion (L3D-IF) paradigm that extends multimodal image fusion from 2D images to free-viewpoint 3D scenes, enabling language-guided fusion tailored to diverse perceptual objectives. First, LIFT formulates Semantic Modality Binding (SMB), which interprets language instructions to identify semantic targets and their associated modality preferences and grounds these targets in corresponding regions of a shared Gaussian scene, establishing persistent semantic-to-spatial correspondences for consistent fusion control across viewpoints. Second, Language-Conditioned Gaussian Appearance (LCGA) is developed to achieve scene-wide multimodal appearance modeling by combining adaptive multimodal blending with language- and viewpoint-conditioned appearance refinement, enabling spatially adaptive realization of diverse fusion appearances while preserving source fidelity at pure-modality endpoints. Finally, we construct a Multi-View Language-Supervision (MVLS) dataset that associates language instructions and calibrated viewpoints with modulated fused images, providing effective supervision for learning instruction-dependent fusion from finite multi-view observations. Abstract: Image-to-slide reconstruction must recover both visual appearance and native object structure for subsequent editing. Yet visually faithful charts and tables may lack editable series and cells, and direct code generation couples content prediction to rendering implementation. We present Slide2PPT, a model-agnostic, training-free framework that equips both open- and closed-source vision-language models with native PowerPoint reconstruction capabilities. Through a unified typed interface, the model predicts object records while a fixed renderer materializes their native properties, enabling integration with different backbones without task-specific training or architectural changes. Localization-guided refinement then connects visual discrepancies to object records for correction. Regional updates preserve unselected records, while whole-slide updates address cross-region relationships. Record refinement and targeted object editing support structural and attribute-level changes, with rendered feedback governing iterative acceptance. To evaluate this framework across diverse reconstruction demands, we introduce Slide2PPT-Bench, covering controlled elements, real slides, and scientific figures. Evaluation separately measures file production, rendered agreement, and native-object availability. Details

Motivation: 人类熟练度估计具有挑战性，因为技能是高级且主观的属性，无法仅通过动作身份捕获。现有方法利用多模态观察（如自我-外部视频、注视、姿态、惯性信号），但传感器级证据与抽象熟练度标签之间的差距难以弥合。

Abstract: Human proficiency estimation is challenging because skill is a high-level and often subjective property that is not captured by action identity alone. Existing approaches leverage multimodal observations such as ego-exo video, gaze, pose, and inertial signals, but the gap between sensor-level evidence and abstract proficiency labels remains difficult to bridge. We present SkillLens, a proficiency estimation framework that uses atomic action descriptions as temporal guidance, rather than direct semantic supervision, to localize proficiency-relevant windows for dense visual-dynamics extraction. Each window is summarized into an action-dynamics feature and scored by a high-low prototype margin against higher- and lower-proficiency executions from the same activity-action category. Fusing these ego/exo PDM cues with a global ego-exo-gaze baseline through CORAL ordinal calibration, our method achieves 0.542 accuracy on Ego-Exo4D. TL;DR: 论文研究 DeepStack 风格视觉语言模型中，输入层视觉 token（主入口）与更深层视觉 token（后续入口）分别对最终答案的贡献。作者通过构造仅在物体颜色上冲突的两套视觉输入、降低主入口饱和度、同时退化后仅恢复后续特征、以及置零某一入口等干预实验，比较 DeepStack、Qwen3-VL-8B/32B 和 Granite Vision 的依赖与纠错行为，并据此探索拆分推理中的精度分配。

Result: 在照片冲突测试中，原始 DeepStack 更跟随后续入口，而 Qwen3-VL-8B/32B 更跟随主入口；削弱主入口后，后续特征对颜色相对分数的影响在四个模型中均增强，即使轻微去饱和且无冲突准确率仍为 100%。退化两个入口并仅恢复后续入口时，同样模糊使两个 Qwen 在条纹方向任务上损失 50 个百分点，32B 恢复 48 点而 8B 仅恢复 8 点；单入口干净准确率可预测可丢失入口，6 个预测中 5 个在新 GQA 属性问题上成立。拆分推理中仅以更高精度发送 DeepStack 主入口特征，可在相近准确率下比所有入口共享精度少传 49% 字节。

Insight: 创新点在于用受控视觉冲突和入口级干预来因果区分主入口与深层视觉 token 的作用，而不是仅观察注意力；它揭示了不同模型对视觉入口的依赖差异、深层特征的纠错潜力及规模效应，并给出按入口分配精度以节省通信的实用思路。

Abstract: DeepStack-style vision-language models feed visual tokens to the language model at its input, and again at deeper layers. We call these the main entry and the later entries. What do the later entries add when the model already has the image? We give the entries versions that differ in one object’s color and see which one the answer follows. On photographs, the original DeepStack follows the later entries, while Qwen3-VL-8B/32B follow the main entry. Does weakening the main entry give the later features more influence? On synthetic images, we keep this conflict and reduce color saturation at the main entry only. The unchanged later features gain a stronger effect on the relative scores of the two colors, in all four models, including Granite Vision. This holds even under mild desaturation, when accuracy without the conflict stays at 100%. To test whether later features can also correct errors, we degrade both entries and restore only the later ones. The same blur costs both Qwen models 50 percentage points of accuracy on stripe orientation. The 32B model recovers 48 points and the 8B model only 8. Accuracy with one clean entry predicted which entry each model can lose, and five of six tested predictions held on new GQA attribute questions. With the other entry zeroed, Qwen3-VL-8B loses only 0.3 points on its main entry alone, and DeepStack at most 3.45 points on either entry alone. In split inference on these questions, DeepStack can then send only its main-entry features at higher precision. This takes 49% fewer bytes than all entries at a shared precision, at similar accuracy.

TL;DR: 论文提出 SteerTrace，一种针对世界行动模型（WAM）的定向通用攻击，并用因果干预追踪攻击效应在模型内部的作用路径。它用每个模型共享的 4/255 ℓ∞ 扰动，将执行动作推向攻击者指定目标，同时在 8 个 WAM 上分析未来 token、当前帧 token、视频专家 FFN 神经元和残差方向对攻击效果的贡献。实验表明该攻击可显著降低任务成功率并揭示攻击依赖的内部表征。

Details

Motivation: WAM 将预测的未来视频视为安全信号，但其在定向攻击下是否可靠、攻击如何影响动作生成尚不清楚；论文关注 WAM 的安全脆弱性并追踪攻击在模型内部的作用机制。

Result: 在 LIBERO 和 RoboTwin 上，对四个家族八个 WAM，单模型共享扰动使任务成功率接近 0，并将动作推向目标；同预算噪声和 BadWAM 的目标方向动作偏移很小。干预显示恢复未来 token 可消除 61–94% 定向效应，某些约束下恢复当前帧 token 可消除 87–99%，恢复视频专家 10–29 层仅 0.36% FFN 神经元可消除 72–96%。

Abstract: World action models (WAMs) predict future videos and actions with one backbone, and their imagined future is often seen as a safety signal. We introduce SteerTrace, a targeted universal attack on WAMs that also traces its effect inside the model. One $\ell_\infty$-bounded (4/255) perturbation per model, shared across frames, tasks, and camera views, pushes the executed action toward an attacker-chosen target. On eight WAMs from four families (FastWAM, Faster-WAM, OpenWAM and LingBot-VA) on LIBERO and RoboTwin, it drives task success close to zero and moves the attacked action toward the target. Same-budget noise and BadWAM produce little action shift in the target direction. We use the signed targeted effect to measure how much of the effect each intervention removes or reproduces. Under the standard attack, restoring future tokens removes 61-94% of the targeted effect in the five models whose action experts attend to them. When the attack is constrained to keep the predicted future close to the clean prediction, the targeted effect can become more dependent on current-frame tokens. In three of these five models, restoring current-frame tokens removes 87-99% of the effect, compared with 9-15% for future tokens. In six models, restoring just 0.36% of the FFN neurons in layers 10-29 of the video expert removes 72-96% of the targeted effect. Residual directions 2-16 hold only 11-18% of the shift energy, yet removing them cancels most of the targeted effect.

Abstract: 3D visual grounding (3D-VG) is a task that bridges the natural language and 3D space, serving as a foundational capability for embodied intelligence and human-computer interaction. Although recent 3D-VG agents overcome the reliance on pre-processed 3D point clouds, they essentially employ a unidirectional, pipeline-style tool-calling architecture. This architecture separates semantic understanding from geometric reconstruction, leading to error accumulation and an inability to autonomously recover from failures. To address this flaw, this paper proposes the Dual-Anchor Collaborative Agent (DACA), which reinterprets 3D-VG as a semantic-geometric dual-anchor collaborative reasoning paradigm. DACA comprises two complementary specialized tools: the semantic anchor tool holds the visual semantic features of the target and achieves efficient target tracking via optical flow and VLM local verification; the geometric anchor tool holds the 3D spatial geometric features of the target and performs global spatial reasoning via voxelization and multi-keypoint projection. The two components exchange information bidirectionally via a standardized tool call protocol, autonomously deciding on the next perception-action step to form a closed-loop optimization. [81] CGM: Chaos-Guided Graph Attention and Expert Routing for Autism Stereotypy Detection cs.CV | cs.LGPDF

TL;DR: 论文提出CGM（Chaos-Guided Graph attention and Mixture-of-experts）模型，用于从视频中检测自闭症儿童的刻板行为。该模型用角度速度的加窗样本熵计算每个关节、每一帧的运动不规则性张量，并让这个张量直接驱动计算：一方面在图注意力logit上加入成对项，把注意力集中到不规则关节上；另一方面用同一信号作为门控，把每帧路由到4个专家中的2个。

Details

Motivation: 现有骨架图注意力只按内容（运动幅度）为关节加权，会把动作混乱的手腕与静止的关节同等对待；但刻板行为的本质是运动展开方式不可预测，并非运动量大小。实验中所有基于幅度的运动学描述子都无法区分刻板片段，只有角度速度的加窗样本熵显著上升（Hedges’ g=+1.05）。

Result: 在116个片段上做被试分组五折交叉验证，达到0.930平衡准确率，比图注意力基线提高0.187；组件排序在公开MMASD数据集上可复现；在留出的Panoptic Studio会话上，不做重训练即可保持0.86特异性，而对比的无混沌基线最多只有0.35。消融显示，不带不规则性的路由无稳定收益，单用混沌调制贡献+0.132；同一张量导出的严重度指数分半信度为0.87、组间Hedges’ g=+1.08，且在同一儿童多次治疗录像间保持一致。

Insight: 论文宣称的创新点在于把“混沌”（运动不规则性）作为结构信号，用来驱动注意力与专家路由，而不只作为特征输入：该信号显式修改注意力logit，并决定MoE门控。从客观角度看，更可借鉴的是将熵类不规则性度量嵌入注意力权重和路由机制这一设计思路，以及用同一信号派生可解释的严重度指数；此外，论文通过“置零张量使94%片段注意力重对齐”验证了因果作用。

Abstract: Skeleton graph attention for autism video analysis weights joints by content alone, giving an erratic wrist the same standing as a still one. Stereotypy, however, is defined by how unpredictably movement unfolds rather than how much of it occurs: in our cohort, every amplitude-based kinematic descriptor falls in stereotyped clips, while windowed Sample Entropy of angular velocity alone rises (Hedges’ g = +1.05). We compute this irregularity as a per-joint, per-frame tensor that structurally drives computation. The proposed chaos-guided graph attention and mixture-of-experts model (CGM) couples two mechanisms. First, a learned pairwise term added to the graph-attention logit concentrates attention on irregular joints and re-ranks the remaining edge weight toward jointly irregular pairs; its unconstrained coefficient neither vanishes nor inverts, and zeroing the tensor realigns attention in 94% of clips. Second, a gate conditioned on the same signal routes each frame to two of four experts, whose weights track frame-level irregularity. Under subject-grouped five-fold cross-validation on 116 clips, CGM reaches 0.930 balanced accuracy, exceeding the graph-attention baseline by +0.187; the component ordering replicates on the public MMASD dataset, and on held-out Panoptic Studio sessions, CGM keeps 0.86 specificity without retraining, against at most 0.35 for chaos-free baselines. In ablation, routing without irregularity yields no reliable gain, whereas chaos modulation alone contributes +0.132. A severity index from the same tensor reaches 0.87 split-half reliability, separates the cohorts with Hedges’ g = +1.08, and stays consistent within children across therapy recordings, supporting video-only monitoring of stereotypy during intervention.

Bumyoon Kim, Yongseong Kim, Inhyuk Jeong, Byeungwoo Jeon

TL;DR: 论文研究面向专业级高保真质量的学习视频压缩，指出当前学习视频编解码器在编码器固定前馈结构和有限潜变量容量下难以覆盖专业质量范围。作者提出一种基于搜索的学习帧间编解码器，联合搜索块运动、学习块变换、分区、跳过决策和量化系数，并按率失真代价选择，从而显著提升学习视频压缩的高质量覆盖范围。

Details

Motivation: 现有学习视频编解码器通常采用固定潜变量容量的前馈编码器，难以达到专业视频工作流的高保真质量范围；论文希望缩小学习编解码器与专业编解码器（如VVC、ProRes）在高质量区间上的差距。

Result: 在10-bit YCbCr 4:2:2测试序列上，四个近期学习编解码器仅覆盖专业质量范围的24%到38%，而所提方法在全部六个序列上达到该范围的上端；当残差块固定为16x16、潜变量容量为0.25时，同一编解码器可覆盖55%的范围，且有无量化时均如此。

Insight: 创新点在于将传统编解码器式的搜索机制引入学习视频压缩：编码器搜索块运动并由网络细化，同时在学习到的多种块变换间搜索，按率失真代价决定分区、跳过和量化系数，并支持最高为1的潜变量容量。可借鉴之处是可变潜变量容量与量化感知的率失真搜索相结合，以提升学习编解码器的高质量表达能力。

Abstract: Professional video workflows operate at qualities that learned video codecs do not reach. We measure how much of a professional quality range a codec covers, from the lowest quality of the standard test conditions, VVC at QP 37, to the highest 4:2:2 grade of the production codec ProRes. To compare inter-frame coding alone, every codec receives original frames as references at the intra positions. On 10-bit YCbCr 4:2:2 test sequences, four recent learned codecs cover 24 to 38% of this range. Their encoders are feed-forward networks with a fixed latent capacity, the number of latent values per sample, of at most 0.25, and removing their quantization raises the quality by at most 1 dB. We propose a learned inter-frame codec whose encoder searches, as the encoders of conventional codecs do. It searches block motion, which a learned network refines, and it searches among learned block transforms whose latent capacity ranges up to 1, selecting the partition, the skip decisions, and the quantized coefficients by a rate-distortion cost. Our codec reaches the upper end of the range on all six sequences. With all residual blocks fixed to 16x16, whose latent capacity is 0.25, the same codec covers 55% of the range, with or without quantization.

Abstract: Promptable segmenters answer what a thing is. Dense prediction tasks also ask class-agnostic questions, such as what stands out or hides, how light is blocked, transmitted or reflected, and where structure lies. Robots, surveillance systems and image editors build on their answers. We study promptable concept maps (PCM), which extend SAM 3’s promptable concept segmentation from noun instances to maps of concepts named by short noun phrases such as `”transparent object”, with “none” as an answer. PCM-Bench groups the standard test sets of seven such tasks by the question they ask. On it, we find that SAM 3 sees more than it detects. Its semantic path responds to many of these concepts, yet a low image-level presence score can block every detection of its instance path. We present UNVEIL, which trains a copy of SAM 3’s semantic path for these maps, with LoRA on copies of its last blocks. The same weights map thin structures such as edges and skeletons as well as object categories. Run next to SAM 3, it leaves SAM 3’s own outputs unchanged. Abstract: Identifying a driver’s gaze target provides object-level information for visual attention monitoring in intelligent vehicles. Gaze-zone classification and continuous attention localization provide limited information about the identity of the attended scene target. This paper proposes a modular framework that identifies object- or region-level gaze targets from synchronized in-cabin driver images and forward-view road images. The framework comprises four components: MFGaze, which combines a Vision Transformer pretrained with a masked autoencoder (MAE) and head-pose conditioning through feature-wise linear modulation (FiLM); panoptic scene understanding, which constructs a structured candidate set; polynomial ridge regression, which maps gaze features to 2D scene-image coordinates through individualized calibration; and entropy-driven target inference, which adaptively fuses boundary-distance geometry with class-level semantic priors. The complete framework achieves an overall Top-1 gaze target identification accuracy of 96.27% in the laboratory experiment. After sequence-specific calibration, it achieves Top-1 accuracies of 79.92% for task-relevant objects and 85.58% for the road class on LBW. MFGaze achieves the lowest mean angular errors among the compared methods, with 4.06$^\circ$, 4.23$^\circ$, and 10.52$^\circ$ on MPIIFaceGaze, ETH-XGaze, and Gaze360, respectively, and demonstrates strong cross-dataset generalization. [85] Suppressing the Shortcut Hidden Behind the Gains of VideoLLM Fine-Tuning cs.CVPDF

Youngwoo Shin, Jongsuk Kim, Junmo Kim

TL;DR: 论文研究 VideoLLM 监督微调中隐藏的文本捷径问题：模型会利用训练数据中的文本规律直接作答而绕过视频内容。作者提出 VETO，在微调更新层面校准基座模型中仅由文本占据的激活子空间，并将更新投影到其正交补空间，从而在捷径形成时就加以抑制。

Details

Motivation: VideoLLM 的 SFT 虽提升视频推理能力，但也学到可绕过视频的文本捷径；该捷径难以在数据层面定义，且不受训练轮数、数据规模和骨干模型影响，并会掩盖分布偏移下视频信息带来的真实收益。

Result: 在 7 个 VideoLLM 上，VETO 保持 SFT 在训练分布上的增益并抑制捷径；在 6 个分布偏移 benchmark 上取得高于 SFT 的表现，且模型更多依据完整输入而非捷径作答。

Insight: 创新点在于不依赖数据层面定义捷径，而是对微调更新进行正交投影干预，抑制文本专用子空间中的更新。可借鉴之处是将捷径治理从数据改造转向训练更新约束。

Abstract: Supervised fine-tuning (SFT) is the stage in which Video Large Language Models (VideoLLMs) advance their capability to reason over video. However, the VideoLLM also learns to answer from textual regularities of its training data, a shortcut that bypasses the video. The regularities behind the shortcut are hard to define at the data level, and the shortcut forms regardless of training epoch, data size, or backbone. The shortcut is hidden behind a rise in accuracy within the training distribution, yet it is not required for the rise and even costs SFT the gain the video offers under distribution shift. We therefore propose Video-Evidence Training via Orthogonal projection (VETO), which intervenes on the fine-tuning update where the shortcut is written rather than on the data. VETO calibrates the subspace that the text alone occupies in the activations of the base model and projects the update onto its orthogonal complement. The VideoLLM is thus left to ground its answer in the full input, with the shortcut suppressed as it forms and never defined. Across seven VideoLLMs, VETO preserves the gain of SFT on the training distribution and suppresses the shortcut. On six benchmarks under shift it gains more than SFT and answers from the full input rather than the shortcut. Code is available at https://github.com/Youngwoo-git/VETO.

Result: 在 CARLA 合成数据上达到 81.33 BEV AP@0.7，同输入预算下比 CenterPoint 高 13.76 点，比 CenterPoint 原生更密预算高 1.91 点；但验证集按帧而非场景划分，属分布内结果。零样本迁移到 nuScenes 真实关键帧时无模型成功迁移，仅保留 3.7%–12.2% 的 CARLA 精度，且 GeoPillars 是七个模型中最慢的。

Insight: 论文宣称的创新包括几何 pillar 特征、柱内分组向量注意力、BEV 绝对位置先验、大核骨干、表面法向辅助监督和全分辨率检测头；但逐级分解显示新组件整体解释力有限，提升检测头分辨率单独就恢复 13.76 点中的 10.19 点，其余模块联合仅加 3.37，法向监督仅加 0.20 且落在运行波动内，说明复杂模块的收益需要严格消融验证。

Abstract: GeoPillars is a pillar-based 3D detector for single-sweep LiDAR under a hard budget of eight points per 0.4 m pillar, a budget that discards 46.6% of in-range points. It adds geometric pillar features, grouped vector attention within each pillar, a learned absolute positional prior on the bird’s-eye-view (BEV) grid, a large-kernel backbone, auxiliary surface-normal supervision and a full-resolution detection head. Trained on 123k synthetic CARLA scans and evaluated on cars, it reaches 81.33 BEV AP@0.7, 13.76 points above CenterPoint at the same input budget and 1.91 points above CenterPoint at its native, denser budget. These are in-distribution figures, because the validation split is per-frame rather than scene-disjoint. A rung-by-rung decomposition shows that the new components explain little of the gain. Raising the detection-head resolution alone recovers 10.19 of the 13.76 points, the joint change of encoder, backbone, neck and positional prior adds 3.37, and the normal-supervision branch adds 0.20, which is within run-to-run variation. With heading reversals folded out, orientation error is similar for all seven models, although GeoPillars reverses headings about half as often as a resolution-matched baseline. It is also the slowest of the seven models. Evaluated zero-shot on 6,743 real nuScenes keyframes, no model transfers, and each retains 3.7% to 12.2% of its CARLA accuracy. A collection defect left LiDAR intensity constant, so every model learned from geometry alone. Code and checkpoints are publicly available.

Haonan Wang, Hanyu Zhou, Xiaobin Hu, Luxin Yan

TL;DR: 论文提出 World-delta，一个面向增量 3D 场景生成的框架，用于在新观测不断到来时持续扩展和修正 3D 世界。它将视觉证据编码为统一 world tokens，并通过观测校准的持久世界建模区分已观测支持内容与临时补全假设，再利用 delta 引导扩散仅更新受影响区域并集成新的 3D chunks。

Details

Motivation: 现有 3D 生成方法多基于固定输入，难以持续扩展和修订场景；迭代式方法常把生成内容当作既定结构，导致早期生成错误在新证据到来后仍持续存在并累积。

Result: 实验表明 World-delta 在增量 3D 场景生成、观测驱动校正、场景一致性和连续更新效率方面有效；摘要未给出具体 benchmark 数值或 SOTA 对比。

Insight: 创新点在于不确定性感知的持久世界建模与 delta 引导扩散相结合：选择性生成 world deltas 以修正冲突假设并保留可靠内容，同时用 delta 掩码限制扩散和仅解码受影响 3D 区域，从而减少冗余生成并保持场景一致性。

Abstract: Existing 3D generation methods synthesize visually compelling scenes from fixed inputs, yet struggle to continually expand and revise a 3D world as new observations arrive. Iterative approaches often fuse real observations and generated content into a single representation, treating generated content as established structure rather than revisable predictions. Consequently, early generation errors can persist and accumulate despite new evidence. We propose World-delta, an incremental 3D scene generation framework combining uncertainty-aware persistent world modeling with delta-guided diffusion for continual scene expansion and correction. Our framework comprises three components: 1) Visual Evidence Encoding integrates geometric and semantic cues from incoming images into unified world tokens. 2) Observation-Calibrated World Modeling maintains a persistent latent world state that distinguishes observation-supported content from provisional completion hypotheses. New evidence drives selective world deltas to revise conflicting hypotheses while preserving well-supported content. 3) Incremental World Diffusion Generation uses delta-derived masks to restrict diffusion to regions requiring updates, freezing unchanged regions. Only affected 3D chunks are decoded and integrated into the existing scene. Together, these components enable continual world expansion and revision while reducing redundant generation and preserving scene consistency. Experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of World-delta in incremental 3D scene generation, observation-driven correction, scene consistency, and update efficiency over successive scene updates.

Abstract: Preference training is the standard recipe for reducing object hallucination in vision-language models, and each new method reports a lower CHAIR score. We ask what that reduction consists of. Every trained system is compared with its base model’s caption truncated to the same object recall, which removes the part of the gain that comes from saying less; the remainder is compared with what a deliberately weak, frozen, eleven-feature post-hoc probe fitted on 500 annotated images can reproduce. All criteria were specified in the analysis code and frozen before the outputs they judge existed. On our own DPO checkpoints the accounting holds on Qwen2.5-VL but fails on InternVL2.5 on COCO (2 of 4 training designs). At matched recall, the base model plus the probe beats truncation for 8 of 9 LLaVA-1.5 systems on COCO and 9 of 9 on AMBER, on the two newer backbones, and when realized as actual sentence deletion. The same measure prices training signals: DPO on the model’s own samples labelled by the probe or by an object-level CLIP score recovers 0.70 and 0.87 of the beyond-brevity gain of ground-truth-labelled DPO on Qwen2.5-VL (the probe 0.73 on InternVL2.5), both beat length-matched random labels, and CLIP labels buy more than probe labels on COCO. Human judgements show that the sentences the probe deletes are wrong more often than those truncation would delete; a human check of the CHAIR evaluator’s precision falls below our pre-set bar, which we report as a limitation.

Abstract: Precise forest species mapping using spaceborne hyperspectral data is challenging due to high spectral similarity and the inherent sub-pixel heterogeneity of natural forest stands. While traditional deep learning models exploit spatial context through convolutional architectures, these approaches can introduce noise in fragmented or mixed-species forests. In this paper, we propose a hierarchy-aware, multimodal deep learning framework to resolve sub-pixel forest composition by integrating PRISMA (PRecursore IperSpettrale della Missione Applicativa) hyperspectral imagery, high-resolution panchromatic data, and digital elevation models. The proposed method consistently outperforms state-of-the-art multi-label and ranking-based paradigms, particularly in identifying subordinate species and their relative dominance. The bidirectional learning mechanism allows sub-pixel compositional features to refine macroscopic forest type predictions, while the integration of topographic priors effectively resolves spectral ambiguities in complex terrains. [90] Rank2Replace: VLM-Ranked Slot Replacement for Out-of-Distribution Generalization cs.CV | cs.LGPDF

Bo-Han Feng, Keng-Te Liao, Shou-De Lin

TL;DR: 论文提出 Rank2Replace，利用预训练视觉-语言模型（VLM）对图像的 object-centric slots 按任务相关性排序，并用另一张图像中同秩 slot 嵌入进行秩相关替换，作为训练时增强来提升小模型的分布外（OOD）泛化。该方法保留原图标签，促使预测器依赖被保留的任务相关证据，并可结合现有 OOD 方法。实验覆盖四个数据集和六个划分，默认交叉熵配置在无需环境标签和数据集特定替换调参下取得 macro 平均的 average-group 80.43% 与 worst-group 65.28%。

Details

Motivation: 图像预测器容易利用虚假相关获得高分布内精度，但在分布偏移下泛化差。论文希望将预训练 VLM 中任务相关证据的知识迁移给小模型，以提升 OOD 泛化。

Result: 默认配置达到 average-group 80.43%、worst-group 65.28%；slot corruption 对全部 14 种 OOD 训练方法的两项 macro 指标均有提升，结合 slot ranking 与 IRM 达到 81.96% 和 72.68%。消融支持排序与替换设计，并在 5 个排序 VLM 和 6 种 extractor-predictor 组合上有效。

Insight: 创新点在于用 VLM 的任务相关性排序指导同秩 slot 替换，保留标签做训练时增强，且无需环境标签或数据集特定替换调参。客观看，其可借鉴之处是将 VLM 作为训练期证据排序器，以槽级替换解耦任务证据与偶然线索，并保持推理高效。

Abstract: Spurious correlations can give image predictors high in-distribution accuracy but poor generalization under distribution shift. We introduce $\textbf{Rank2Replace}$, which transfers knowledge of task-relevant evidence from pretrained vision–language models (VLMs) to small predictors through training-time augmentation for out-of-distribution (OOD) generalization. The VLM ranks object-centric slots by task relevance and guides rank-dependent replacement with same-rank slot embeddings from another image. This augmentation retains the original image’s label, encouraging reliance on preserved task evidence as incidental cues change, and supports combination with existing OOD methods. Across four datasets and six splits under shared frozen slot embeddings, the default cross-entropy configuration reaches macro-averaged average-group and worst-group accuracies of $80.43%$ and $65.28%$ without environment labels or dataset-specific replacement tuning. Slot corruption improves both macro-averaged metrics for all fourteen OOD training methods. Combining it with slot ranking and IRM reaches $81.96%$ and $72.68%$. Controlled ablations support the ranking and replacement design, with gains across five ranking VLMs and six extractor–predictor combinations. Permutation-invariant predictors further improve both macro-averaged metrics over their unaugmented counterparts while using VLM guidance only during training for efficient inference.
