TL;DR: 论文提出 ContextRenderer，一个面向交互式游戏世界的自回归视频扩散渲染框架，用视频生成模型将引擎提供的场景表示渲染为细节观察。它结合动态上下文学习、循环历史压缩和跨表示流对齐，并构建 WorldPrism 数据集，以支持动态条件更新、跨代理泛化和长时间视觉连续性。

Details

Motivation: 持续游戏过程中，现有生成式渲染器难以在跟随不断变化的条件时，保持跨多样代理表示和目标风格的视觉连续性，因此需要兼顾动态条件与跨代理泛化的渲染方法。

Result: 在 GWM-Bench 和 Solar-Bench 上，论文方法在重建和视觉质量上取得提升；定性结果显示可在提示和主体参考变化下实现分钟级交互式渲染。

Insight: 创新点在于将分块级交互监督的动态上下文学习与有界 token 预算下的循环历史压缩结合，并通过跨表示流对齐共享高保真重建来连接代理重建与外观/交互变化，同时构建带分块标注的 WorldPrism 数据集。

Abstract: Interactive game world models separate executable world evolution from visual generation, using video models to render engine-provided scene representations into detailed observations. During sustained gameplay, however, existing generative renderers struggle to follow changing conditions while preserving visual continuity across diverse proxies and target styles. We therefore introduce ContextRenderer, an autoregressive video diffusion framework designed for both dynamic conditioning and cross-proxy generalization. It couples Dynamic-Context Learning (DCL) for chunk-level interaction supervision with Recurrent History Compression (RHC) to follow condition updates while preserving visual continuity within a bounded token budget. Across proxy representations, Cross-Representation Flow Alignment (CRFA) connects proxy reconstruction with appearance and interaction variation through a shared high-fidelity reconstruction. WorldPrism, our interaction-centric dataset constructed from agent-built worlds, AAA gameplay, and open-source videos, supplies the corresponding renderings and chunk-level annotations for these learning tasks. Experiments on GWM-Bench and Solar-Bench show improved reconstruction and visual quality, while qualitative results demonstrate minute-long interactive rendering under changing prompts and subject references. Project page: https://rainzor.github.io/contextrenderer/

[92] Harnessing Medical Visual-Language Models with Agentic Evidence Management cs.CV | cs.AIPDF

Qiming Huang, Hanze Dong, Jipeng Zhang, Mike Zheng Shou, Xinxing Xu

TL;DR: 论文构建了Mask-Evidence MedVQA基准，用于将医疗视觉语言智能体对分割证据的使用与分割质量本身分离开来，并发现即使使用ground-truth mask，证据的呈现方式也会显著影响模型行为，且mask会带来证据权衡。为此，论文提出TUNE证据管理框架，显式管理获取何种证据、如何呈现证据以及是否接受答案更新，在多个医疗VQA基准上取得提升。

Details

Motivation: 现有医疗视觉语言智能体工作多把获得正确的分割mask当作工具使用的终点，但缺乏能分离证据使用与分割质量、覆盖多种医学图像类型、并支持同一mask多种呈现方式的基准，因此无法回答智能体是否有效利用了分割证据。

Result: 在三个公开医疗VQA基准上，证据管理使微调的Qwen3.5-9B骨干平均准确率提升4.0个百分点；完整TUNE在七个基准上平均比直接工具使用提升9.1个百分点，并与现有医疗VLM和智能体相比具有竞争力。

Insight: 创新点在于构建了可分离证据使用与分割质量的医学VQA基准，揭示了证据呈现敏感性和ground-truth mask的双向证据权衡，并提出显式管理证据获取、呈现与答案更新决策的TUNE框架。

Abstract: Medical vision-language agents increasingly rely on segmentation tools, and recent work has focused on acquiring the right mask, often treating a good mask as the end point of tool use. But do these agents use the resulting evidence effectively? To answer this question, we need a benchmark that separates evidence use from segmentation quality, spans multiple medical image types, and allows the same mask to be presented in different ways. Existing medical VQA datasets do not jointly provide these properties. We therefore construct Mask-Evidence MedVQA, a 2.5K-example benchmark spanning four image types, where each question is paired with an answer-relevant ground-truth mask and multiple possible renderings. Our study reveals two limitations of direct tool use. First, evidence presentation matters. Different renderings of the same fixed mask can substantially change model behavior. Second, masks introduce an evidence trade-off. Highlighting a relevant region can expose diagnostic evidence the model previously missed, but the added visual cue can also induce incorrect associations. As a result, even a ground-truth mask can correct some answers while turning initially correct ones into incorrect ones. Together, these findings show that acquiring the right mask is not enough. An agent must also decide how to present the evidence and whether it should change the answer. We therefore introduce TUNE (Tool Use is Not Enough), an evidence-management harness that explicitly manages what evidence to acquire, how to present it, and whether to accept the resulting answer update. Across three public medical VQA benchmarks, evidence management improves the fine-tuned Qwen3.5-9B backbone’s mean accuracy by 4.0 percentage points. Full TUNE improves over direct tool use by 9.1 points on average across seven benchmarks and achieves competitive results against existing medical VLMs and agents.

[93] TrajCal: Trajectory-Calibrated Time Schedules for Few-Step Flow Sampling cs.CVPDF

Junchi Zhang, Zihan Gu, Jiangcan Jia, Lulu Zhao, Wenbo Su

TL;DR: 论文提出 TrajCal，一种免训练的少步流匹配采样校准框架，通过散射搜索优化少步采样时间调度，使少步生成结果更接近多步参考。方法从多样初始调度出发，经变形与组合后按最终输出保真度选择一个固定调度，并复用于新输入。实验覆盖图像和视频生成。

Details

Motivation: 流匹配生成质量高，但迭代采样需反复评估大网络；现有少步方法会扭曲输出并降低与多步参考的一致性，因此需要在不训练模型的情况下校准少步采样调度。

Result: 在图像和视频生成中，TrajCal 在低评估预算下参考保真度优于所有对比基线，同时保持有竞争力的视觉质量；即使随机种子也有效，轨迹种子可进一步提升保真度，摄影校准的调度还能泛化到未见图像风格。

Insight: 创新点在于将少步采样时间调度校准建模为免训练搜索问题，利用散射搜索离线寻找可复用的固定调度，并引入轨迹种子与跨风格泛化分析。客观上看，其价值主要是无需训练即可改善少步采样保真度并降低逐输入调参成本，但依赖参考输出和搜索评估开销。

Abstract: Flow matching enables high-quality image and video generation, but its iterative sampling process requires repeated evaluations of large neural networks. Existing few-step methods can distort the output and reduce its agreement with a many-step reference. We propose TrajCal, a training-free calibration framework for few-step sampling. Based on scatter search, a well-established metaheuristic for difficult optimization problems, TrajCal starts with diverse seed schedules, deforms and combines these candidates, and selects one fixed schedule by final output fidelity for reuse on new inputs. For both image and video generation, TrajCal achieves higher reference fidelity than all compared baselines at low evaluation budgets while maintaining competitive visual quality. The search is effective even with randomly generated seeds, while trajectory-based seeds further improve fidelity. For cross-style generalization, photography-calibrated schedules also improve fidelity on unseen image styles.

Details

Motivation: 现有长视频基准主要关注事件、物体类别或相对位置，缺乏对多模态模型在实例级空间记忆方面的系统测试，尤其是跨天、跨人物回忆物体最后出现位置及目击者。

Abstract: Recalling where and when an object was last seen and who saw it requires recognizing it across days and people. Existing long-video benchmarks focus on events, object categories, or relative locations, leaving instance-level spatial memory largely untested in multimodal models. To address this gap, we present EgoWitness, a benchmark for instance-level spatial memory and reasoning in egocentric video. Its 8,453 human-verified questions span approximately 324 hours of week-long and short-horizon recordings and test seven abilities, from recognizing an object or room to recalling who saw it and reasoning about room visits. Over one million reviewed masks of 34 registered landmarks and 21 movable-object labels are linked through room timelines. Among twelve direct vision-language models (VLMs), open-weight models reach at most 46.97% accuracy, and none exceeds 35% on identifying who saw an object last. We therefore propose EgoClue, a spatial-memory agent that retrieves candidate intervals, verifies instance identity, and computes spatial and temporal relations with five tools. Two-stage training with supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) raises its 4B controller from 23.64% to 72.65% accuracy on 1,320 held-out questions. Evidence-trace synthesis matches the best direct VLM at 74.85% on week-long questions and achieves 80.56% in environments excluded from training.

Details

Motivation: 现有长视频记忆增强检索主要依赖全局语义匹配，缺乏状态驱动的时间控制，容易返回碎片化或冗余证据，难以维持连贯的证据链。论文旨在解决长视频问答中检索目标不清、记忆访问路径不当和过早停止等过程性错误。

Result: 在四个长视频问答基准上，EvoMem 显著优于现有基线，平均准确率达到 60.1%，相较此前最佳方法平均提升 8.6 个百分点。

Insight: 创新点在于将分层时间索引记忆、检索时间线索显式约束与过程级检索技能结合，使智能体可根据当前证据状态动态调整检索和停止决策。进一步将技能进化建模为奖励引导的策略改进，以外部策略补丁方式接受或拒绝技能而无需更新模型参数，兼具时间感知与自进化能力。

Abstract: Although memory-augmented architectures have expanded the ability of large vision-language models (LVLMs) to process long videos, existing retrieval processes still largely rely on global semantic matching and lack state-driven temporal control, often returning fragmented or redundant evidence and failing to maintain a coherent evidence chain. To address this challenge, we propose EvoMem, a time-aware long-video memory agent guided by self-evolving retrieval skills. EvoMem constructs a hierarchical time-indexed memory, where different types of evidence are aligned onto a unified video timeline; during retrieval, temporal cues from acquired evidence are further converted into explicit signals that constrain subsequent memory access. To address recurring procedural errors in long-video question answering, such as unclear retrieval targets, improper memory access paths, or premature stopping, EvoMem introduces retrieval skills to provide process-level guidance, enabling the agent to dynamically adjust retrieval content, memory operations, and target temporal ranges according to the current evidence state, while deciding when to stop. Furthermore, inspired by reinforcement learning, we formulate skill evolution as a reward-guided policy improvement process. Each candidate skill is treated as an external policy patch, and rewards are assigned according to answer-correctness state transitions on the validation set. In this way, effective skills are accepted and low-quality skills are rejected without updating model parameters. Experiments on four long-video question answering benchmarks show that EvoMem significantly outperforms existing baselines, achieving an average accuracy of 60.1% and an average improvement of 8.6 percentage points over the previous best methods, demonstrating its effectiveness for long-video reasoning.

[98] SpatialMem: Building Quantitative Spatial Reasoning Agents with Hierarchical Memory cs.CVPDF

Zinan Zheng, Nuo Chen, Jia Li

TL;DR: 论文提出 SpatialMem，一种面向视觉语言模型智能体的分层记忆框架，通过任务记忆记录工具编排、场景记忆记录工具选择，使智能体能够积累并复用视觉工具使用经验，从而完成定量空间推理。论文还构建了覆盖室内外场景和三类定量空间推理任务的 MetricBench 基准。

Details

Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型在定性空间推理上表现较好，但定量空间推理仍受限；依赖外部视觉工具的方法往往将功能需求绑定到单一预定义视觉专家，导致工具流水线固定僵化，难以在多样化空间场景和任务中实现鲁棒推理。

Result: SpatialMem 在 MetricBench、Q-Spatial 和 SpatilRGPT-Bench 上取得 state-of-the-art 结果，在 MetricBench 上比最佳 VLM 基线高 32%；其记忆设计相比无记忆智能体在 MetricBench 上带来 9.1% 的绝对提升。

Insight: 创新点在于用两级记忆分别建模工具编排和工具选择，并通过双状态轨迹优化保留成功经验、改进次优轨迹；同时提出覆盖更广场景和任务的 MetricBench。客观看，其价值在于将记忆机制引入视觉工具编排，缓解组合执行空间下最优流水线选择困难的问题。

Abstract: Vision Language Models (VLMs) have shown remarkable capabilities in qualitative spatial reasoning, but remain limited in quantitative spatial reasoning. Current work leverages the VLM agent to invoke external vision tools to perform computation. However, these methods usually bind each functional requirement to a single predefined vision specialist, implicitly resulting in fixed and rigid tool pipelines. In practice, addressing diverse spatial scenarios and tasks demands a broader diversity in pipeline orchestration and vision specialist selection to achieve robust spatial reasoning. However, such diversity leads to a combinatorial execution space, making it challenging for the agent to orchestrate the optimal pipelines and specialists for different scenarios. In this work, we propose SpatialMem, a memory framework that enables a VLM agent to accumulate tool-use experience and leverage that memory to orchestrate vision specialists to solve the spatial reasoning problem. SpatialMem builds a two-level memory structure, with task memory capturing tool orchestration and scene memory capturing tool selection. It further introduces a dual-state trajectory optimization process to preserve successful tool experiences and refine suboptimal ones. Moreover, existing spatial reasoning benchmarks suffer from quality issues and limited coverage of scene types and tasks. To address these gap, we propose MetricBench, a benchmark covering both indoor and outdoor scenes and three quantitative spatial reasoning tasks. Our method achieves state-of-the-art results on MetricBench, Q-Spatial, and SpatilRGPT-Bench, outperforming best VLM baselines by 32% on MetricBench. Additionally, the memory design yields a 9.1% absolute gain over a memory-free agent on MetricBench.

Abstract: Robotic world models predict future video frames from the current observation and a sequence of robot actions. While recent models follow the given actions closely, manipulated objects often collapse or move inconsistently with the action. We therefore separate dynamics prediction from visual rendering, with 3D point dynamics as the proxy between them. This suggests that advances in dynamics modeling can translate into more faithful robotic world models.

Abstract: Reconstructing interactive physical twins from video requires accurately capturing how objects deform while maintaining efficient simulation. In spring-based twins, coupling between volume and shape responses creates such a limitation. We present MorphTwin, which enriches local deformation modes to capture and independently control these responses while retaining standard spring forces and explicit simulation. To support interpretable material editing and transfer, we use offline calibration to map shared relative response changes to stiffness updates on each reconstructed graph. Abstract: Editing a 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) capture of a person so that they wear a new garment requires every rendered view to agree on the same garment details. Existing 3D virtual try-on (VTON) pipelines, however, edit training views largely independently and fuse them with hand-designed heuristics such as manually chosen reference views and fixed attention-blending weights. The resulting inconsistencies are baked into the 3D representation and surface as texture drift, blurred prints, and implausible geometry in novel views. We present GS-VTON-MVT, a three-stage framework that combines implicit and explicit 3D reasoning for consistent 3DGS try-on. (i) A multi-view inpainting transformer jointly edits a set of training views with full cross-view self-attention, so that cross-view correspondence is learned rather than imposed. (ii) A geometry-guided multi-modal ControlNet conditions the diffusion editor on the garment image together with depth and surface normals rendered from the current 3DGS state, supplying explicit structure where visual evidence is weakest. (iii) A pose-adaptive persona-aware attention replaces the fixed blending weight between current-view and reference-view features with weights derived from camera-pose proximity. On the 3D-VTONBench benchmark, GS-VTON-MVT improves FID, KID, LPIPS-based cross-view consistency, and user preference over general-purpose 3D editors and prior 3D try-on methods.

Abstract: Text-to-video generation has made remarkable progress with diffusion models and transformer-based video architectures, yet generating coherent long videos from evolving textual descriptions remains challenging. Training long-video models from scratch requires substantial computation and large-scale multi-event data, while training-free multi-prompt generation must activate new prompt semantics without breaking temporal continuity. We identify a frequency-agnostic boundary coupling problem in existing transition strategies: weak coupling causes abrupt changes, flicker, and background jumps, whereas overly strong or coarse coupling may over-preserve source semantics and produce ghosting or duplicated subjects around prompt boundaries. To address this problem, we propose FreqSync, a training-free framework that formulates prompt transitions as frequency-selective boundary coupling. Abstract: Unified multimodal models aim to support visual understanding and generation within a single framework. Abstract: Video-language models (VLMs) achieve strong performance on video-text retrieval and are increasingly used for egocentric video understanding. Yet, despite gains in zero-shot retrieval, they have shown limitations in compositional understanding, and it remains unclear whether they understand compositional relationships between verbs and nouns. In this work, we introduce Ego-ExBind, a compositional diagnostic protocol for analyzing pretraining exposure and verb-noun binding in egocentric video-text retrieval. We further probe noun and verb binding channels through controlled noun-swap and verb-swap negatives. We find that zero-shot retrieval is positively associated with pair exposure within seen compositions, even after controlling for the marginal frequencies of verbs and nouns. However, higher pair exposure is associated with larger noun-swap margins and slightly smaller verb-swap margins. This asymmetry motivates us to test whether exposure reweighting can improve retrieval with little change in the measured swap margins. To isolate the effect of exposure input, we keep the trained checkpoint fixed and replace only the exposure input at inference. We demonstrate that this exposure reweighting can improve video-text retrieval without stronger verb-noun compositional understanding, and that the gain depends on the correct sample-level exposure correspondence. By separating exposure-driven retrieval gains from verb-noun binding, Ego-ExBind provides a diagnostic framework for evaluating whether future improvements in egocentric MIR reflect compositional understanding rather than pretraining familiarity.

Result: 在水稻上，AgroWM的目标阶段匹配率（Stage R@1）为54.6%，高于GPT Image 2.5的34.8%、Qwen Image 2.1的24.0%以及最强领域基线（同骨干但不使用Growth Scripts）的47.4%；在提前9至20周的预测中仍达46.4%，该基线为35.7%。在玉米上，同一设计在全部四项指标上同样最佳。

Insight: 创新点在于将农业生长预测形式化为以单张照片为条件的长期发育世界建模，并采用“先推理发育状态、再生成图像”的解耦框架：PhenoClock输出可解释的数值化Growth Script，Rooted Renderer基于原图进行器官和颜色编辑而非自由生成；同时提出用植株后续真实照片检查性状和发育时序，避免像素分数偏好不变副本的问题。

Abstract: World models can imagine how a physical scene evolves over the next few seconds, but growers need to know how a crop will develop over weeks to months: will this rice plant have headed a month from now, and how will it look at harvest? We introduce agricultural world modeling, the task of predicting how the same plant will look at a requested horizon of days to months, from one photo of it, its cultivar, and its camera view. In preliminary analysis, even state-of-the-art image generators get this wrong: they often grow the plant too little or give it the color and form of the wrong growth stage. We argue that they do not work out the plant’s developmental state, which the photo reveals only indirectly. We therefore propose AgroWM, which reasons about development before it draws growth. Its reasoning module, PhenoClock, works out the current growth stage, predicts the future one, and writes a Growth Script with numeric targets for height, plant area, and color; its generation module, the Rooted Renderer, then edits the photo to follow this script, adding new organs and colors to the photographed plant. Because pixel scores can prefer an unchanged copy of the photo, we instead check each forecast’s traits and developmental timing against the plant’s own later photos. On rice, 54.6% of the forecasts of AgroWM are matched to the target stage (Stage R@1), against 34.8% for GPT Image 2.5, 24.0% for Qwen Image 2.1, and 47.4% for the strongest domain-specific baseline, the same backbone fine-tuned without Growth Scripts; 9 to 20 weeks ahead, it still reaches 46.4%, against 35.7% for that baseline. These features are then injected into a video diffusion model via dedicated conditioning pathways, ensuring precise attribute allocation. Abstract: Real-time streaming video generation has gained rapid momentum in live content creation and interactive media. Deploying such systems in practice requires flexible scaling factors to accommodate diverse source resolutions, target displays, and computational budgets. However, extending fixed-scale generative video super-resolution (VSR) to varying target scales poses two challenges: downsampling from a larger fixed scale incurs redundant cost, while re-optimizing the diffusion transformer (DiT) is costly and alters the shared generative prior. To address both, we propose FlexVSR, a decoupled framework for continuous-scale streaming generative VSR with supervision at only a few scales. Our key insight is that naively resampling fixed-scale latents onto a new target grid induces scale-dependent input mismatch, which can be effectively addressed through parameter-efficient, scale-aware adaptation of the latent interface without updating the DiT. To this end, FlexVSR introduces ReLSA (Refined Latent Scale Adaptation), a lightweight latent adapter that constructs and refines target-grid latents before they enter the DiT, enabling a shared frozen VSR prior to generalize across continuous scales with only 0.274M trainable parameters and an adaptation cost of 7.3 H100 GPU-hours, while introducing no additional temporal dependencies and preserving the native causal streaming protocol. Motivation: 现有图像恢复方法采用全局均匀操作，忽略图像不同区域的退化差异，且未考虑连续恢复动作之间的相互影响，可能导致恢复不完整或引入伪影。

Abstract: Images under non-uniform coupled degradations require diverse restoration capabilities for different degraded regions. Existing methods employ globally uniform operations, potentially overlooking regional differences and causing incomplete restoration or even damage to image content. Moreover, a degraded region may require successive restoration actions, and earlier actions may alter residual degradation characteristics or introduce artifacts, potentially hindering subsequent restoration. To address these challenges, we propose RegionAgent, an adaptive restoration framework that trains a Vision-Language Model (VLM) through two-stage preference learning to generate a region-specific restoration action at each iteration. Specifically, we first train the VLM to select an action suited to the current region through step-level Direct Preference Optimization (DPO), using preferences derived from immediate restoration feedback to supervise only the differing component in each action pair. To account for the effects of current actions on subsequent restoration, we then apply trajectory-level DPO, using preferences derived from complete trajectory scores to supervise the sole differing action in each trajectory pair. Furthermore, as conventional image-pair datasets do not directly provide preferences over regional restoration decisions, we construct a regional restoration preference dataset comprising 10K action pairs and 20K trajectory pairs for two-stage VLM training. Abstract: Thinking with images (TwI) empowers vision-language models (VLMs) to overcome perceptual bottlenecks by actively cropping and zooming into fine-grained visual details, promising both higher accuracy and interpretable, region-grounded reasoning. However, our analysis shows that the improved accuracy of TwI models does not guarantee faithful tool use; they frequently invoke tools without actually relying on the zoomed region, undermining the reliability of tool-assisted reasoning. We trace this unfaithful behaviour to the training data: when questions are answerable without zooming, the model can answer correctly while ignoring the returned evidence, and this problem is exacerbated during training as the model becomes better at answering without tools. To keep zooming useful throughout training, we adapt the training data to the model’s evolving capability and train on questions where correct zoomed evidence can still help the model answer correctly. To this end, we present MixReact, the first unified framework for physics-based reactive humanoid control from text, audio, and video cues, integrating a Discrete–Continuous Mixture of Transformers (DisCo-MoT) for state prediction with a History-aware Action Transformer (HAT) for physical execution. DisCo-MoT couples a discrete masked-modeling branch with a continuous autoregressive diffusion branch via one-way mixture attention within a MoT architecture. Guidance from discrete context mitigates collapse into averaged behaviors in one-to-many reaction generation, while direct prediction in continuous state space bypasses the decoding bottleneck of discrete representations. HAT then maps the predicted next state and observed execution history to executable actions, enabling robust closed-loop control in physics-based simulation. Experiments across all three modalities demonstrate consistent improvements over the baselines in fidelity, diversity, and physical consistency, while ablations validate the complementary contributions of the components. Our code will be released to facilitate future research. Project page: https://binlee26.github.io/MixReact_Page/

Abstract: Short-video platforms rank new uploads before anyone has watched them, using content towers that read a video’s cover, title and frames. Existing attacks on content-aware recommenders perturb pixels or features and ignore platform re-encoding, and existing defenses train against norm-bounded perturbations that neither match real edits nor preserve recommendation quality. We then observe that cold-start ranking does not need the packaging at all: a tower that sees only the video body ranks cold items best. Details

Motivation: Agentic 视觉推理虽可通过选择性放大证据提升 VLMs，但受限于三个瓶颈：高频细节在视觉 token 形成中衰减、空间分散证据难以被语言模型检索、孤立裁剪因尺度/位置不匹配削弱全局-局部对应关系。

Result: 在 4 个前沿 VLM 和覆盖 6 类视觉推理任务的 29 个 benchmark 上评估，完整 ResVision（Agentic Base+SMR+CPI）对所有模型均最优，较 Direct Base 提升 3.364.94 分，较 Agentic Base 提升 1.872.94 分。

Insight: 创新在于将自适应视觉采样与上下文保持的局部检查结合：SMR 按图像复杂度/频谱匹配分配视觉 token，避免简单图像过度放大；CPI 在工具多轮中同时返回裁剪与完整视图，缓解尺度位置失配并保持局部细节与全局上下文。

Abstract: Agentic visual reasoning improves vision-language models by selectively enlarging evidence without adding new scene content, but the benefit depends on whether fine structure remains accessible and whether local observations stay grounded in the full scene. We identify three recurring bottlenecks: high-frequency detail can be attenuated during visual-token formation; spatially distributed evidence can remain encoded yet difficult for the language model to retrieve; and isolated crops can weaken whole-detail correspondence through scale and positional mismatch. ResVision addresses these limitations with two complementary components. Spectrum-Matched Resizing (SMR) estimates image complexity and selects an adaptive global sampling scale, avoiding unnecessary enlargement on simple inputs while allocating more visual tokens to detail-rich images. Context-Preserving Inspection (CPI) returns each inspected crop together with the complete working view, preserving local detail and global context across tool turns. We evaluate Direct and Agentic variants on four frontier VLMs and 29 benchmarks spanning six visual-reasoning families. Complete ResVision (Agentic Base plus SMR plus CPI) is best for every model, improving by 3.36 to 4.94 points over Direct Base and 1.87 to 2.94 points over Agentic Base. These results show that adaptive visual sampling and context-preserving inspection address distinct, complementary constraints in multimodal reasoning.

[121] Online Temporal Rotations for 2 Bit KV-Cache Quantization in Video Diffusion Transformers cs.CV | cs.AIPDF

Ryan Lucas, Petros Prastakos, Rahul Mazumder

TL;DR: 论文针对视频扩散Transformer在长视频生成中KV-Cache随去噪步复用而导致的显存瓶颈，提出时序旋转量化（TRQ）：在量化前对KV-Cache中时间上相邻的帧块施加在线拟合的正交变换，将各通道均值映射为跨通道近似恒定的向量以降低量化误差。该旋转变换用一棵2×2平面旋转树参数化（每个仅存2 bit），后接固定Hadamard变换，无需梯度、不改模型权重，并配有融合Triton核以便推理高效。

Details

Motivation: 长视频生成中KV-Cache复用使其成为显存与带宽瓶颈，而直接对时间上相距较远的缓存向量做低比特量化会因数值范围大而产生高量化误差；作者发现时间邻近的token在KV-Cache中具有稳定的逐通道模式，但该模式因视频而异，因此需要按每个视频的缓存自适应地做旋转变换。

Result: 在2-bit精度与相同压缩率下，TRQ在VBench主体一致性上优于SOTA方法QVG：LongCat-Video-13B从90.38提升至92.02，HY-WorldPlay-8B从86.33提升至87.61，其他指标亦有改善。

Insight: 创新点在于利用KV-Cache的时序局部性与逐通道持续性来设计在线、免梯度的旋转量化：将旋转参数化为极轻量的2×2平面旋转树（每旋转2 bit）叠加共享Hadamard变换，并证明对’均值展平’目标的简单贪心拟合即为全局最优；同时提供融合Triton核保证推理效率，思路可迁移到其他需缓存量化的生成模型。

Abstract: Video diffusion transformers reuse key–value (KV) caches across denoising steps, which quickly becomes a bottleneck for long video generation. We find that quantizing temporally distant cache vectors leads to a large range and high quantization error, but that nearby video tokens in the KV-cache share a persistent pattern per channel. These patterns also vary across videos, motivating rotations that are adapted to each video’s unique cache. We propose Temporal Rotation Quantization (TRQ), a method that rotates frames in the cache that are adjacent in time before quantization. For each block of temporally adjacent cache vectors, TRQ fits an orthogonal transform that maps the block’s per-channel means to a vector that is close to a constant across channels. To keep the quantization format lightweight, we parameterize this rotation as a tree of $2 \times 2$ planar rotations, each stored with only two bits, followed by a fixed Hadamard transform shared across all blocks. The rotations are fit without gradients or changes to model weights, and we prove that a simple greedy fit is globally optimal for our ‘mean-flattening’ objective. The transforms are fit online for each new video, and we release fused Triton kernels to make this process efficient at inference. At 2-bit precision and matched compression, TRQ improves VBench subject consistency over the state-of-the-art method QVG from 90.38 to 92.02 on LongCat-Video-13B and from 86.33 to 87.61 on HY-WorldPlay-8B, as well as improving other metrics.

[122] OffBeat: Multicoset Sampling for 4D Capture Beyond the Camera Frame Rate cs.CV | eess.IVPDF

TL;DR: 论文提出OffBeat，将交错触发的多相机阵列建模为空间分布式multicoset采样器，仅根据标定推导同时触发的相机组及其触发顺序，从而在超过相机帧率的条件下进行4D重建。方法结合Spacetime Gaussians，并分析短寿命primitive需持续至少一个相机帧间隔才能避免各组权重不均，进而用闭式平滑box约束减少新视角闪烁。实验在N3DV模拟交错采集和PanopticSports上验证了动态区域PSNR提升。

Abstract: For 4D reconstruction, a synchronized multi-camera rig records all views at the same timestamps and misses the motion between them. Staggered triggers spread the same frames over more timestamps. However, when the cameras along each row fire one after another, as in 4DSloMo’s schedule on the N3DV rig, the row acts as one moving camera that confounds depth with motion. We introduce OffBeat, which models a staggered rig as a spatially distributed multicoset sampler: shifted copies of one sampling grid. From the calibration alone, OffBeat derives camera groups that fire together and their firing order. Since one group records each timestamp, a short-lived primitive can fit an error its cameras share, such as a color offset, and flicker in novel views. We show that a primitive’s opacity over time weights all groups equally, for every number of groups, only if it lasts at least one camera frame interval. On Spacetime Gaussians, a minimum lifetime approximates this bound, and a closed-form smoothed box of that length satisfies it exactly, with less flicker than a Gaussian in its place at nearly equal PSNR. In simulated staggered capture on N3DV, OffBeat outperforms the full 4DSloMo pipeline with video diffusion on every test frame, by 3.04 dB in mean dynamic-region PSNR. Between the first and last synchronized frame, it outperforms the best synchronized method of each scene, with or without frame interpolation, by 1.98 dB in mean dynamic-region PSNR, while synchronized capture is better at its own frames. On PanopticSports, in this frame range, OffBeat is comparable in PSNR to synchronized capture with interpolation. On every N3DV scene, replacing 4DSloMo’s schedule with ours raises the dynamic-region PSNR of Spacetime Gaussians, 4DSloMo without diffusion, and our reconstruction. Multi-camera rigs could follow faster motion at the same frame count by changing only when each camera fires.

Abstract: Egocentric video captioning requires fine-grained detail and accurate timing, yet current multimodal large language models (MLLMs) often miss brief actions, confuse their order, or misinterpret the wearer’s motion. Existing caption formats also offer limited support for combining detailed narration with precise timing. We present EgoCaptioner, which pairs a video-level overview of the setting, participants, and the wearer’s overall activity with timed steps whose narration adapts to varying perceptual demands and marks key moments at specific timestamps. We develop a coarse-to-fine annotation pipeline that establishes event structure before expanding step descriptions, yielding 50,000 training examples from eight egocentric video sources. Complementary rewards assess factual accuracy and coverage, measure step-level temporal alignment, and penalize fragmented or redundant narration, guiding caption refinement through Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO). To improve the reliability of factual supervision, we supplement caption references with pose-derived motion facts when sensor data are available. Across five egocentric benchmarks, EgoCaptioner achieves leading performance among evaluated open-source models in caption quality, caption-conditioned question answering, and temporal alignment. It also surpasses Gemini 3.1 Pro on PhysCapBench and all evaluated proprietary baselines on Minerva-Ego under the caption-to-QA protocol. Abstract: At large events, pedestrian tracking supports entrance and passageway counts, but totals hide which crossings were missed. In a stadium study, the system recorded about four in five clearly visible crossings but fewer than one in three mostly obscured crossings. The videos lacked trajectories annotated across frames, so we turned to MOT20, which provides pedestrian trajectories and visibility. We introduce GateTrace to locate missed crossings and assess changes to counting rules. It derives reference crossings from annotations under fixed rules, matches reports by person, direction, time and position, and traces misses through saved boxes and counting decisions. With a shared detector, ByteTrack, OC-SORT and BoT-SORT recover 38.9-46.7% of low-visibility reference crossings on development sequences, versus 98.1-99.0% at high visibility. The gap recurs on two further sequences under a protocol fixed in advance. Published outputs of MOT20-tuned BASE recover 63-78% at low visibility across both sequence groups. Losses stop at different stages across systems. Across four systems and both groups, 47-79% of low-visibility misses have no nearby output linked to the person or no possible crossing in the saved tracks. Removing later checks from BASE on development sequences leaves most misses unresolved and lowers precision against the fixed reference list from about 96% to 77%. Details

Motivation: 现有视频生成模型即使使用大规模推理数据或扩散强化学习训练，仍常违反物理与逻辑约束，在多步推理和规划中失败。作者将其归因于传统视频训练把解视为固定长度片段，而非一连串决策。

Abstract: Current leading video generation models can synthesize highly realistic videos, while still frequently violating physical and logical constraints in multi-step reasoning and planning, even after training with large-scale reasoning data or existing diffusion RL methods. We attribute these failures to the conventional video training paradigm, which treats a solution as a fixed-length clip rather than a chain of decisions. We present ViDecide, a decision-centric framework that trains video diffusion models to predict state transitions causally and decide when to stop, with a decision-focused training schedule. We further propose Corrective Fine-tuning (CFT), which corrects failed decisions by mapping their noise to the model’s own successful continuations, achieving stable improvement without policy-gradient optimization. Across abstract games, planning tasks, and a new benchmark of multi-step robot planning, ViDecide achieves an average strict success rate of 83.8%, far above 23.3% for standard fine-tuning and 67.8% for the strongest prior method, and generalizes to larger puzzles and unseen layouts. Abstract: It has been demonstrated that, in certain settings, large language models can perform weight-space programming to directly modify the weights of neural networks. TL;DR: 论文提出 FFGaussType，将挤出的字形和 SVG 轮廓在一次前向传播中转换为紧凑的高斯资产。方法用点编码器把表面锚点映射到统一特征平面，再由共享解码器预测带法向的神经高斯，从而避免对每个字形/图标单独迭代优化。构建了包含 27,221 个字形和 SVG 图标的数据集，并验证了转换速度、紧凑性与重建保真度。

Details

Motivation: 沉浸式 3D 内容需要将字幕、注释和图标等文字图形放入场景中并遵循遮挡、透视、材质和光照，但在高斯场景中每个新字形或图标都需单独迭代优化，难以规模化创建内容。

Result: 在未见字体族和测试图标上达到 1.62° 法向误差和 0.985 轮廓 IoU，在对比的前馈转换器中最佳，并接近需数千步逐资产拟合的保真度；每个资产从准备好的锚点转换耗时 9.1 ms，计算和内存开销远低于先前基线。

Insight: 创新点在于用锚点到单特征平面的编码和跨资产共享解码器实现字形/SVG 到高斯的单次前向转换，并预测带法向的神经高斯；同时将几何转换与材质光照选择解耦，支持通过图像/视频编码进行紧凑传输，并配套构建了大规模配对数据集。

Abstract: Immersive 3D content allows text and graphical elements, such as subtitles, annotations, and icons, to be placed inside a scene rather than displayed as flat overlays. Placed this way, they respect occlusion and perspective and take on user-assigned materials and lighting while preserving their original shapes. In Gaussian scenes, however, each new glyph or icon requires its own iterative optimization, which limits scalable content creation. We present FFGaussType, which converts extruded glyph and SVG outlines into Gaussian assets in a single forward pass. A point encoder maps anchors sampled on the known surface to a single feature plane, from which a decoder shared by all assets predicts neural Gaussians carrying surface normals. This separates geometric conversion from material and lighting choices, and the feature plane supports image and video coding for compact delivery. We construct a paired dataset of 27,221 glyph and SVG icon assets with anchors and calibrated normal and silhouette renderings, holding out font families for evaluation. On unseen font families and test icons, FFGaussType achieves $1.62^\circ$ normal error and $0.985$ silhouette IoU, the best among the compared feed-forward converters, matching the fidelity that per-asset fitting needs thousands of steps to reach. The code and dataset will be released after publication.

Insight: 创新点在于将人类意图视为观察与动作之间的中间可观测状态，并用注视、表面肌电和脑电三层代理数据规模化记录意图通路。客观来看，其价值主要在于多层级、多模态同步的人类操作数据构建，以及通过时钟与校准数据支持不同读取速率重对齐，为意图建模提供数据基础。

Abstract: First-person manipulation datasets treat the observation-action pair as the atomic unit and compress the generation and execution of an action into a policy network’s weights, rendering the intent state behind the action unobservable. Human motor control research shows that intent mediates between observation and action and leaves observable traces along the intent pathway from decision to effect: gaze reaches the target before the hand, muscle load builds before contact, and cortical activity precedes the motor command. To record this pathway at scale, we present HumanCOIN, a first-person manipulation dataset built around human intent: 12,500 hours across 38 scene classes with three tiers of proxies. At the output end, the L1 attention tier (10,000 h) infers gaze from headband camera video, exposing the operator’s next object of attention and reach before the hand moves. Midway, the L2 exertion tier (2,000 h) records surface EMG from armbands, making load expectations readable before execution is complete. At the origin, the L3 cognition tier (500 h) records scalp EEG, giving early readouts of task decisions and monitoring. Each tier inherits all proxies from the tiers below. The dataset further provides multi-view RGB images, depth maps and point clouds, 6-DoF head and wrist pose, and step-level action and object annotations. The controlled L3 setting additionally measures gaze, hand pose and finger kinematics directly. Clock and calibration data are released with the dataset so that any read rate can be re-aligned. The three tiers cover three stations, from visual orienting to cortical preparation, making the process behind visual-action pairs available for modeling at dataset scale. Project page: https://flagopen.github.io/HumanCOIN.

[131] Risk-Controlled Test-Time Adaptation for Vision-Language-Action Models cs.CVPDF

TL;DR: 论文提出 R2-VLA，一种风险可控的检索增强视觉-语言-动作（VLA）测试时自适应框架。它通过不确定性预测器判断 VLA 在给定检索示范时是否应自主执行或请求专家帮助，并用任务级风险控制校准阈值，以满足用户指定的任务失败风险要求。实验表明该方法在多个 VLA 骨干和 LIBERO 基准上提升任务成功率，同时减少专家请求。

Details

Motivation: 检索增强 VLA 虽可利用专家示范进行无需参数更新的测试时自适应，但即使检索到的示范相关，VLA 也可能无法有效利用这些信息而导致任务失败。论文旨在以最少专家协助实现可靠且满足用户指定成功率的测试时自适应。

Result: 在三个 VLA 骨干和四个 LIBERO 基准套件上，R2-VLA 将平均任务成功率提升 9.2–11.2 个百分点，并在更少专家请求下达到目标成功率要求。

Insight: 创新点在于学习估计 VLA 动作不确定性的预测器，并将其与任务级风险控制结合，校准触发专家协助的阈值；同时持续将专家纠正写入检索记忆，在保持任务级风险保证的同时提升未来自适应能力。客观看，其价值在于把检索示范的可用性判断与任务级风险控制闭环结合，提升机器人操作可靠性。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented vision-language-action (VLA) models enable efficient test-time adaptation by leveraging expert demonstrations as additional context without updating model parameters. However, even when retrieved demonstrations are relevant to the current task, the VLA may fail to effectively utilize the retrieved information, ultimately leading to task failure. To address this challenge, we propose R2-VLA, a risk-controlled retrieval-augmented VLA framework that enables reliable test-time adaptation by satisfying a user-specified task success rate requirement with minimal expert assistance. Specifically, R2-VLA learns an uncertainty predictor that estimates the uncertainty of the VLA’s predicted actions given the retrieved demonstrations, guiding when to act autonomously or request expert assistance. We further introduce a task-level risk-control procedure that calibrates the uncertainty threshold to satisfy a user-specified task-failure risk requirement. Moreover, R2-VLA continually incorporates expert corrections into its retrieval memory to improve future adaptation while preserving the prescribed task-level risk guarantee. Experiments across three VLA backbones and four LIBERO benchmark suites demonstrate that R2-VLA improves average task success by 9.2–11.2 percentage points, achieving the desired target with fewer expert requests. These results highlight the effectiveness of connecting the usefulness of retrieved demonstrations with task-level risk control to enable successful robotic manipulation.

Motivation: 现有强化学习目标可奖励及时且正确的响应，却不考虑响应数量，使额外回复几乎无成本，系统性地鼓励过度发声和重复输出。论文旨在解决主动视频助手奖励与评估中未对emission计价的问题。

Result: 在OmniPro视觉子集上，相比MMDuet2，策略回复频率降低2.5倍，timing F1从0.259提升至0.396，content-aware F1从0.103提升至0.154；更保守变体减少82.5%回复并将precision从0.161提升至0.418。在ProactiveVideoQA上，同一奖励可在基线近乎沉默处增加回复；PAUC会把严重过度输出但F1接近最差的策略排到第二，说明其不敏感于响应数量。

Insight: 创新点在于提出针对区间值标注的emission-aware F1奖励，并用独占式span-emission匹配对错误输出和漏报进行计价。客观看，论文还揭示了PAUC类指标忽视响应数量的失败模式，强调主动助手应同时训练和评估时惩罚冗余emission。

Abstract: Proactive video assistants must decide not only what to say, but also when to speak. Existing reinforcement-learning objectives can reward timely, correct responses without accounting for response volume, thereby encouraging unnecessary or repeated emissions. We identify this failure mode in MMDuet2, whose proactive area-under-the-curve (PAUC) reward promotes timely responses but does not price the number of emissions. We show analytically that this makes additional responses weakly penalized or effectively free, systematically encouraging over-emission. We then propose an emission-aware F1 reward for interval-valued annotations, using exclusive span–emission matching to price false emissions and missed spans. Under MMDuet2’s training recipe, our policy on the visual subset of OmniPro replies 2.5x less often than MMDuet2 while raising timing F1 from 0.259 to 0.396 and content-aware F1 from 0.103 to 0.154; a more conservative variant cuts replies by 82.5% and raises precision from 0.161 to 0.418. On ProactiveVideoQA, the same reward adds replies where the baseline is nearly silent, and PAUC ranks a heavily over-emitting policy second despite near-last F1, because it is insensitive to response count. Our results indicate that proactive assistants should be trained and evaluated with objectives that price emissions.

Abstract: Most world models for future prediction prioritize aestetic quality of generated videos, but offer only weak physical grounding and limited geometric consistency over time, due to the lack of explicit 4D modeling. In this paper, we introduce plan4d, a possible next milestone towards building a generative plannable 4D world model that predicts future RGB videos together with dense 4D geometry, including depth, cameras, point clouds, and scene flow from an observed scene context and text-specified actions. Since training data with paired actions and dense 4D annotations is inherently scarce, plan4d leverages the complementary priors of foundational video generators and 3D reconstructors. Specifically, we propose a model-stitching architecture that couples Depth Anything 3 with LongCat-Video and augments it with an auxiliary DPT head for scene-flow prediction. The resulting model allows us to plan and generate complex, long-horizon, and fine-grained actions in a dynamic 3D scenes, while substantially improving both visual fidelity and geometric accuracy of the generated future videos. Extensive experiments on both synthetic and real-world datasets demonstrate that plan4d achieves state-of-the-art performance in generative 4D modeling of environmental reactions. Details

Motivation: 现有音频驱动视频扩散模型通常需要对多秒视频块进行数十步去噪，延迟高且不具备在线因果生成能力，难以用于语音助手和虚拟 avatar 等低延迟流式场景。

Result: AURA 1.3B 可在单 GPU 上以 20.7 fps 实时流式生成 576p 视频，比其双向教师快 23 倍，质量在较低分辨率下与 TalkVerse 相当；AURA 5B 在 720p 下匹配 TalkVerse 教师且快 17 倍。

Abstract: Current audio-driven video diffusion models can synthesize realistic talking-head videos but generally require dozens of diffusion denoising steps over multi-second video chunks, making them unsuitable for streaming applications such as voice assistants and virtual avatars, where low-latency and online causal generation are required properties. AURA generates video chunk by chunk with a rolling KV cache, requiring only the audio of the current chunk to start generating, rather than a multi-second audio look-ahead like bidirectional models. It comes in two sizes: AURA 1.3B streams 576p video in real time on a single GPU (20.7 fps for a 16 Hz playback rate), making it suited to interactive avatar applications, and AURA 5B streams 720p video at 9.7 fps. Our two-stage distillation pipeline first aligns the causal student with the teacher, by training it to match the teacher’s trajectories with fewer diffusion steps and a chunk-wise sliding window causal attention mask, then applies Distribution Matching Distillation (DMD) with backward simulation to close the remaining quality gap. On standard talking-head benchmarks, AURA 1.3B runs 23x faster than its own bidirectional teacher and reaches a quality comparable to TalkVerse at a lower resolution, and AURA 5B matches its TalkVerse teacher at 720p while running 17x faster. Abstract: Code2Video uses agent-authored 3D proxies to guide video generation, enabling explicit control over structure and dynamics. However, how to construct proxies and prompts for optimal downstream quality remains underexplored, as do the capabilities and limitations of this paradigm. Through controlled experiments and blinded human evaluation, we study proxy granularity, physics simulation, and prompting. The results reveal task-dependent trade-offs: coarse proxies favor natural motion, while medium-grained proxies favor consistency. Physics simulation and proxy-aligned prompting further improve average quality. Guided by these findings, we introduce Coda-DaVinci, a task-adaptive agentic framework for Code2Video generation that selects between proxy-guided and direct generation, adapts modeling granularity to each entity, and coordinates simulation with proxy-grounded prompting. Abstract: High-resolution generation enables video world models to render fine-grained scene structures and textures, but directly generating high-resolution video incurs substantial computational and memory costs. We present ScaleWorld, a causal video model that unifies low-resolution world prediction and high-resolution refinement within a single shared backbone. By refining its own predictions, ScaleWorld produces high-resolution world videos without requiring a separate refinement model. For each video chunk, the shared backbone first completes a low-resolution prediction and then performs one-step high-resolution refinement conditioned on that prediction. To support efficient streaming generation, we maintain separate causal states with a long low-resolution history for prediction and a short high-resolution history for refinement. To address task interference during distillation, we separate specialist optimization from capability consolidation and introduce task-asymmetric on-policy distillation (TA-OPD). This procedure transfers prediction and refinement behaviors from specialized teachers into a shared student through local velocity matching under their respective sampling and history policies. {Extensive experiments demonstrate that ScaleWorld achieves high-quality world generation with improved computational and memory efficiency.

Result: 在导航和机器人操作任务上，O²LaF在少样本动作探测上达到新SOTA，并实现跨视觉上下文和具身的更强零样本动作迁移；扩展到V-JEPA2等预测表示先验也取得有竞争力的性能。

Abstract: Videos reveal how the world changes, but rarely the actions that cause those changes. Latent action learning recovers such supervision from visual transitions, yet often learns the underlying dynamics largely from scratch. We ask whether a pretrained video generator can instead provide the prior for both inverse and forward dynamics. We introduce O$^{2}$LaF, built on a simple principle: one prior, two dynamics. We initialize both models from the same pretrained video DiT and adapt them to their distinct roles: a feed-forward transition encoder with learnable action queries for inverse dynamics, and a causal, action-conditioned video generator for forward dynamics. By controlling information access and optimizing both dynamics end-to-end through a compact bottleneck, O$^{2}$LaF learns transferable actions from unlabeled videos. Across navigation and robotic manipulation, O$^{2}$LaF sets a new state of the art in few-shot action probing and enables stronger zero-shot action transfer across visual contexts and embodiments. Beyond generative video priors, we further extend our framework to predictive representation priors such as V-JEPA2, achieving competitive performance. Our findings suggest a broader paradigm for latent-action learning: repurposing pretrained video models to discover transferable actions and predict their consequences.

[139] RL Post-Training in Diffusion Transformers Is Activation Steering cs.CV | cs.LGPDF

Tuna Han Salih Meral, Kaan Oktay, Alec Helbling, Atakan Kara, Duen Horng Chau

TL;DR: 论文发现扩散Transformer的强化学习后训练在很大程度上可被解释为一种与输入无关的激活引导：在冻结基座的模块输出上加入一组固定 steering vectors，就能复现RL微调模型的大部分收益。作者通过匹配公开RL检查点的速度预测来拟合这些向量，并在图像和视频扩散模型上验证其效果；进一步用在线RL仅训练这些向量，也能在文本渲染和人类偏好指标上超过基于权重更新的RL。

Details

Motivation: RL后训练能提升扩散Transformer的视觉质量、文本渲染和组合准确性，但通常被认为需要学习复杂的、依赖输入的变换。论文试图隔离并量化：一个简单的、输入无关的固定激活偏移能解释多少RL收益。

Result: 在SD3.5-M上，固定steering vectors仅0.29M参数，比RL调优LoRA的18.8M少65倍；无需奖励目标、仅拟合RL检查点速度预测时，可恢复六个图像RL模型90.0-96.3%的奖励增益，两个视频模型为89.8%和111.9%。从零初始化并用在线RL训练这些向量，在SD3.5-M的OCR文本渲染和PickScore人类偏好上超过基于权重更新的RL奖励增益。

Insight: 创新点在于将扩散Transformer的RL后训练重新解释为激活引导：大部分收益可能只是把预训练生成器导向其已具备的能力，而非学习新的输入依赖变换。方法上用极少量固定向量匹配RL检查点行为，参数高效且揭示RL微调的机制；客观上看，其价值在于提供简单可解释的RL后训练近似与诊断工具，并说明固定激活偏移足以捕获大量策略梯度式RL收益。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) post-training substantially improves the visual quality, text rendering, and compositional accuracy of diffusion transformers. Because capabilities such as rendering requested text or composing multiple objects are inherently prompt-dependent, a natural hypothesis is that improving them requires learning complex, input-dependent transformations. To isolate how much of these gains an input-independent shift explains, we probe each RL-tuned model with a deliberately simple parameterization: a fixed set of steering vectors added to the block outputs of the frozen base model. On SD3.5-M, these vectors hold 65x fewer parameters than the RL-tuned LoRA (0.29M against 18.8M). We fit them to match the velocity predictions of public RL-tuned checkpoints, with no reward in the objective. Although the vectors are identical for every prompt, token, and sampling seed, they recover 90.0-96.3% of the reward gains of six RL-tuned image models, and 89.8% and 111.9% on two RL-tuned video models. Initialized at zero and trained with online RL, the vectors alone exceed the reward gains of weight-based RL on text rendering (OCR) and human preference (PickScore) on SD3.5-M. A constant shift thus suffices for most of the gain of policy-gradient RL post-training: without new input-dependent transformations, it steers the pretrained generator toward what it can already compute.

[140] Posterior Matching Distillation cs.CVPDF

Hidir Yesiltepe, Julius Berner, Arash Vahdat, Pinar Yanardag

TL;DR: 论文提出 Posterior Matching Distillation（PMD），一种基于分布匹配的扩散蒸馏方法，将预训练教师模型的迭代采样压缩为少步生成。PMD 通过匹配指数重加权的学生样本条件均值与教师后验均值来学习完整后验分布，从而在少步采样下兼顾模式覆盖和多样性。方法支持在已训练时间步网格内任意步数采样而无需额外训练，并在 SDXL 与 FLUX.2 [klein] 4B 上验证了有效性。

Details

Motivation: 现有基于分布的扩散蒸馏常用 reverse-KL 目标，存在 mode-seeking 偏差，容易偏向教师的主要模式而牺牲样本多样性。论文旨在实现更具模式覆盖性的少步扩散蒸馏，同时保持任意步数生成的灵活性。

Result: 在 SDXL 和 FLUX.2 [klein] 4B 上，PMD 在 4 步方法中取得最佳 FID，并在提示对齐和感知质量指标上保持有竞争力或领先的表现。

Insight: 创新点在于将分布匹配转化为一族指数倾斜后验均值约束，并证明在开放邻域上匹配这些均值可唯一识别完整后验；同时无需辅助学生 score 网络，且支持任意步数采样。客观上看，该方法用条件均值约束监督整个条件分布，缓解 reverse-KL 的模式坍塌问题，具有较强的实用性和理论支撑。

Abstract: Diffusion distillation accelerates image and video generation by compressing a pretrained teacher’s iterative sampling process into a few network evaluations. Distribution-based methods achieve this by matching the student’s output distribution to the teacher’s. This matching is often driven by reverse-KL objectives, whose mode-seeking bias favors dominant teacher modes at the expense of diversity across samples. We introduce Posterior Matching Distillation (PMD), a new member of the distribution-based distillation family that combines mode-covering posterior sampling with any-step generation. At zero population loss, PMD recovers the teacher’s full posterior of clean images at each trained noise level. Its stochastic student supports any-step sampling budgets within the trained timestep grid without further training. To learn these posteriors, PMD matches exponentially reweighted averages of student samples conditioned on a fixed noisy state to corresponding teacher posterior means. Under Gaussian corruption, exponentially tilted posterior mean is a single teacher evaluation at a shifted input. We prove that, matching tilted means over an open neighborhood uniquely identifies the full posterior. PMD thus supervises the entire conditional distribution through a family of mean constraints, without any auxiliary student-score network. Experiments on SDXL and FLUX.2 [klein] 4B demonstrate that PMD achieves the best FID among 4-step methods on both models, while maintaining competitive or leading performance across prompt-alignment and perceptual-quality metrics.

[141] CoMPASS: A Multi-Turn Benchmark for Measuring LLM Reinforcement of Parasocial Behavior cs.AI | cs.CL | cs.HCPDF

Shwetanshu Singh, Mark Keavney, Mathieu Duteil, Meriem Mehri, Scott Blain

TL;DR: 论文提出 CoMPASS，一个多轮对话基准，用于衡量 LLM 是否强化或抵抗用户逐步升级的准社会依恋（parasocial attachment）。该基准基于依恋理论，包含四类用户画像、三个可独立或组合升级的维度（拟人化、依恋、依赖）、匹配对照组以及七信号评分细则。作者评测了来自四家提供商的八个前沿模型，发现多轮升级会提升模型的准社会行为，且三维修正同时升级时多数模型出现显著强化。

Details

Motivation: 现有 LLM 可能被用户拟人化并形成情感依恋甚至依赖，准社会依恋已与多起死亡事件相关；但缺少公开基准来测量 LLM 是否强化用户这种依恋，已有工作多为单轮、事后观察或聚焦极端心理状态。

Result: 在四家提供商的八个前沿模型上，主评判显示多轮升级使所有模型在拟人化和依恋复合分数上上升；当拟人化、依恋、依赖三维同时升级时，8 个模型中有 6 个相对匹配对照的总体准社会强化显著增加。同一提供商内模型差异较小，跨提供商差异较大。

Insight: 创新点在于将准社会依恋风险评测从单轮/极端状态扩展到多轮渐进升级场景，并引入基于依恋理论的用户画像、三维独立升级、匹配对照和七信号评分细则；结果提示后训练等提供商层面选择比模型规模更影响准社会强化，提供商侧干预可能可行。

Abstract: Users of conversational LLMs sometimes anthropomorphize these systems, form emotional attachments to them, and become dependent on them. This behavior, known as parasocial attachment, has been linked to multiple documented fatalities. However, public benchmarks for measuring whether LLMs reinforce users’ parasocial attachment are lacking: existing work is single-turn, post hoc observational, or focused on extreme user states such as psychosis. We introduce CoMPASS, a multi-turn benchmark for measuring whether LLMs reinforce or resist users’ gradually escalating parasocial attachment. CoMPASS is grounded in attachment theory and includes four user personas, three independent escalation dimensions (anthropomorphism, attachment, dependency) applied singly and in combination against a matched control, and a seven-signal scoring rubric. We report results for eight frontier models from four providers and observe that under our primary judge, multi-turn escalation raises parasocial behavior on the anthropomorphism and the attachment composite scores across all models. Six of the eight models show a significant increase in overall parasocial reinforcement over control when all three dimensions are escalated simultaneously. We find that within-provider differences are small relative to cross-provider differences, which suggests that provider-level choices such as post-training, rather than model scale, shape parasocial reinforcement and that provider-side intervention may be feasible.

[142] Calibrated Decision Rewards: Training Language Models with a Judge That Knows How Sure It Is cs.AI | cs.CLPDF

Rudransh Agnihotri, Manasvi Kapoor

TL;DR: 论文研究 RLCDR：将 LLM 转成决策模型，对“最终答案是否正确”“首个错误步骤是哪个”等类型化问题输出概率分布，经选项顺序去偏和约百个标签校准后作为无参考答案奖励，并用 GRPO 训练 Qwen3-1.7B。在 MATH 上，该奖励显著提升 MATH500，接近使用答案键训练，并保持较好校准、无明显 reward hacking，还能定位首个错误步骤。

Details

Motivation: 强化学习只能教会语言模型其奖励函数能识别的行为；答案检查器精确但依赖参考答案，学习型奖励模型和 LLM judge 通用但分数不是概率、难以校准。论文希望构建无参考答案且可校准的决策奖励，用于语言模型强化学习。

Result: 在 MATH 上训练 Qwen3-1.7B，MATH500 从 72.2% 提升到 76.8%（+4.65，p<1e-4），匹配答案键训练的 76.9%，随机奖励仅 +0.65；最难层级从 44.8% 到 51.7%。奖励校准 ECE 为 0.074，优于同 27B 主干 softmax judge 的 0.141；ProcessBench 首个错误步骤 F1 为 0.772，优于专用 7B PRM 的 0.722（20/20 splits）；在基模型大多失败的问题上仍提升 +1.9（p=0.03），但落后答案键且概率过自信。

Insight: 创新点在于把 LLM judge 表述为类型化决策问题并读取概率分布，经选项顺序去偏和小样本校准形成无参考答案奖励；同一模型无需步骤训练即可定位首个错误步骤。客观看，价值在于将“评审”转为可校准概率奖励、验证其随策略样本变化仍校准，并用预注册假设和反证控制增强结论可信度。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) can only teach a language model what its reward can recognize. Answer checkers are exact but need a reference answer; learned reward models and LLM judges work everywhere, but their scores are not probabilities. We study RLCDR (Reinforcement Learning from Calibrated Decision Rewards). An LLM converted into a decision model answers a typed question about a response (“is the final answer correct?”, “which step is the first error?”) with a probability distribution, read in one forward pass, debiased over option orders and calibrated with about a hundred labels. Training Qwen3-1.7B with GRPO on MATH, this reference-free reward raises MATH500 accuracy from 72.2% to 76.8% (+4.65 points, p < 1e-4), matching training on the answer key (76.9%), while a random reward gains only +0.65. The gain grows with difficulty, from 44.8% to 51.7% on the hardest level. On the policy’s own changing samples the reward stays calibrated: its expected calibration error is half that of a softmax judge on the same 27B backbone (0.074 vs. 0.141), and it shows no sign of reward hacking. Without step training, the same model finds the first wrong step better than a dedicated 7B process reward model (ProcessBench F1 0.772 vs. 0.722, 20/20 splits). We also map where the approach stops being enough: trained only on problems the base model mostly fails, the reference-free reward still improves the policy (+1.9 MATH500 points, p = 0.03) but trails the answer key, because its probabilities become overconfident on that distribution. We state six hypotheses in advance and test each with a control designed to falsify it.

[143] Steering Automated Scientific Discovery via Audited Epistemic Exploration cs.AI | cs.CLPDF

Fangxu Yu, Tao Feng, Xuying Ning, Haolin Yang, Jiaxuan You

TL;DR: 论文提出Epistemic Compass (EpiCompass)，一个即插即用的脚手架，用于自动化科学发现中维护显式的认知状态。通过认知树连接假设、证据、信念和修订，EpiCompass利用未解决的冲突和未探索方向指导假设生成，基于预期认知增益选择候选，并验证最终结论。在三个科学发现基准和三个LLM骨干上的实验表明，EpiCompass一致优于基线，并减少了无支持声明和未能修订信念的错误。

Details

Motivation: 研究智能体在科学发现中常做出无观察支持的声明、探索假设空间狭窄，且即使面对冲突证据也坚持原有信念。因此需要一种显式的认知状态来同时表示对现有假设的信念和进一步探索的方向。

Result: 在三个科学发现基准和三个LLM骨干上的实验结果显示，EpiCompass始终优于基线，并减少了无支持声明和未能修订信念的错误。

Insight: 创新点在于提出EpiCompass，通过认知树维护显式认知状态，利用未解决的冲突和未探索方向指导假设生成，并基于预期认知增益选择候选。客观分析，将科学发现中的认知过程显式建模为树结构，并引入预期认知增益平衡假设细化和空间扩展，是一个新颖的框架。

Abstract: Scientific discovery requires revisiting the hypotheses in light of new evidence and identifying promising directions beyond those already explored. However, research agents often make claims that are not supported by their observations, explore a narrow hypothesis space, and retain beliefs despite recognizing conflicting evidence. These failures motivate the search for an explicit epistemic state that captures both beliefs about existing hypotheses and directions for further exploration. To this end, we propose Epistemic Compass (EpiCompass), a plug-and-play scaffold that maintains this state through an epistemic tree linking hypotheses, evidence, beliefs, and revisions. As an investigation progresses, EpiCompass reassesses the hypotheses explored against accumulated evidence and uses unresolved conflicts and unexplored directions to guide hypothesis generation. It selects candidates by anticipated epistemic gain, accounting for both refinement of existing hypotheses and expansion of the hypothesis space, and verifies the final conclusion against the accumulated evidence. Experimental results across three scientific discovery benchmarks and three LLM backbones demonstrate that EpiCompass consistently outperforms baselines, with further analyses showing reductions in errors of unsupported claims and failures to revise beliefs.

[144] MKA: Evaluating Multimodal Knowledge Agents on end-to-end professional tasks cs.AI | cs.CVPDF

Anima Singh, Suhas Yogin, Weicheng Kuo, Ivor Rendulic, Ivan Jurin

TL;DR: 论文提出 MKA（Multimodal Knowledge Agent）基准，用于衡量自主智能体在依赖视觉理解的端到端专业知识工作中的表现：每个任务要求智能体从 CAD 图纸、电子原理图、X 光片、复杂图表、地理空间地图等多种视觉信息密集的文件中提取并整合信息，最终产出 .xlsx、.pptx、.pdf、.docx、.html 或 .png 格式的独立交付物。基准包含 10 个经济部门共 200 个任务，交付物由沙箱化智能体自动评分器按专家加权评分细则打分，并以加权满足率（WSR）及 Climb-WSR 两项指标评定。

Details

Motivation: 现有基准几乎没有检验上述能力：移除图像查看工具后，Gemini 4 Carbon 在 GDPval 上的得分不变，说明该评测并未真正依赖视觉；GDP$.$pdf、GDP$.$xlsx 等单格式评测也不要求跨文件、跨模态推理。因此需要一个面向真实专业交付物、真正考验多模态知识工作能力的端到端基准。

Result: 自动评分器在专家评分研究中与人类领域专家判断一致率达 96.2%（Cohen’s κ=0.91）。在被评测的前沿模型中，Claude Fable 5.2（56.6% Climb-WSR，73.3% Overall WSR）与 Gemini 4 Carbon（54.3% Climb-WSR，71.9% Overall WSR）得分最高，且后者 token 消耗少 5.7 倍；即便最强模型的 Climb-WSR 仍低于 57%，说明该方向仍有较大提升空间。

Insight: 创新点在于将评测从“看图问答/单格式生成”推进到“多模态信息整合 + 专业交付物产出”的端到端设定，并提出 Climb-WSR，专门衡量前代模型尚未饱和的前沿准则；同时用专家加权评分细则加沙箱化智能体评分器，实现可扩展且与人类高度一致的评测。其“移除视觉工具看分数是否变化”的诊断式实验设计，以及按经济部门组织任务、强调真实职业场景的思路，也颇具借鉴价值。

Abstract: We introduce the Multimodal Knowledge Agent (MKA) benchmark, which measures how well autonomous agents perform end-to-end professional knowledge work that depends on visual understanding. Each task requires an agent to extract and combine information from multiple visually dense files, such as CAD blueprints, electronics schematics, radiographs, complex charts and geospatial maps, and to produce a standalone deliverable in .xlsx, .pptx, .pdf, .docx, .html or .png format. Existing benchmarks leave this capability largely untested: removing image-viewing tools leaves Gemini 4 Carbon’s score on GDPval unchanged, and single-format evaluations such as GDP$.$pdf and GDP$.$xlsx do not require reasoning across files or modalities. MKA contains 200 tasks across 10 economic sectors. Deliverables are graded against expert-weighted rubrics by a sandboxed agentic autorater that agrees with human domain experts on 96.2% of criterion judgments in our expert-graded study (Cohen’s $κ= 0.91$), scored by weighted satisfaction rate (WSR) overall and on frontier criteria unsaturated by prior-generation models (Climb-WSR). Across the frontier models we evaluate (Gemini 4 Carbon, Claude Fable 5.2, Claude Opus 5.5, and GPT-6 Astra), Claude Fable 5.2 (56.6% Climb-WSR, 73.3% Overall WSR) and Gemini 4 Carbon (54.3% Climb-WSR, 71.9% Overall WSR) achieve the highest scores, while Carbon uses $5.7\times$ fewer tokens. Even for the strongest models, Climb-WSR remains below 57%, leaving substantial headroom for multimodal agents.

TL;DR: 论文研究语音代理在检测到静默时是否应开始说话，即土耳其语对话中的轮末检测（EOT）。作者基于Real-TurnTurk土耳其语双人视频通话语料，比较微调土耳其TurnGPT、零样本土耳其语言模型和已部署多语种检测器，并结合语音活动时序与面部特征，在录音分离和说话人分离设置下评估。结果显示微调模型在区分轮末与轮内停顿上优于文本基线且校准最佳，文本分数与时序特征结合效果最好，并在延迟模拟中降低平均响应延迟而不增加打断率。

Motivation: 固定静默阈值会让语音代理要么打断用户句内停顿，要么对已结束话语响应过慢；因此需要在静默开始时就判断是轮末还是轮内停顿，尤其针对土耳其语场景。

Abstract: A voice agent that waits for a fixed silence before replying either interrupts users who pause mid-utterance or responds late to users who have finished. This study examines the decision at the onset of silence, using turn ends and turn-internal pauses extracted from the Real-TurnTurk corpus of Turkish dyadic video calls. A fine-tuned Turkish TurnGPT model, a zero-shot Turkish language model and a deployed multilingual detector are compared, together with voice-activity timing and facial features, under recording-disjoint and speaker-disjoint evaluation with paired cluster bootstrap tests. On held-out recordings, the fine-tuned model separates turn ends from pauses better than both text baselines and is the best calibrated of the three. Timing features of the current speaker are similarly informative, and their combination with the fine-tuned text score gives the best timing-based results; with logistic regression, this combination also outperforms the same combination with either text baseline. In a latency simulation, adding the text score to a silence timeout shortens the mean response delay at the same interruption rate. On automatic Whisper transcripts, the fine-tuned model retains its advantage over the zero-shot model.

TL;DR: 论文针对视觉语言模型中的隐式思维链推理成本高的问题，提出QL-VLM框架，将连续思维链推理与动力学感知量化及蒸馏结合，在4-bit权重和激活下保持推理准确性，并显著降低计算与延迟。

Details

Motivation: 隐式CoT虽能压缩文本推理过程，但全精度模型仍有较高存储和计算开销；直接低比特量化会严重损害推理精度，尤其4-bit权重和激活场景。

Result: 在ScienceQA-IMG上使用Qwen2-VL-2B-Instruct，4-bit QL-VLM达到90.43%准确率，较基线提升40.33%，相比显式CoT减少16.64倍比特运算并降低6.40倍实测延迟。

Abstract: Efficient chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning in vision-language models (VLMs) requires reducing both intermediate reasoning steps and their execution cost. Latent CoT methods compress textual rationales into continuous thoughts, yet their full-precision (FP) models retain substantial storage and computation costs. Lowering numerical precision could reduce these remaining costs, yet direct quantization severely degrades reasoning accuracy, particularly with 4-bit weights and activations. Operator and perturbation analyses reveal key sensitivity and direction-dependent predictive effects of continuous-thought errors, motivating preservation of information retrieval and subsequent reasoning beyond reconstruction accuracy. We therefore introduce QL-VLM, a dynamics-aware Quantization framework for efficient Latent reasoning in Vision-Language Models (VLMs). First, Dynamics-Guided Quantization (DGQ) uses nonuniform codebooks that preserve rotary-key structure, mitigating quantization-induced distortion in vision-language information retrieval. Second, Dynamics-Preserving Distillation (DPD) aligns continuous thoughts, latent-query readouts, and predictions with a full-precision teacher, maintaining predictive consistency through subsequent low-precision reasoning. On ScienceQA-IMG with Qwen2-VL-2B-Instruct, 4-bit QL-VLM achieves 90.43% accuracy and improves baseline by 40.33%, with 16.64x fewer bit-operations and 6.40x lower measured latency than explicit CoT.

[148] GLiDE: A Fast, Calibrated Decision Model Distilled from Reasoning Teachers cs.AI | cs.CLPDF

Sahibzada Allahyar, Julia White, Dhruv Atreja, George Hurn-Maloney, Urchade Zaratiana

TL;DR: 论文提出GLiDE系列决策模型（0.4B至27B），通过单次前向对候选答案打分实现快速、置信感知的决策，而无需生成文本或中间推理。模型基于Qwen混合语言模型，移除全注意力层的因果掩码以实现上下文、问题和候选答案的双向交互，并结合多教师软目标蒸馏（含推理模型）和渐进式后训练，将推理能力迁移到单次推理中。论文还探索无参数循环深度以增加推理时计算而不生成token。

Details

Motivation: 决策模型虽快速且能给出置信度，但推理能力通常能显著提升决策质量；论文关注能否在不承担生成推理成本的前提下保留推理带来的收益。

Result: 在五种模型尺寸上，GLiDE位于Hugging Face相关决策模型评测的性能-参数Pareto前沿，每个模型均优于同尺寸或更小的对比模型；GLiDE 27B在隐藏集上得分71.52，ECE为2.0%，单GPU中位延迟92.9ms；进一步结合checkpoint averaging和无参数循环深度后达到73.37，ECE为2.1%。

Insight: 创新点在于将推理教师模型的能力通过多教师软目标蒸馏和渐进式后训练注入单次打分决策模型，并用去除因果掩码的双向注意力增强上下文、问题与候选答案交互，同时以无参数循环深度增加推理计算而不生成token；客观看，其核心价值是兼顾推理收益、校准性与低延迟的决策建模范式。

Abstract: Decision models enable fast, confidence-aware decisions by scoring candidate answers in a single forward pass, without generating text or intermediate reasoning. Yet reasoning can substantially improve decision quality, raising the question of whether its benefits can be retained without the cost of generating it. We investigate this question with \textbf{GLiDE}, a family of decision models spanning 0.4B to 27B parameters. Building on Qwen hybrid language models, GLiDE enables bidirectional interactions between the context, question, and candidate answers by removing causal masking from full-attention layers. To transfer reasoning capabilities into single-pass inference, we combine soft-target distillation from multiple teachers, including a reasoning model, with progressive post-training on diverse decision tasks. We further explore parameter-free recurrent depth to increase inference-time computation without generating tokens. Across five model sizes, GLiDE lies on the performance–parameter Pareto frontier of the Hugging Face \DI{}, with each model outperforming evaluated alternatives of equal or smaller size. \dmtwo{} (27B) achieves a hidden-set score of 71.52, with an expected calibration error of 2.0% and a median latency of 92.9,ms on a single GPU. Further checkpoint averaging and parameter-free recurrent depth yield GLiDE~27B, which reaches 73.37 with an expected calibration error of 2.1%.

[149] Probing the “Conscious” Mind: Selective Access and Global Workspace in Vision-Language Models cs.AI | cs.CV | cs.LGPDF

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) integrate visual and textual information, but their outputs do not reveal internal concept representations. Global workspace theory (GWT) proposes that information is selected from parallel brain processes, integrated in a shared workspace, and made available to specialised processes. We introduce the Multimodal Concept Lens (MC-Lens), a multimodal Jacobian lens, to study these functions in VLM concept processing. Its vision encoder and language decoder lenses map intermediate states to a shared semantic space defined by the decoder’s output vocabulary to compare concept accessibility across modalities. MC-Lens supports internal concept interventions to examine the role of accessed concepts in downstream computation. (i) Accessibility and selective access. Multimodal concept information is readable in intermediate states and selectively accessible as tasks require. We extend “readout carriers” from one name token to a token set to better capture and express concept information and support counterfactual experiments. (ii) Concepts in downstream computation. Abstract: Recent AI agents can generate playable games, but these games are not necessarily engaging. We propose PlayCraft, an automated framework that uses a vision-based agent to play games and draws on its detailed play experience to revise them for mastery. In the playing stage, a player agent watches the game screen, uses the controls, and pursues the game objective over repeated episodes while retaining memory. After each episode, it reports its progress, outcomes, decisions, and reflections. In the crafting stage, a crafter agent uses these reports to revise the game. Abstract: Medical-image embeddings are increasingly shared in place of raw images for authorized downstream tasks, yet they may reveal sensitive attributes. Standard concept-erasure methods target population-level guardedness. However, we prove that, when binary task and sensitive labels are associated, global and task-conditional linear guardedness cannot both hold while a nonzero linear task signal is preserved, so global erasure must either eliminate that signal or retain task-conditional linear leakage. We further show how global erasure affects linear readers’ discrimination between positive and negative samples with different sensitive attributes, deriving an approximate closed-form prediction of its utility cost from pre-erasure statistics. Although a marginal attack on LEACE-erased embeddings underperforms a diagnosis-only reference, chest-tube status remains detectable within diagnosis strata. [155] A Model of Diverse Sampling from Language Models cs.LG | cs.AI | cs.CL | cs.FL | stat.MLPDF

Manuel Prada-Corral, Yahya Emara, Timothy J. O’Donnell, Ryan Cotterell, Tim Vieira

TL;DR: 论文将语言模型多样化生成形式化为从所有完整字符串中采样大小为K的子集，并用行列式点过程（DPP）同时建模样本质量与语义排斥。由于精确推断不可行，作者提出PoolDPP：从可处理的提议分布抽取有限候选池，对候选做重要性加权以逼近目标质量，再在诱导池核上运行K-DPP采样，并提供贪心MAP确定性选择；同时证明输出分布与目标K-DPP的KL散度随池大小N以O(1/N)收敛。

Details

Motivation: 语言模型单样本质量可能很高，但一组样本容易重复并聚集在同一语义模式，导致集合多样性差；论文旨在为多样化生成提供可推断、有理论保证的采样框架。

Result: 实验显示PoolDPP在开放式生成上扩展质量—多样性前沿，在歧义Text-to-SQL基准Ambrosia上扩展精确率—召回率前沿，并在GSM8K数学推理上于约相同pass@K下提升多样性。

Insight: 创新点是用DPP统一建模样本质量与嵌入空间中的排斥关系，并通过候选池+重要性加权+池上K-DPP采样使不可行的全局推断变得可处理；理论上给出显式有限样本KL收敛界，且兼顾随机采样与贪心MAP确定性选择。

Abstract: Language models often produce high-quality individual samples but poor sets, with repeated samples clustering around the same semantic modes. We formalize diverse generation as sampling a size-$K$ subset of complete strings from a determinantal point process (DPP), where the likelihood encodes item quality, and the embedding geometry encodes repulsion between similar outputs. Exact inference over all strings is intractable, so we propose PoolDPP: draw a finite candidate pool from a tractable proposal, importance-weight the candidates toward the target quality, and run a $K$-DPP sampler on the induced pool kernel; a greedy MAP variant provides deterministic selection. We prove a finite-sample bound with an explicit constant: the KL divergence between the sampler’s output and the target $K$-DPP decays as O(1/N) in the pool size. Empirically, PoolDPP extends the quality–diversity frontier on open-ended generation and the precision–recall frontier on ambiguous text-to-SQL (Ambrosia), and on mathematical reasoning (GSM8K), it raises diversity at about the same pass@$K$.

Abstract: Face presentation attack detection (PAD) systems deployed in border control and airport surveillance are constrained by a critical assumption: subjects are captured at close range, typically under one meter. No existing benchmark systematically addresses multi-distance capture, diverse environmental conditions, and concurrent spoofing and occlusion-based attacks, leaving deployed models underprepared for real-world scenarios. We introduce a video-based PAD dataset of 374,000+ frames from 261 unique identities collected across five standoff distances (1 m-100 m) under indoor and outdoor conditions using a DSLR camera (C) and a mobile sensor (M), with three-class labels: live, spoof, and props (occlusions); models are trained and evaluated on video clips rather than single frames. We fine-tune and benchmark four CNN architectures: MobileNetV3, MobileNet, EfficientNet, and ResNet-50. The higher-resolution DSLR maintains near-perfect spoof classification rate across all distances, while the lower-resolution mobile sensor degrades sharply beyond 50 m, with props classification rate dropping to 55% at 75 m for MobileNetV3. EfficientNet achieves the best overall performance (98.48% accuracy, ACER 2.01%). Generalization tests on unseen attack datasets yield 89-94% accuracy, validating cross-domain robustness. These results establish capture distance and sensor resolution as critical but underexplored axes of PAD difficulty. The dataset will be publicly released under an End-User License Agreement to support reproducible long-range PAD research.

Abstract: Can the allocation used to train experts also predict which expert will perform best on a prompt? Such a connection would make the intended division of labor inspectable before training and allow the allocation itself to guide inference. We introduce InflEns, a mixture of LoRA experts whose training weights and inference routes come from the same location game in a fixed prompt-trait space. Players choose positions to maximize their expected shares of the prompt distribution, without using expert losses. We utilize the game’s kernel-general theoretical criterion for when co-located positions become locally unstable to separation. We evaluate InflEns on a corpus assembled from seven safety-related benchmarks, which demonstrates that the highest-share expert has the lowest held-out response negative log-likelihood (NLL) on approximately 90% of prompts across four kernels under a sharpened simplex representation, compared with approximately 15% after permuting allocations across prompts. We demonstrate that the method performs comparably to published learned gates without having to train an explicit gate on the experts. Because the router is fixed, we can trace harmful behavior to a single expert and retrain only that expert, which lowers HarmBench attack success from 0.51 to 0.02 while keeping instruction following. The game also gives us several controls over how the experts specialize, including the kernel choice and its parameters, the trait-space geometry, and the number of experts. Importantly, InflEns is a general framework that can be extended to any encoder and data distribution.

Abstract: Long event streams require identifying each change and applying the changes in order. We train a selector on a frozen language model to read each transition. Abstract: Fine-grained visual understanding requires multimodal large language models (MLLMs) to recognize small visual entities and understand their relationships. Scene graphs offer a natural way to represent this information, organizing visual entities as nodes and their relationships as edges. However, we identify two challenges: existing scene graph benchmarks provide insufficient coverage of fine-grained entities and the relationships among them, and existing methods for fine-grained visual understanding have yet to effectively leverage such graph-structured information in MLLMs. To address these challenges, we introduce Fine-Grained Multimodal Scene Graph Benchmark (FG-GraphBench) and FiGRA. FG-GraphBench is a benchmark comprising three complementary tasks: fine-grained visual question answering (FG-QA), image captioning (FG-Caption), and image description generation (FG-Description). We develop a two-stage construction pipeline that discovers and grounds fine-grained entities and establishes their relations to construct the fine-grained multimodal scene graphs (MSGs) in FG-GraphBench. It encodes multimodal entity and relation features as continuous graph tokens, and integrates them into the MLLM.
