[160] RouteVerify: Routing Interventions for Step-Level Verification in Mixture-of-Experts Models cs.LG | cs.CLPDF

Yucheng Du, Ryan A. Rossi, Xiyang Hu

TL;DR: 论文提出 RouteVerify，对 MoE 模型进行受控路由干预，将来自不同解题轨迹的内部激活替换到当前输入中，以检验路由状态是否因果影响 step-level 验证判断。作者在 9 个 MoE checkpoint 上比较 router logits、MoE outputs 和 post-MoE residual states 三类干预接口，并改变层与 token 位置。结果表明路由可区分正确/错误步骤，且替换路由会改变验证判断，但这种改变很少随源步骤正确性变化。

Details

Motivation: 论文关注 MoE 路由状态虽能编码正确性信息，但这是否意味着模型 step-level 验证判断真正依赖路由编码的正确性。其出发点是检验“路由可读”是否等于“路由因果决定判断”，避免将相关性误读为因果性。

Result: 在 9 个 MoE checkpoint 上，router logits 探针在 8 个 checkpoint 中以 AUROC 0.80-0.88 区分正确与错误步骤；全层替换 router logits 在 9 个 checkpoint 中均改变判断，且强于范数匹配的随机替换。但源步骤正确性对判断变化影响很小：单层下 9 个 checkpoint 均无法区分来自正确/错误步骤的 margin，全层下仅 1 个呈正差异；局部 residual 替换则在 8/9 个 checkpoint 中检测到该依赖。

Insight: 论文的创新点在于提出受控干预框架 RouteVerify，系统比较不同干预接口、层和 token 位置，并设计源步骤正确性对照来区分“路由可读”与“路由因果决定判断”。客观来看，其价值更多在于机制分析与实验方法学：揭示路由替换虽能移动验证判断，但判断变化并不忠实跟随路由所编码的正确性，提示需谨慎解释 MoE 路由状态的因果作用。

Abstract: Routing states in mixture-of-experts (MoE) models can distinguish correct from erroneous reasoning steps. If changing routing also changes a model’s step-level verification judgment, it is tempting to conclude that the judgment follows what routing encodes. We test this inference with RouteVerify, a controlled intervention study that replaces selected internal activations from one solution trace with activations from another, while keeping the evaluated input unchanged. Across nine MoE checkpoints, we compare three intervention interfaces: router logits, MoE outputs, and post-MoE residual states. We vary the layers and token positions at which activations are replaced. We measure changes in a verification margin that quantifies the model’s preference for INCORRECT over CORRECT. We also evaluate the same input with activations from correct versus erroneous steps to test whether the resulting judgment depends on the source step’s correctness. We report four linked results. First, probes on router logits separate correct from erroneous steps in eight checkpoints (AUROC 0.80-0.88). Second, replacing router logits across all routed layers changes the judgment in all nine checkpoints, and by more than a norm-matched random replacement. Third, this change rarely depends on whether the source step is correct. At one layer, routing from erroneous steps and from correct steps produces indistinguishable margins in all nine checkpoints. Under all-layer routing, their difference is resolved and positive in only one. Fourth, the same difference is positive for local residual replacements in eight of nine checkpoints, so the measure does detect such dependence where it exists. Correctness is therefore readable from routing, and routing replacements move the judgment, but the movement rarely follows correctness.

Qin-Wen Luo, Haoxuan Gao, Sheng Ren, Xiang Chen, Sheng-Jun Huang

TL;DR: 论文提出一种面向视觉语言模型持续强化学习的策略改进引导回放方法 CoLiR。该方法利用策略差异引理，用当前策略在历史任务分布上的期望优势刻画历史性能变化，并据此回放历史提示；为分配有限回放内存，CoLiR 对比最终任务策略在正确回答上的似然增益与初始策略在错误回答上的似然降低来选择提示。

Details

Motivation: 传统持续学习多从稳定性角度强调知识保持与干扰抑制；本文从强化学习的基本策略改进原则出发，关注在持续强化学习中保留已获得的性能增益，并解决有限回放内存下如何选择历史提示以巩固策略改进的问题。

Result: 在 MRCL 基准上，使用三种 Qwen3-VL 模型规模时，CoLiR 在对比方法中取得最高平均最终准确率和平均任务准确率，同时保持有竞争力的新任务性能。

Insight: 创新点在于用策略差异引理将历史性能变化转化为当前策略对历史任务分布的优势，并提出对比似然回放：对比最终任务策略在正确回答上的似然增益与初始策略在错误回答上的似然降低；分析将其联系到 KL 正则化最优更新下的参考策略优势，并给出内生奖励解释。客观看，其创新在于把回放选择从稳定性/干扰视角转向策略改进视角，且内存构建仅需额外跨策略打分、无需额外生成回答。

Abstract: Continual reinforcement learning (CRL) requires models to acquire new capabilities while retaining performance on previous tasks. A stability-oriented perspective common in conventional continual learning emphasizes knowledge preservation and interference mitigation. Complementing this perspective, we revisit CRL for vision-language models through policy improvement, a fundamental principle of RL, to preserve acquired performance gains. Using the performance difference lemma, we characterize historical performance changes through the current policy’s expected advantages on historical task distributions, motivating replay of historical prompts with the current policy. To allocate limited replay memory, we propose Contrastive Likelihood Replay (CoLiR), which contrasts likelihood gains on correct responses from the final task policy with likelihood reductions on incorrect responses from the initial policy. Our analysis links correct-response likelihood gains to reference-policy advantages under an idealized KL-regularized optimal update and provides an endogenous-reward interpretation for general policy updates. CoLiR estimates scores from finite training rollouts and cached log-likelihoods, selects prompts at task boundaries, and replays them uniformly during subsequent training, with KL regularization restricted to replayed prompts. Memory construction only requires a small amount of additional cross-policy scoring but no additional response generation. Across three Qwen3-VL model sizes on MRCL, CoLiR achieves the highest mean final accuracy and mean task accuracy among the compared methods while maintaining competitive new-task performance.

[162] RL Can Only Reinforce What It Sees Succeed cs.LG | cs.CLPDF

Ruben Sanchez-Corcuera, Unai Zulaika, Aritz Bilbao-Jayo, Miguel Fernandez-de-Retana

TL;DR: 论文在一个先验与正确答案均已知的谜题环境中，从头训练小语言模型并研究结果奖励强化学习如何把预训练中的犹豫转化为决策。作者发现RL只能强化它已经观察到成功的动作；当正确动作稀有且奖励偏向它时，训练运行呈全有或全无，是否成功可由训练前“正确动作获胜的尝试组数”预测，约35组时达到50%成功概率。

Details

Motivation: 关注当已有规则失效、预训练先验与新观察冲突时，outcome-reward RL如何改变模型的决策平衡，尤其探讨RL能否学会稀有但正确的动作。

Result: 实验表明RL学习稀有正确动作存在可预测阈值：训练前期望成功尝试组数约35组时达到even odds；该规律跨新/重复问题、4到256次尝试的组大小、学习率、优化器、GRPO变体和1.3M到85M参数模型成立，模型规模等会移动阈值但不改变计数对象。

Insight: 创新点在于将结果奖励RL对稀有成功动作的学习能力归结为训练前“成功尝试组数”这一可预测统计量，并揭示组式RL中成功覆盖对学习起飞的限制；客观看，工作强调探索/覆盖与预训练先验的交互，为理解GRPO类方法在稀有正确动作场景下的全有或全无现象提供了简洁诊断指标。

Abstract: When a familiar rule stops working, an agent has to weigh what pre-training taught it against what it now observes. We ask how outcome-reward reinforcement learning (RL) changes this balance, in a puzzle world where both sides are known: small language models trained from scratch on games played by a Bayesian player, so that the prior and the right answer are exact. Base models hedge as their corpus did, and RL turns that hedging into decisions, in whichever direction the reward favours. But RL can only reinforce what it sees succeed. When the right action is rare, each run either learns it everywhere or not at all, and which runs take off is predicted before training by one count: the expected number of groups of attempts in which the action wins. About 35 such groups give even odds. The count holds for new and repeated problems and for groups of 4 to 256 attempts, and across learning rates, optimizers, GRPO variants and model sizes from 1.3M to 85M parameters, which move the threshold but not what is counted.

Result: 在相同架构ViT从零训练时，Frozen FAct较GELU提升top-1 1.46个百分点；在4个较小目标架构和4个图像分类数据集上，16种设置中15种取得11个激活函数中的最高平均精度，最高较最佳常规激活提升1.71个百分点；即使逐激活调参，仍在11/16设置排第一、4个第二、1个第四。

Abstract: Transfer learning typically reuses knowledge acquired by a source network through its learned weights. We investigate whether a learned activation function can also be transferred and remain useful in networks trained from scratch. We study this complementary form of transfer through a learn-freeze-transfer protocol, in which the activation is optimized jointly with a source network in a single training run, and is then frozen and reused in target networks trained from scratch. A pointwise activation with globally shared parameters is independent of layer dimensions and can therefore be incorporated into networks of different architectures trained from scratch. We instantiate this protocol with FAct, a five-parameter activation represented by a two-harmonic truncated Fourier series, initialized from a Fourier approximation of GELU and learned in a Vision Transformer (ViT) on ImageNet-1K. When reused in a ViT of the same architecture trained from scratch on ImageNet-1K, the frozen activation (Frozen FAct) improves top-1 accuracy by 1.46 percentage points over GELU. Across four smaller target architectures (standard ViT, Swin Transformer, linear-attention ViT, and MLP-Mixer) and four image-classification datasets, it achieves the highest mean accuracy among eleven activation functions in 15 of 16 settings under a common training configuration, with improvements of up to 1.71 percentage points over the best conventional activation. When the learning rate and weight decay are tuned separately for each activation under the same search budget, Frozen FAct still ranks first in 11 of the 16 settings, second in four, and fourth in one. These results provide empirical evidence that a learned activation function can retain its usefulness when transferred across vision architectures, independently of the weights with which it was learned.

Details

Motivation: 传统文本技能是离散的，无法进行连续优化；而微调等梯度方法通常与特定模型绑定，难以在不同智能体之间迁移。论文希望结合可梯度优化与跨模型迁移的优势。

Result: 在 4 个 VLM 和 4 个 benchmark 的 16 个设置中，Skill.png 在 9 个设置上显著优于前沿 LLM 优化的 SOTA 文本技能，同时推理仅需 64 至 256 个图像 token，而文本技能约需 900 至 2000 个 token；文本与图像联合优化的混合技能可进一步提升性能，集成 VLM 优化的图像也能跨模型迁移。

Insight: 核心创新是将技能编码到像素空间，使技能可通过冻结 VLM 的视觉编码器进行梯度优化，同时保留文本技能的跨模型迁移能力；图像技能与任意 VLM 兼容，且单张图像可对模型集成联合优化，混合技能进一步扩展了优化空间。

Abstract: AI agents are commonly specialized for new tasks and domains using skills, i.e. natural language instructions placed in their context. However, the discrete nature of textual skills prevents continuous optimization. Gradient-based approaches such as finetuning, on the other hand, are typically model-specific, preventing skill transfer across different agents. Since many agents today are built on vision-language models (VLMs), we can address these limitations by introducing Skill.png: a method for obtaining skill-like image inputs whose pixels are optimized with gradient descent through the vision encoder of a frozen VLM. Image skills are compatible with any VLM, and a single image can be optimized jointly for an ensemble of models. We show that Skill.png significantly outperforms state-of-the-art textual skills optimized using frontier LLMs in 9 of 16 settings (across 4 VLMs and 4 benchmarks) while requiring only 64 to 256 image tokens instead of roughly 900 to 2000 text tokens at inference time. Hybrid skills, i.e. text and image skills jointly optimized, further improve agent performance. Moreover, an image optimized with an ensemble of VLMs can successfully transfer across the models in the ensemble. Skill.png thus presents the first agent skill that can be optimized with gradient descent while retaining transfer benefits of text skills. Our code is available at: https://github.com/omseeth/skill.png

[165] MAWAM: A Multi-Agent World-Action Model for Coordinated Manipulation cs.RO | cs.AI | cs.CVPDF

Xianzhe Fan, Chi-Pin Huang, Fu-En Yang, Jing Wang, Yecheng Wu

TL;DR: 论文提出 MAWAM，一种面向多机器人协同操作的多智能体世界-动作模型。它基于预训练的单机器人世界-动作骨干，通过跨智能体注意力联合去噪所有机器人的动作 token 与未来视角 token，在单次采样中同时生成每个机器人的未来视图和可执行动作块，并保留各机器人独立的控制接口。

Details

Motivation: 多机器人团队中每个机器人的动作都会改变队友所处的物理世界，因此协同操作需要同时预测团队动作及其共同创造的物理未来，而不仅是单机器人动作或孤立世界模型。

Result: 在三个真实机器人任务上均取得最高成功率，平均 67.8%，比同数据与相机设置的 MA-VLA 高 18.9 个百分点；仿真中在最多四臂的八个任务上平均成功率达 90.3%。消融显示跨智能体注意力和团队阶段未来视频监督均关键，去掉注意力后四臂接力从 95.0% 降至 39.0%，去掉监督后 Place Food 从 78.0% 降至 47.0%。

Abstract: Every move in a robot team changes the world in which its teammates must act. Coordinated manipulation therefore calls for predicting the team’s actions together with the physical future they jointly create. We introduce MAWAM, a multi-agent world-action model that jointly generates each robot’s future views and executable action chunks in a single sample. Built from a pretrained single-robot world-action backbone, MAWAM jointly denoises all robots’ action and future-view tokens through cross-agent attention, while preserving a separate control interface for each robot. MAWAM achieves the highest success on all three real-robot tasks (laundry-bag collection, bath-towel folding, and umbrella handover), with 67.8% mean success, 18.9 percentage points above MA-VLA trained on the same demonstrations and cameras. In simulation, MAWAM reaches 90.3% mean success on eight tasks with teams of up to four arms. Ablations show that cross-agent attention and team-stage future-video supervision both improve coordinated control: without attention, a four-arm relay falls from 95.0% to 39.0%, and without this supervision, Place Food falls from 78.0% to 47.0%.

Abstract: Autonomous navigation in diverse real-world environments remains one of the greatest challenges in robotics. It demands both robust low-level motion control to handle diverse terrain and high-level reasoning to interpret environmental cues. We present ROAM (Robust Open-world Autonomous Mobility), a system that enables legged robots to perform kilometer-scale navigation in complex alpine and urban settings. ROAM combines the world knowledge of a Vision-Language Model (VLM), pre-trained on internet-scale data, with robust locomotion and local planning policies learned in simulation via reinforcement learning (RL). The VLM provides common-sense reasoning and semantic interpretation of the scene, assessing risks, interpreting signs, and producing semantic labels with associated navigation costs. An open-vocabulary segmentation module converts these labels into a dense, real-time risk map, which conditions two pre-trained RL policies responsible for agile locomotion and local navigation, effectively coupling high-level VLM reasoning with low-level physical control. Real-world experiments showed the system’s ability to autonomously navigate a wide range of complex real-world environments. The robot completes kilometer-long hikes with minimal intervention, interprets signs to avoid danger, and evaluates its environment to identify safe areas on which to step. This work represents a step toward the reliable deployment of systems in unstructured environments. Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies must react while visual inference is still running. Overlapping computation and execution reduces waiting, yet leaves perceptual memory stale and successive action chunks disconnected. We present CauSyncVLA, an asynchronous dual-system controller that aligns perceptual-memory prediction and trajectory correction with actual robot execution. Details

Motivation: 当前世界模型虽能生成逼真视频，但常忽略微小动作变化对后续观测的影响，导致难以用于机器人策略评估和模型规划等需要区分近似动作后果的任务。论文旨在让世界模型更忠实反映动作变化引起的环境动态。

Result: 在OGBench仿真、真实DROID和ALOHA-2设置中，AFFECT使世界模型rollout更接近真实结果；DROID上世界模型与真实机器人策略成功率的Pearson相关从0.47升至0.79，FVD最多降低23%，规划任务成功率最多提升8.3%。

Abstract: World models aim to predict environment dynamics under actions, enabling the prediction of action consequences without executing them in the real world. Yet despite producing increasingly realistic videos, current world models often fail to capture how small action changes affect future observations. This limits their use in tasks such as robotic policy evaluation and model-based planning, where nearly identical actions can lead to qualitatively different outcomes. To address this, we propose AFFECT, an auxiliary training objective for learning the distribution of visual changes induced by action perturbations. Specifically, we inject temporally correlated noise into the actions of a policy trained on task-agnostic human teleoperation. We then train the world model so that its generated videos respond to this noise as the recorded videos do, without requiring repeated executions from the same initial state. Across OGBench simulation and real-world DROID and ALOHA-2 setups, AFFECT makes world-model rollouts more faithful to real outcomes. Details

Motivation: 现有流匹配机器人策略通常在整个动作生成过程中固定使用一种预测目标，但从噪声中恢复动作与精修接近干净的动作面临不同挑战，因此需要针对不同噪声阶段采用更合适的预测目标，以提升少步生成效率和精度。

Abstract: Flow-matching robot policies usually use one prediction target throughout action generation, even though recovering actions from noise and polishing nearly clean actions bring different challenges. We systematically compare velocity prediction (V), endpoint prediction (X), and velocity-weighted endpoint prediction (X–V) across multiple robot policies, benchmarks, and inference budgets. On StarVLA and LiLa-WAM, X–V works best for few-step generation, while V gains more from iterative refinement. We further uncover their complementary strengths across noise levels: clean actions have concentrated covariance spectra, endpoint predictions stay more stable under high noise, and velocity predictions offer more accurate corrections near clean actions. Inspired by these findings, we introduce UniVEP, which learns both prediction targets together with consistency supervision and switches from endpoint recovery to velocity refinement as noise drops. Each step only needs one network run, and joint training also boosts single-step endpoint generation. Tests on simulated and real-world manipulation show better success rates and efficiency. TL;DR: 论文提出 TacInno，用视觉观测和已执行动作条件化地提取触觉中无法被视觉与动作解释的接触动力学，称为触觉创新，并仅将其作为训练目标。具体通过 TacInno tokenizer 将未来触觉变化压缩为离散编码，策略 TacInno-VLA 学习预测该编码，部署时不增加额外模块。

Details

Motivation: 现有视触觉 VLA 模型加入触觉输入后收益不稳定，部分模型甚至很少依赖触觉，因为夹爪闭合时的凝胶形变等触觉变化可从视觉和动作中预测。论文旨在隔离视觉和动作无法解释的接触动力学，以提供更有效的触觉监督。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have recently been equipped with tactile sensing to perceive visually subtle contact events in contact-rich manipulation. To address this problem, we introduce TacInno, which isolates the contact dynamics that vision and executed actions cannot explain, termed tactile innovation, and uses tactile innovation only as a training target. Specifically, the TacInno tokenizer, conditioned on visual observations and executed actions, compresses the future tactile change into a discrete code, and the policy, TacInno-VLA, learns to predict the code, adding no module for the code at deployment. [172] Mind2Act World: A Diagnostic Benchmark for Embodied Mind, Physical Action, and Mind-Action Composition in Robotic Manipulation cs.RO | cs.AI | cs.CVPDF

Yuzhi Huang, Jie Wu, Fanding Huang, Junchao Lin, Weichen Zhang

TL;DR: 论文提出 Mind2Act World，一个面向机器人操作的诊断基准，包含5个真实应用领域的30个模拟操作任务，每个任务有3个难度级别和2种场景设置，共180种配置。基准从Mind（基于历史观察决策）、Act（精确及时执行）和Mind2Act（闭环组合推理与执行）三个视图评估系统，并通过诊断工具将失败回合归因于推理、执行或二者组合。实验表明，单视图能力不能直接迁移到组合任务，最强智能体在Mind和Act上分别为52.7%和39.0%，但在Mind2Act上仅12.0%。

Details

Motivation: 现有基准要么在同一任务中评估推理与执行，要么将二者分开评估而不考察其组合，因此难以判断机器人失败究竟来自认知推理、物理执行，还是二者的组合。论文希望提供诊断性评估，定位具身智能系统的能力短板。

Result: 论文评估了多模态智能体、视觉-语言-动作模型和世界-动作模型。最强智能体在Mind视图成功率为52.7%，Act视图为39.0%，但闭环Mind2Act视图仅12.0%；结果说明单一视图能力不能自动转化为组合能力。

Insight: 创新点在于用Mind、Act、Mind2Act三视图构建诊断基准，并提供失败回合归因和系统画像卡，从而区分“缺少能力”与“不会组合已有能力”。客观上看，其价值在于把推理-执行组合通过环境反馈这一关键瓶颈显式化、可测量化。

Abstract: Embodied intelligence aims to let robots perform complex physical tasks with human-like adaptability and reliability, which requires three complementary capabilities: history-based cognitive reasoning, precise and timely physical execution, and the composition of reasoning and execution through environmental feedback. Yet existing benchmarks either evaluate reasoning and execution within the same tasks or assess them separately without their composition, so they reveal little about whether a failure comes from reasoning, execution, or their composition. We introduce Mind2Act World, a diagnostic benchmark of 30 simulated manipulation tasks from five real-world application domains. Each task has three difficulty levels and two scene settings (Clean and Random), yielding 180 configurations. The tasks form three views: Mind tests decisions based on past observations, Act tests precise and timely execution, and Mind2Act combines both in closed-loop tasks where action outcomes guide later decisions. Comparing the three views shows whether a system lacks a capability or fails to compose capabilities it already has. A diagnosis harness further attributes the failed episodes of three representative systems to Mind, Act, or their composition and summarizes each system in a profile card. Our evaluation of multimodal agents, vision-language-action models (VLAs), and world-action models (WAMs) shows that competence in a single view does not carry over to Mind2Act tasks. Even the strongest agent succeeds in 52.7% of Mind and 39.0% of Act episodes but in only 12.0% of Mind2Act episodes. The strongest agent retains task state but struggles with sustained contact, whereas end-to-end policies execute practiced motions but retain little task state. These findings point to composing reasoning and execution through environmental feedback as the main open problem.

[173] AgentRoboCritic: Understanding the Capability of Frontier Agents on Direct Robot Control cs.RO | cs.CL | cs.CVPDF

Yiyang Du, Yuxuan Kuang, Qitao Zhao, Yixuan Wang, Zhanqiu Guo

TL;DR: 论文对GPT-6 Astra等前沿多模态语言智能体在12个机器人操作基准上的直接机器人控制能力进行动作级系统分析，提出意图驱动的AgentRoboCritic批评框架：策略智能体为每个动作声明局部意图，批评器据此评估动作结果和失败模式。研究揭示了智能体在闭环恢复、语义 grounding 和零样本操作上的能力，也指出抓取、对齐等物理执行瓶颈及失败模式恢复不均衡的问题。

Details

Motivation: 现有基准通常只报告回合级成功率，无法说明前沿智能体为何成功、在哪里失败，因此需要细粒度的动作级分析来理解其直接机器人控制能力。

Result: 在12个操作基准上开展动作级分析，并在400个动作的人工标注基准上验证批评器；发布12,844个标注动作数据集及微调开源批评器Muse-Glimmer-30B，其失败模式准确率从32.58%提升至74.24%。

Insight: 创新点在于提出意图 grounding 的动作级批评框架，将局部意图、动作结果与失败模式关联，实现低成本、可解释的细粒度诊断；客观看，其价值还包括系统揭示前沿智能体的能力边界和物理执行瓶颈，并提供可复用的标注数据与开源批评器。

Abstract: Frontier multimodal language agents such as GPT-6 Astra have demonstrated strong capabilities in controlling robots directly via low-level numerical actions without high-level primitives. However, existing benchmarks report only episode-level success rates, which show neither how these agents succeed nor where they fail. In this paper, we present a systematic action-level analysis of frontier agents on direct robot control across 12 manipulation benchmarks. We introduce AgentRoboCritic, an intent-grounded critique framework in which the policy agent states a local intent alongside each action, and a critic evaluates the action’s outcome and failure mode against this intent. Our action-level analysis demonstrates the promising capabilities of frontier agents on direct robot control, including closed-loop recovery from local execution failures, reliable semantic grounding across diverse scenes, and strong zero-shot manipulation without task-specific adaptation. It also identifies their current limitations, including task-relevant physical execution bottlenecks such as grasping and alignment, and uneven recovery across failure modes. Finally, we validate our critic on a 400-action human-annotated benchmark and release a dataset of 12,844 annotated actions alongside a fine-tuned open-source critic, Muse-Glimmer-30B, whose failure-mode accuracy rises from 32.58% to 74.24%, enabling low-cost action-level analysis.

eess.IV [Back]

[174] Life-IQA+: Boosting Blind Image Quality Assessment via GCN-enhanced Layer Interaction and Flexible Hierarchical Expert Specialization eess.IV | cs.CVPDF

Long Tang, Guoquan Zheng, Huiyu Duan, Jie Hao, Yucheng Zhu

TL;DR: 论文提出 Life-IQA+，一种用于盲图像质量评估的质量表示解码框架，结合 GCN 增强的跨层交互与灵活分层专家专业化。方法利用 GCN 精炼最深阶段特征构建质量查询，并通过跨注意力与倒数第二层特征交互，同时设计包含密集激活共享专家和稀疏激活路由专家的 MoE 结构，并对部分共享专家施加辅助分类监督。

Details

Motivation: 现有 BIQA 方法通常直接聚合多个 backbone 阶段特征，未考虑不同阶段对质量预测贡献不均衡；同时对所有专家统一监督会限制专家灵活性和质量表示多样性。

Result: 在合成失真和真实失真 benchmark 上，以及专家配置和监督策略的消融实验中，Life-IQA+ 取得有竞争力的预测准确率，并在准确率与计算成本之间实现较好平衡。

Insight: 创新点在于用 GCN 精炼深层特征并指导跨层 cross-attention 交互，以及采用密集共享专家加稀疏路由专家的分层 MoE；同时用失真类型或离散质量等级标签辅助监督部分共享专家，以增强表示多样性和专家能力。

Abstract: Blind image quality assessment (BIQA) is essential for evaluating and optimizing visual experience. Existing BIQA methods typically aggregate features from multiple backbone stages without accounting for their unequal contributions to quality prediction. Furthermore, uniformly supervising all experts may constrain their flexibility and limit the diversity of the learned quality representations. To address these issues, we present Life-IQA+, a quality representation decoding framework that combines graph convolutional network (GCN) enhanced layer interaction and flexible hierarchical expert specialization. Specifically, the layer interaction module constructs quality queries from GCN-refined deepest-stage features and uses penultimate-stage features as keys and values, thereby enabling cross-layer representation interaction via the cross-attention mechanism. Building on these representations, we further introduce a flexible hierarchical mixture-of-experts (MoE) architecture comprising a densely activated shared expert layer and a sparsely activated routed expert layer. To enhance the representation diversity and ability of experts, we apply auxiliary classification supervision to a subset of shared experts using distortion-type labels for synthetic datasets and discretized quality-level labels for authentic datasets. Experiments on synthetic and authentic distortion benchmarks, together with controlled ablations of expert configurations and supervision strategies, show that Life-IQA+ achieves competitive prediction accuracy with a favorable balance between accuracy and computational cost. The code is available at: https://github.com/TANGLONG2/Life-IQA-PlusPlus.
